# HTML Standard: elements attributes

Attribution and reformatting notice added for Surgeist on 2026-10-11. Copyright © WHATWG (Apple, Google, Mozilla, Microsoft). Original English standard and normative/informative labels remain authoritative. This partial format conversion supports the selected HTML language baseline; it is not a replacement specification or an implementation-conformance claim. No endorsement is implied.

Source: [HTML Standard commit snapshot](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/). Source revision `877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a` (9 October 2026); retrieved 11 October 2026 UTC. This immutable snapshot is the selected language baseline, independently of the html5ever implementation ingestion revision.

The exact [WHATWG LICENSE](../licenses/whatwg/LICENSE), also present in the HTML repository at this revision, has SHA-256 `85dc6f5ccb57a6fe8c33d158f9fc8fc7ee5655a5d3db2cdd131c6a3d0f48a864`. Specification prose is Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 International; the BSD 3-Clause scope applies when portions are incorporated into code, not as an alternative license for this Markdown.

Complete original HTML: 14,930,614 bytes, SHA-256 `877d6cc00dae6c18c16d952fdcbf944acc58919579621b4257fa0ed1888a871f`. Complete-section extraction input: 642,230 bytes, SHA-256 `2ca83b5ac984f8233878054aea9e93c3ef941a5a6aeb6f046851258624dae552`.

Retained complete heading sections, including all descendant clauses and bounded by the next equal-or-higher-level heading: `the-root-element`, `document-metadata`, `elements-3`, `element-content-categories`, `attributes-3`. Original snapshot header is retained. The [edition map](SOURCE-EDITIONS.md#pinned-html-language-baseline) records the ten companion captures and bounded ownership. Every §4 element definition is retained across the collection. Omitted browser runtime chapters remain linked to this immutable snapshot; retaining JavaScript interfaces, networking, media, script execution or custom-element algorithms as source evidence does not assign their implementation to the native HTML crate. HTML syntax, author conformance and required parsing recovery remain distinct; malformed input is not rejected solely for an author-conformance error.

Conversion uses the repository preparation/filter, approved Pandoc 3.1.11.1 and parsed-GFM comparison. Source scripts/styles are omitted without execution. Source headings, IDs, links, prose, inline code, variables, literal blocks, images and table cells/spans are checked. Selected complex tables and inline SVG diagrams remain literal semantic HTML/SVG, preserving source structure without flattening. Cross-capture links retain the same pinned edition.

#### <a id="the&#45;html&#45;element"></a>4&#46;1&#46;1 The <strong><code>html</code></strong> element[](#the-html-element)

<strong>✔</strong>MDN

[Element&#47;html](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTML/Element/html)

<strong>✔</strong>MDN

[HTMLHtmlElement](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/HTMLHtmlElement)

Support in all current engines&#46;

Firefox1&#43;Safari1&#43;Chrome1&#43;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

Opera12&#46;1&#43;Edge79&#43;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

Edge &#40;Legacy&#41;12&#43;Internet Explorer5&#46;5&#43;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

Firefox Android&#63;Safari iOS&#63;Chrome Android&#63;WebView Android&#63;Samsung Internet&#63;Opera Android12&#46;1&#43;

<strong><a id="the&#45;html&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;categories"></a>[Categories](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-categories)&#58;</strong>

None&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;html&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;contexts"></a>[Contexts in which this element can be used](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-contexts)&#58;</strong>

As document&#39;s <a id="the&#45;html&#45;element&#58;document&#45;element"></a>[document element](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#document-element)&#46;

Wherever a subdocument fragment is allowed in a compound document&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;html&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;content&#45;model"></a>[Content model](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-content-model)&#58;</strong>

A <a id="the&#45;html&#45;element&#58;the&#45;head&#45;element"></a><code>[head](#the-head-element)</code> element followed by a <a id="the&#45;html&#45;element&#58;the&#45;body&#45;element"></a><code>[body](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#the-body-element)</code> element&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;html&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;tag&#45;omission"></a>[Tag omission in text&#47;html](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-tag-omission)&#58;</strong>

An <a id="the&#45;html&#45;element&#58;the&#45;html&#45;element"></a><code>[html](#the-html-element)</code> element&#39;s <a id="the&#45;html&#45;element&#58;syntax&#45;start&#45;tag"></a>[start tag](html-language-syntax-parser--877d6146487f--37a9ba51eed8.md#syntax-start-tag) can be omitted if the first thing inside the <a id="the&#45;html&#45;element&#58;the&#45;html&#45;element&#45;2"></a><code>[html](#the-html-element)</code> element is not a <a id="the&#45;html&#45;element&#58;syntax&#45;comments"></a>[comment](html-language-syntax-parser--877d6146487f--37a9ba51eed8.md#syntax-comments)&#46;

An <a id="the&#45;html&#45;element&#58;the&#45;html&#45;element&#45;3"></a><code>[html](#the-html-element)</code> element&#39;s <a id="the&#45;html&#45;element&#58;syntax&#45;end&#45;tag"></a>[end tag](html-language-syntax-parser--877d6146487f--37a9ba51eed8.md#syntax-end-tag) can be omitted if the <a id="the&#45;html&#45;element&#58;the&#45;html&#45;element&#45;4"></a><code>[html](#the-html-element)</code> element is not immediately followed by a <a id="the&#45;html&#45;element&#58;syntax&#45;comments&#45;2"></a>[comment](html-language-syntax-parser--877d6146487f--37a9ba51eed8.md#syntax-comments)&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;html&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;attributes"></a>[Content attributes](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-attributes)&#58;</strong>

<a id="the&#45;html&#45;element&#58;global&#45;attributes"></a>[Global attributes](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#global-attributes)

<strong><a id="the&#45;html&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;accessibility&#45;considerations"></a>[Accessibility considerations](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-accessibility-considerations)&#58;</strong>

[For authors](https://w3c.github.io/html-aria/#el-html)&#46;

[For implementers](https://w3c.github.io/html-aam/#el-html)&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;html&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;sanitization"></a>[Sanitization](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-sanitization)&#58;</strong>

<a id="the&#45;html&#45;element&#58;sanitizer&#45;category&#45;default"></a>[Default](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#sanitizer-category-default)&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;html&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;dom"></a>[DOM interface](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-dom)&#58;</strong>

<a id="htmlhtmlelement"></a><a id="the&#45;html&#45;element&#58;htmlelement"></a><a id="the&#45;html&#45;element&#58;htmlconstructor"></a>

``` text
[Exposed=Window]
interface HTMLHtmlElement : HTMLElement {
  [HTMLConstructor] constructor();

  // also has obsolete members
};
```

The <a id="the&#45;html&#45;element&#58;the&#45;html&#45;element&#45;5"></a><code>[html](#the-html-element)</code> element <a id="the&#45;html&#45;element&#58;represents"></a>[represents](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#represents) the root of an HTML document&#46;

Authors are encouraged to specify a <a id="the&#45;html&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;lang"></a><code>[lang](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#attr-lang)</code> attribute on the root <a id="the&#45;html&#45;element&#58;the&#45;html&#45;element&#45;6"></a><code>[html](#the-html-element)</code> element&#44; giving the document&#39;s language&#46; This aids speech synthesis tools to determine what pronunciations to use&#44; translation tools to determine what rules to use&#44; and so forth&#46;

<strong>Example&#58;</strong>

The <a id="the&#45;html&#45;element&#58;the&#45;html&#45;element&#45;7"></a><code>[html](#the-html-element)</code> element in the following example declares that the document&#39;s language is English&#46;

``` text
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<title>Swapping Songs</title>
</head>
<body>
<h1>Swapping Songs</h1>
<p>Tonight I swapped some of the songs I wrote with some friends, who
gave me some of the songs they wrote. I love sharing my music.</p>
</body>
</html>
```

### <a id="document&#45;metadata"></a>4&#46;2 Document metadata[](#document-metadata)

#### <a id="the&#45;head&#45;element"></a>4&#46;2&#46;1 The <strong><code>head</code></strong> element[](#the-head-element)

<strong>✔</strong>MDN

[Element&#47;head](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTML/Element/head)

Support in all current engines&#46;

Firefox1&#43;Safari1&#43;Chrome1&#43;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

Opera&#63;Edge79&#43;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

Edge &#40;Legacy&#41;12&#43;Internet ExplorerYes

------------------------------------------------------------------------

Firefox Android&#63;Safari iOS&#63;Chrome Android&#63;WebView Android&#63;Samsung Internet&#63;Opera Android&#63;

<strong>✔</strong>MDN

[HTMLHeadElement](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/HTMLHeadElement)

Support in all current engines&#46;

Firefox1&#43;Safari1&#43;Chrome1&#43;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

Opera12&#46;1&#43;Edge79&#43;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

Edge &#40;Legacy&#41;12&#43;Internet Explorer5&#46;5&#43;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

Firefox Android&#63;Safari iOS&#63;Chrome Android&#63;WebView Android&#63;Samsung Internet&#63;Opera Android12&#46;1&#43;

<strong><a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;categories"></a>[Categories](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-categories)&#58;</strong>

None&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;contexts"></a>[Contexts in which this element can be used](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-contexts)&#58;</strong>

As the first element in an <a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;the&#45;html&#45;element"></a><code>[html](#the-html-element)</code> element&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;content&#45;model"></a>[Content model](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-content-model)&#58;</strong>

If the document is <a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;an&#45;iframe&#45;srcdoc&#45;document"></a>[an <code>iframe</code> <code>srcdoc</code> document](html-language-elements-embedded--877d6146487f--48a1a13e5cfe.md#an-iframe-srcdoc-document) or if title information is available from a higher&#45;level protocol&#58; Zero or more elements of <a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;metadata&#45;content&#45;2"></a>[metadata content](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#metadata-content-2)&#44; of which no more than one is a <a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;the&#45;title&#45;element"></a><code>[title](#the-title-element)</code> element and no more than one is a <a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;the&#45;base&#45;element"></a><code>[base](#the-base-element)</code> element&#46;

Otherwise&#58; One or more elements of <a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;metadata&#45;content&#45;2&#45;2"></a>[metadata content](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#metadata-content-2)&#44; of which exactly one is a <a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;the&#45;title&#45;element&#45;2"></a><code>[title](#the-title-element)</code> element and no more than one is a <a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;the&#45;base&#45;element&#45;2"></a><code>[base](#the-base-element)</code> element&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;tag&#45;omission"></a>[Tag omission in text&#47;html](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-tag-omission)&#58;</strong>

A <a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;the&#45;head&#45;element"></a><code>[head](#the-head-element)</code> element&#39;s <a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;syntax&#45;start&#45;tag"></a>[start tag](html-language-syntax-parser--877d6146487f--37a9ba51eed8.md#syntax-start-tag) can be omitted if the element is empty&#44; or if the first thing inside the <a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;the&#45;head&#45;element&#45;2"></a><code>[head](#the-head-element)</code> element is an element&#46;

A <a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;the&#45;head&#45;element&#45;3"></a><code>[head](#the-head-element)</code> element&#39;s <a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;syntax&#45;end&#45;tag"></a>[end tag](html-language-syntax-parser--877d6146487f--37a9ba51eed8.md#syntax-end-tag) can be omitted if the <a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;the&#45;head&#45;element&#45;4"></a><code>[head](#the-head-element)</code> element is not immediately followed by <a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;space&#45;characters"></a>[ASCII whitespace](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-whitespace) or a <a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;syntax&#45;comments"></a>[comment](html-language-syntax-parser--877d6146487f--37a9ba51eed8.md#syntax-comments)&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;attributes"></a>[Content attributes](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-attributes)&#58;</strong>

<a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;global&#45;attributes"></a>[Global attributes](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#global-attributes)

<strong><a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;accessibility&#45;considerations"></a>[Accessibility considerations](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-accessibility-considerations)&#58;</strong>

[For authors](https://w3c.github.io/html-aria/#el-head)&#46;

[For implementers](https://w3c.github.io/html-aam/#el-head)&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;sanitization"></a>[Sanitization](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-sanitization)&#58;</strong>

<a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;sanitizer&#45;category&#45;default"></a>[Default](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#sanitizer-category-default)&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;dom"></a>[DOM interface](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-dom)&#58;</strong>

<a id="htmlheadelement"></a><a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;htmlelement"></a><a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;htmlconstructor"></a>

``` text
[Exposed=Window]
interface HTMLHeadElement : HTMLElement {
  [HTMLConstructor] constructor();
};
```

The <a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;the&#45;head&#45;element&#45;5"></a><code>[head](#the-head-element)</code> element <a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;represents"></a>[represents](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#represents) a collection of metadata for the <a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;document"></a><code>[Document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document)</code>&#46;

<strong>Example&#58;</strong>

The collection of metadata in a <a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;the&#45;head&#45;element&#45;6"></a><code>[head](#the-head-element)</code> element can be large or small&#46; Here is an example of a very short one&#58;

``` text
<!doctype html>
<html lang=en>
 <head>
  <title>A document with a short head</title>
 </head>
 <body>
 ...
```

Here is an example of a longer one&#58;

``` text
<!DOCTYPE HTML>
<HTML LANG="EN">
 <HEAD>
  <META CHARSET="UTF-8">
  <BASE HREF="https://www.example.com/">
  <TITLE>An application with a long head</TITLE>
  <LINK REL="STYLESHEET" HREF="default.css">
  <LINK REL="STYLESHEET ALTERNATE" HREF="big.css" TITLE="Big Text">
  <SCRIPT SRC="support.js"></SCRIPT>
  <META NAME="APPLICATION-NAME" CONTENT="Long headed application">
 </HEAD>
 <BODY>
 ...
```

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

The <a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;the&#45;title&#45;element&#45;3"></a><code>[title](#the-title-element)</code> element is a required child in most situations&#44; but when a higher&#45;level protocol provides title information&#44; e&#46;g&#46;&#44; in the subject line of an email when HTML is used as an email authoring format&#44; the <a id="the&#45;head&#45;element&#58;the&#45;title&#45;element&#45;4"></a><code>[title](#the-title-element)</code> element can be omitted&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;categories"></a>[Categories](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-categories)&#58;</strong>

<a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;metadata&#45;content&#45;2"></a>[Metadata content](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#metadata-content-2)&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;contexts"></a>[Contexts in which this element can be used](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-contexts)&#58;</strong>

In a <a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;the&#45;head&#45;element"></a><code>[head](#the-head-element)</code> element containing no other <a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;the&#45;title&#45;element"></a><code>[title](#the-title-element)</code> elements&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;content&#45;model"></a>[Content model](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-content-model)&#58;</strong>

<a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;text&#45;content"></a>[Text](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#text-content) that is not <a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;inter&#45;element&#45;whitespace"></a>[inter&#45;element whitespace](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#inter-element-whitespace)&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;tag&#45;omission"></a>[Tag omission in text&#47;html](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-tag-omission)&#58;</strong>

Neither tag is omissible&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;attributes"></a>[Content attributes](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-attributes)&#58;</strong>

<strong><a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;accessibility&#45;considerations"></a>[Accessibility considerations](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-accessibility-considerations)&#58;</strong>

[For authors](https://w3c.github.io/html-aria/#el-title)&#46;

[For implementers](https://w3c.github.io/html-aam/#el-title)&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;sanitization"></a>[Sanitization](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-sanitization)&#58;</strong>

<a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;sanitizer&#45;category&#45;default"></a>[Default](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#sanitizer-category-default)&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;dom"></a>[DOM interface](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-dom)&#58;</strong>

<a id="htmltitleelement"></a><a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;htmlelement"></a><a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;htmlconstructor"></a><a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;cereactions"></a><a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;dom&#45;title&#45;text"></a>

``` text
[Exposed=Window]
interface HTMLTitleElement : HTMLElement {
  [HTMLConstructor] constructor();

  [CEReactions] attribute DOMString text;
};
```

The <a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;the&#45;title&#45;element&#45;2"></a><code>[title](#the-title-element)</code> element <a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;represents"></a>[represents](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#represents) the document&#39;s title or name&#46; Authors should use titles that identify their documents even when they are used out of context&#44; for example in a user&#39;s history or bookmarks&#44; or in search results&#46; The document&#39;s title is often different from its first heading&#44; since the first heading does not have to stand alone when taken out of context&#46;

There must be no more than one <a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;the&#45;title&#45;element&#45;3"></a><code>[title](#the-title-element)</code> element per document&#46;

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

If it&#39;s reasonable for the <a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;document"></a><code>[Document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document)</code> to have no title&#44; then the <a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;the&#45;title&#45;element&#45;4"></a><code>[title](#the-title-element)</code> element is probably not required&#46; See the <a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;the&#45;head&#45;element&#45;2"></a><code>[head](#the-head-element)</code> element&#39;s content model for a description of when the element is required&#46;

<strong><code><var>title</var>&#46;<a id="dom&#45;title&#45;text&#45;dev"></a>[text](#dom-title-text) &#91; &#61; <var>value</var> &#93;</code></strong>

Returns the <a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;child&#45;text&#45;content"></a>[child text content](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-child-text-content) of the element&#46;

Can be set&#44; to replace the element&#39;s children with the given value&#46;

The <a id="dom&#45;title&#45;text"></a><strong><code>text</code></strong> attribute&#39;s getter must return this <a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;the&#45;title&#45;element&#45;5"></a><code>[title](#the-title-element)</code> element&#39;s <a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;child&#45;text&#45;content&#45;2"></a>[child text content](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-child-text-content)&#46;

The <a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;dom&#45;title&#45;text&#45;2"></a><code>[text](#dom-title-text)</code> attribute&#39;s setter must <a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;string&#45;replace&#45;all"></a>[string replace all](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#string-replace-all) with the given value within this <a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;the&#45;title&#45;element&#45;6"></a><code>[title](#the-title-element)</code> element&#46;

<strong>Example&#58;</strong>

Here are some examples of appropriate titles&#44; contrasted with the top&#45;level headings that might be used on those same pages&#46;

``` text
  <title>Introduction to The Mating Rituals of Bees</title>
    ...
  <h1>Introduction</h1>
  <p>This companion guide to the highly successful
  <cite>Introduction to Medieval Bee-Keeping</cite> book is...
```

The next page might be a part of the same site&#46; Note how the title describes the subject matter unambiguously&#44; while the first heading assumes the reader knows what the context is and therefore won&#39;t wonder if the dances are Salsa or Waltz&#58;

``` text
  <title>Dances used during bee mating rituals</title>
    ...
  <h1>The Dances</h1>
```

The string to use as the document&#39;s title is given by the <a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;document&#46;title"></a><code>[document&#46;title](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document.title)</code> IDL attribute&#46;

User agents should use the document&#39;s title when referring to the document in their user interface&#46; When the contents of a <a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;the&#45;title&#45;element&#45;7"></a><code>[title](#the-title-element)</code> element are used in this way&#44; <a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;the&#45;directionality"></a>[the directionality](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#the-directionality) of that <a id="the&#45;title&#45;element&#58;the&#45;title&#45;element&#45;8"></a><code>[title](#the-title-element)</code> element should be used to set the directionality of the document&#39;s title in the user interface&#46;

#### <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element"></a>4&#46;2&#46;3 The <strong><code>base</code></strong> element[](#the-base-element)

<strong><a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;categories"></a>[Categories](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-categories)&#58;</strong>

<a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;metadata&#45;content&#45;2"></a>[Metadata content](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#metadata-content-2)&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;contexts"></a>[Contexts in which this element can be used](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-contexts)&#58;</strong>

In a <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;the&#45;head&#45;element"></a><code>[head](#the-head-element)</code> element containing no other <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;the&#45;base&#45;element"></a><code>[base](#the-base-element)</code> elements&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;content&#45;model"></a>[Content model](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-content-model)&#58;</strong>

<a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;content&#45;nothing"></a>[Nothing](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-content-nothing)&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;tag&#45;omission"></a>[Tag omission in text&#47;html](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-tag-omission)&#58;</strong>

No <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;syntax&#45;end&#45;tag"></a>[end tag](html-language-syntax-parser--877d6146487f--37a9ba51eed8.md#syntax-end-tag)&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;attributes"></a>[Content attributes](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-attributes)&#58;</strong>

<a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;global&#45;attributes"></a>[Global attributes](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#global-attributes)

<a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;base&#45;href"></a><code>[href](#attr-base-href)</code> — <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;document&#45;base&#45;url"></a>[Document base URL](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document-base-url)

<a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;base&#45;target"></a><code>[target](#attr-base-target)</code> — Default <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;navigable"></a>[navigable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#navigable) for <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;hyperlink"></a>[hyperlink](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#hyperlink) <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;navigate"></a>[navigation](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#navigate) and <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;form&#45;submission&#45;2"></a>[form submission](html-language-forms-tables-templates--877d6146487f--2dfc6f632608.md#form-submission-2)

<strong><a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;accessibility&#45;considerations"></a>[Accessibility considerations](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-accessibility-considerations)&#58;</strong>

[For authors](https://w3c.github.io/html-aria/#el-base)&#46;

[For implementers](https://w3c.github.io/html-aam/#el-base)&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;sanitization"></a>[Sanitization](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-sanitization)&#58;</strong>

<a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;sanitizer&#45;category&#45;unsafe"></a>[Unsafe](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#sanitizer-category-unsafe)&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;dom"></a>[DOM interface](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-dom)&#58;</strong>

<a id="htmlbaseelement"></a><a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;htmlelement"></a><a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;htmlconstructor"></a><a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;cereactions"></a><a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;xattr&#45;reflectsetter"></a><a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;dom&#45;base&#45;href"></a><a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;cereactions&#45;2"></a><a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;xattr&#45;reflect"></a><a id="dom&#45;base&#45;target"></a>

``` text
[Exposed=Window]
interface HTMLBaseElement : HTMLElement {
  [HTMLConstructor] constructor();

  [CEReactions, ReflectSetter] attribute USVString href;
  [CEReactions, Reflect] attribute DOMString target;
};
```

The <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;the&#45;base&#45;element&#45;2"></a><code>[base](#the-base-element)</code> element allows authors to specify the <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;document&#45;base&#45;url&#45;2"></a>[document base URL](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document-base-url) for the purposes of parsing <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;url"></a>[URLs](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url)&#44; and the name of the default <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;navigable&#45;2"></a>[navigable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#navigable) for the purposes of <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;following&#45;hyperlinks&#45;2"></a>[following hyperlinks](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#following-hyperlinks-2)&#46; The element does not <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;represents"></a>[represent](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#represents) any content beyond this information&#46;

There must be no more than one <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;the&#45;base&#45;element&#45;3"></a><code>[base](#the-base-element)</code> element per document&#46;

A <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;the&#45;base&#45;element&#45;4"></a><code>[base](#the-base-element)</code> element must have either an <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;base&#45;href&#45;2"></a><code>[href](#attr-base-href)</code> attribute&#44; a <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;base&#45;target&#45;2"></a><code>[target](#attr-base-target)</code> attribute&#44; or both&#46;

The <a id="attr&#45;base&#45;href"></a><strong><code>href</code></strong> content attribute&#44; if specified&#44; must contain a <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;valid&#45;url&#45;potentially&#45;surrounded&#45;by&#45;spaces"></a>[valid URL potentially surrounded by spaces](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#valid-url-potentially-surrounded-by-spaces)&#46;

A <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;the&#45;base&#45;element&#45;5"></a><code>[base](#the-base-element)</code> element&#44; if it has an <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;base&#45;href&#45;3"></a><code>[href](#attr-base-href)</code> attribute&#44; must come before any other elements in the tree that have attributes defined as taking <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;url&#45;2"></a>[URLs](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url)&#46;

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

If there are multiple <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;the&#45;base&#45;element&#45;6"></a><code>[base](#the-base-element)</code> elements with <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;base&#45;href&#45;4"></a><code>[href](#attr-base-href)</code> attributes&#44; all but the first are ignored&#46;

The <a id="attr&#45;base&#45;target"></a><strong><code>target</code></strong> attribute&#44; if specified&#44; must contain a <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;valid&#45;navigable&#45;target&#45;name&#45;or&#45;keyword"></a>[valid navigable target name or keyword](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#valid-navigable-target-name-or-keyword)&#44; which specifies which <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;navigable&#45;3"></a>[navigable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#navigable) is to be used as the default when <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;hyperlink&#45;2"></a>[hyperlinks](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#hyperlink) and <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;the&#45;form&#45;element"></a>[forms](html-language-forms-tables-templates--877d6146487f--2dfc6f632608.md#the-form-element) in the <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;document"></a><code>[Document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document)</code> cause <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;navigate&#45;2"></a>[navigation](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#navigate)&#46;

A <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;the&#45;base&#45;element&#45;7"></a><code>[base](#the-base-element)</code> element&#44; if it has a <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;base&#45;target&#45;3"></a><code>[target](#attr-base-target)</code> attribute&#44; must come before any elements in the tree that represent <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;hyperlink&#45;3"></a>[hyperlinks](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#hyperlink)&#46;

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

If there are multiple <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;the&#45;base&#45;element&#45;8"></a><code>[base](#the-base-element)</code> elements with <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;base&#45;target&#45;4"></a><code>[target](#attr-base-target)</code> attributes&#44; all but the first are ignored&#46;

To <a id="get&#45;an&#45;element&#39;s&#45;target"></a><strong>get an element&#39;s target</strong>&#44; given an <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;the&#45;a&#45;element"></a><code>[a](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#the-a-element)</code>&#44; <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;the&#45;area&#45;element"></a><code>[area](html-language-elements-embedded--877d6146487f--48a1a13e5cfe.md#the-area-element)</code>&#44; or <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;the&#45;form&#45;element&#45;2"></a><code>[form](html-language-forms-tables-templates--877d6146487f--2dfc6f632608.md#the-form-element)</code> element <var>element</var>&#44; and an optional string&#45;or&#45;null <var>target</var> &#40;default null&#41;&#44; run these steps&#58;

1.  If <var>target</var> is null&#58;

    1.  If <var>element</var> has a <code>target</code> attribute&#44; then set <var>target</var> to that attribute&#39;s value&#46;

    2.  Otherwise&#44; if <var>element</var>&#39;s <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;node&#45;document"></a>[node document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-node-document) contains a <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;the&#45;base&#45;element&#45;9"></a><code>[base](#the-base-element)</code> element with a <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;base&#45;target&#45;5"></a><code>[target](#attr-base-target)</code> attribute&#44; set <var>target</var> to the value of the <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;base&#45;target&#45;6"></a><code>[target](#attr-base-target)</code> attribute of the first such <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;the&#45;base&#45;element&#45;10"></a><code>[base](#the-base-element)</code> element&#46;

2.  If <var>target</var> is not null&#44; and contains an <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;ascii&#45;tab&#45;or&#45;newline"></a>[ASCII tab or newline](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-tab-or-newline) and a U&#43;003C &#40;&#60;&#41;&#44; then set <var>target</var> to &#34;<code>&#95;blank</code>&#34;&#46;

3.  Return <var>target</var>&#46;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

A <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;the&#45;base&#45;element&#45;11"></a><code>[base](#the-base-element)</code> element that is the first <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;the&#45;base&#45;element&#45;12"></a><code>[base](#the-base-element)</code> element with an <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;base&#45;href&#45;5"></a><code>[href](#attr-base-href)</code> content attribute <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;in&#45;a&#45;document&#45;tree"></a>[in a document tree](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#in-a-document-tree) has a <a id="frozen&#45;base&#45;url"></a><strong>frozen base URL</strong>&#46; The <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;frozen&#45;base&#45;url"></a>[frozen base URL](#frozen-base-url) must be <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;immediately"></a>[immediately](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#immediately) <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;set&#45;the&#45;frozen&#45;base&#45;url"></a>[set](#set-the-frozen-base-url) for an element whenever any of the following situations occur&#58;

- The <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;the&#45;base&#45;element&#45;13"></a><code>[base](#the-base-element)</code> element becomes the first <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;the&#45;base&#45;element&#45;14"></a><code>[base](#the-base-element)</code> element in <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;tree&#45;order"></a>[tree order](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-tree-order) with an <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;base&#45;href&#45;6"></a><code>[href](#attr-base-href)</code> content attribute in its <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;document&#45;2"></a><code>[Document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document)</code>&#46;

- The <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;the&#45;base&#45;element&#45;15"></a><code>[base](#the-base-element)</code> element is the first <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;the&#45;base&#45;element&#45;16"></a><code>[base](#the-base-element)</code> element in <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;tree&#45;order&#45;2"></a>[tree order](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-tree-order) with an <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;base&#45;href&#45;7"></a><code>[href](#attr-base-href)</code> content attribute in its <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;document&#45;3"></a><code>[Document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document)</code>&#44; and its <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;base&#45;href&#45;8"></a><code>[href](#attr-base-href)</code> content attribute is changed&#46;

To <a id="set&#45;the&#45;frozen&#45;base&#45;url"></a><strong>set the frozen base URL</strong> for an element <var>element</var>&#58;

1.  Let <var>document</var> be <var>element</var>&#39;s <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;node&#45;document&#45;2"></a>[node document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-node-document)&#46;

2.  Let <var>urlRecord</var> be the result of <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;url&#45;parser"></a>[parsing](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-parser) the value of <var>element</var>&#39;s <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;base&#45;href&#45;9"></a><code>[href](#attr-base-href)</code> content attribute with <var>document</var>&#39;s <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;fallback&#45;base&#45;url"></a>[fallback base URL](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#fallback-base-url)&#44; and <var>document</var>&#39;s <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;document&#39;s&#45;character&#45;encoding"></a>[character encoding](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document-encoding)&#46; &#40;Thus&#44; the <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;the&#45;base&#45;element&#45;17"></a><code>[base](#the-base-element)</code> element isn&#39;t affected by itself&#46;&#41;

3.  If any of the following are true&#58;

    - <var>urlRecord</var> is failure&#59;

    - <var>urlRecord</var>&#39;s <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;url&#45;scheme"></a>[scheme](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-scheme) is &#34;<code>data</code>&#34; or &#34;<code>javascript</code>&#34;&#59; or

    - running <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;is&#45;base&#45;allowed&#45;for&#45;document"></a>[Is base allowed for Document&#63;](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-csp/#allow-base-for-document) on <var>urlRecord</var> and <var>document</var> returns &#34;<code>Blocked</code>&#34;&#44;

    then set <var>element</var>&#39;s <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;frozen&#45;base&#45;url&#45;2"></a>[frozen base URL](#frozen-base-url) to <var>document</var>&#39;s <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;fallback&#45;base&#45;url&#45;2"></a>[fallback base URL](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#fallback-base-url) and return&#46;

4.  Set <var>element</var>&#39;s <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;frozen&#45;base&#45;url&#45;3"></a>[frozen base URL](#frozen-base-url) to <var>urlRecord</var>&#46;

5.  <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;respond&#45;to&#45;base&#45;url&#45;changes"></a>[Respond to base URL changes](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#respond-to-base-url-changes) given <var>document</var>&#46;

The <a id="dom&#45;base&#45;href"></a><strong><code>href</code></strong> IDL attribute&#44; on getting&#44; must return the result of running the following algorithm&#58;

1.  Let <var>document</var> be <var>element</var>&#39;s <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;node&#45;document&#45;3"></a>[node document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-node-document)&#46;

2.  Let <var>url</var> be the value of the <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;base&#45;href&#45;10"></a><code>[href](#attr-base-href)</code> attribute of this element&#44; if it has one&#44; and the empty string otherwise&#46;

3.  Let <var>urlRecord</var> be the result of <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;url&#45;parser&#45;2"></a>[parsing](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-parser) <var>url</var> with <var>document</var>&#39;s <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;fallback&#45;base&#45;url&#45;3"></a>[fallback base URL](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#fallback-base-url)&#44; and <var>document</var>&#39;s <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;document&#39;s&#45;character&#45;encoding&#45;2"></a>[character encoding](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document-encoding)&#46; &#40;Thus&#44; the <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;the&#45;base&#45;element&#45;18"></a><code>[base](#the-base-element)</code> element isn&#39;t affected by other <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;the&#45;base&#45;element&#45;19"></a><code>[base](#the-base-element)</code> elements or itself&#46;&#41;

4.  If <var>urlRecord</var> is failure&#44; return <var>url</var>&#46;

5.  Return the <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;url&#45;serializer"></a>[serialization](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-serializer) of <var>urlRecord</var>&#46;

<strong>Example&#58;</strong>

In this example&#44; a <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;the&#45;base&#45;element&#45;20"></a><code>[base](#the-base-element)</code> element is used to set the <a id="the&#45;base&#45;element&#58;document&#45;base&#45;url&#45;3"></a>[document base URL](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document-base-url)&#58;

``` text
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
    <head>
        <title>This is an example for the &lt;base&gt; element</title>
        <base href="https://www.example.com/news/index.html">
    </head>
    <body>
        <p>Visit the <a href="archives.html">archives</a>.</p>
    </body>
</html>
```

The link in the above example would be a link to &#34;<code>https&#58;&#47;&#47;www&#46;example&#46;com&#47;news&#47;archives&#46;html</code>&#34;&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;categories"></a>[Categories](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-categories)&#58;</strong>

<a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;metadata&#45;content&#45;2"></a>[Metadata content](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#metadata-content-2)&#46;

If the element is <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;allowed&#45;in&#45;the&#45;body"></a>[allowed in the body](#allowed-in-the-body)&#58; <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;flow&#45;content&#45;2"></a>[flow content](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#flow-content-2)&#46;

If the element is <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;allowed&#45;in&#45;the&#45;body&#45;2"></a>[allowed in the body](#allowed-in-the-body)&#58; <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;phrasing&#45;content&#45;2"></a>[phrasing content](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#phrasing-content-2)&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;contexts"></a>[Contexts in which this element can be used](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-contexts)&#58;</strong>

Where <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;metadata&#45;content&#45;2&#45;2"></a>[metadata content](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#metadata-content-2) is expected&#46;

In a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;noscript&#45;element"></a><code>[noscript](html-language-elements-interactive-scripting--877d6146487f--05bb1d85cc89.md#the-noscript-element)</code> element that is a child of a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;head&#45;element"></a><code>[head](#the-head-element)</code> element&#46;

If the element is <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;allowed&#45;in&#45;the&#45;body&#45;3"></a>[allowed in the body](#allowed-in-the-body)&#58; where <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;phrasing&#45;content&#45;2&#45;2"></a>[phrasing content](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#phrasing-content-2) is expected&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;content&#45;model"></a>[Content model](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-content-model)&#58;</strong>

<a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;content&#45;nothing"></a>[Nothing](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-content-nothing)&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;tag&#45;omission"></a>[Tag omission in text&#47;html](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-tag-omission)&#58;</strong>

No <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;syntax&#45;end&#45;tag"></a>[end tag](html-language-syntax-parser--877d6146487f--37a9ba51eed8.md#syntax-end-tag)&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;attributes"></a>[Content attributes](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-attributes)&#58;</strong>

<a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;global&#45;attributes"></a>[Global attributes](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#global-attributes)

<a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;href"></a><code>[href](#attr-link-href)</code> — Address of the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;hyperlink"></a>[hyperlink](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#hyperlink)

<a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;crossorigin"></a><code>[crossorigin](#attr-link-crossorigin)</code> — How the element handles crossorigin requests

<a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel"></a><code>[rel](#attr-link-rel)</code> — Relationship between the document containing the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;hyperlink&#45;2"></a>[hyperlink](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#hyperlink) and the destination resource

<a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;media"></a><code>[media](#attr-link-media)</code> — Applicable media

<a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;integrity"></a><code>[integrity](#attr-link-integrity)</code> — Integrity metadata used in Subresource Integrity checks [&#91;SRI&#93;](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#refsSRI)

<a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;hreflang"></a><code>[hreflang](#attr-link-hreflang)</code> — Language of the linked resource

<a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;type"></a><code>[type](#attr-link-type)</code> — Hint for the type of the referenced resource

<a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;referrerpolicy"></a><code>[referrerpolicy](#attr-link-referrerpolicy)</code> — <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;referrer&#45;policy"></a>[Referrer policy](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-referrer-policy/#referrer-policy) for <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;fetch"></a>[fetches](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch) initiated by the element

<a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;sizes"></a><code>[sizes](#attr-link-sizes)</code> — Sizes of the icons &#40;for <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel&#45;2"></a><code>[rel](#attr-link-rel)</code>&#61;&#34;<a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;rel&#45;icon"></a><code>[icon](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#rel-icon)</code>&#34;&#41;

<a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;imagesrcset"></a><code>[imagesrcset](#attr-link-imagesrcset)</code> — Images to use in different situations&#44; e&#46;g&#46;&#44; high&#45;resolution displays&#44; small monitors&#44; etc&#46; &#40;for <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel&#45;3"></a><code>[rel](#attr-link-rel)</code>&#61;&#34;<a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;preload"></a><code>[preload](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-preload)</code>&#34;&#41;

<a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;imagesizes"></a><code>[imagesizes](#attr-link-imagesizes)</code> — Image sizes for different page layouts &#40;for <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel&#45;4"></a><code>[rel](#attr-link-rel)</code>&#61;&#34;<a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;preload&#45;2"></a><code>[preload](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-preload)</code>&#34;&#41;

<a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;as"></a><code>[as](#attr-link-as)</code> — Destination for a preload request &#40;for <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel&#45;5"></a><code>[rel](#attr-link-rel)</code>&#61;&#34;<a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;preload&#45;3"></a><code>[preload](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-preload)</code>&#34; and <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel&#45;6"></a><code>[rel](#attr-link-rel)</code>&#61;&#34;<a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;modulepreload"></a><code>[modulepreload](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-modulepreload)</code>&#34;&#41;

<a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;blocking"></a><code>[blocking](#attr-link-blocking)</code> — Whether the element is <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;potentially&#45;render&#45;blocking"></a>[potentially render&#45;blocking](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#potentially-render-blocking)

<a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;color"></a><code>[color](#attr-link-color)</code> — Color to use when customizing a site&#39;s icon &#40;for <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel&#45;7"></a><code>[rel](#attr-link-rel)</code>&#61;&#34;<code>mask&#45;icon</code>&#34;&#41;

<a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;disabled"></a><code>[disabled](#attr-link-disabled)</code> — Whether the link is disabled

<a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;fetchpriority"></a><code>[fetchpriority](#attr-link-fetchpriority)</code> — Sets the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;request&#45;priority"></a>[priority](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-priority) for <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;fetch&#45;2"></a>[fetches](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch) initiated by the element

Also&#44; the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;title"></a><code>[title](#attr-link-title)</code> attribute <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;title&#45;2"></a>[has special semantics](#attr-link-title) on this element&#58; Title of the link&#59; <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;css&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;set&#45;name"></a>[CSS style sheet set name](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#css-style-sheet-set-name)

[For authors](https://w3c.github.io/html-aria/#el-link)&#46;

[For implementers](https://w3c.github.io/html-aam/#el-link)&#46;

id="dom&#45;link&#45;rel"></a><a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;cereactions&#45;4"></a><a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;dom&#45;link&#45;as"></a><a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;cereactions&#45;5"></a><a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;xattr&#45;reflect&#45;3"></a><a id="dom&#45;link&#45;media"></a><a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;cereactions&#45;6"></a><a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;xattr&#45;reflect&#45;4"></a><a id="dom&#45;link&#45;integrity"></a><a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;cereactions&#45;7"></a><a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;xattr&#45;reflect&#45;5"></a><a id="dom&#45;link&#45;hreflang"></a><a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;cereactions&#45;8"></a><a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;xattr&#45;reflect&#45;6"></a><a id="dom&#45;link&#45;type"></a><a
