id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;xattr&#45;reflect&#45;8"></a><a id="dom&#45;link&#45;imagesrcset"></a><a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;cereactions&#45;10"></a><a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;xattr&#45;reflect&#45;9"></a><a id="dom&#45;link&#45;imagesizes"></a><a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;cereactions&#45;11"></a><a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;dom&#45;link&#45;referrerpolicy"></a><a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;dom&#45;domtokenlist&#45;value&#45;3"></a><a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;xattr&#45;reflect&#45;10"></a><a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;domtokenlist&#45;3"></a><a id="dom&#45;link&#45;blocking"></a><a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;cereactions&#45;12"></a><a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;xattr&#45;reflect&#45;11"></a><a id="dom&#45;link&#45;disabled"></a><a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;cereactions&#45;13"></a><a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;dom&#45;link&#45;fetchpriority"></a><a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;htmllinkelement"></a><a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;linkstyle"></a>

``` text
[Exposed=Window]
interface HTMLLinkElement : HTMLElement {
  [HTMLConstructor] constructor();

  [CEReactions, ReflectURL] attribute USVString href;
  [CEReactions] attribute DOMString? crossOrigin;
  [CEReactions, Reflect] attribute DOMString rel;
  [CEReactions] attribute DOMString as;
  [SameObject, PutForwards=value, Reflect="rel"] readonly attribute DOMTokenList relList;
  [CEReactions, Reflect] attribute DOMString media;
  [CEReactions, Reflect] attribute DOMString integrity;
  [CEReactions, Reflect] attribute DOMString hreflang;
  [CEReactions, Reflect] attribute DOMString type;
  [SameObject, PutForwards=value, Reflect] readonly attribute DOMTokenList sizes;
  [CEReactions, Reflect] attribute USVString imageSrcset;
  [CEReactions, Reflect] attribute DOMString imageSizes;
  [CEReactions] attribute DOMString referrerPolicy;
  [SameObject, PutForwards=value, Reflect] readonly attribute DOMTokenList blocking;
  [CEReactions, Reflect] attribute boolean disabled;
  [CEReactions] attribute DOMString fetchPriority;

  // also has obsolete members
};
HTMLLinkElement includes LinkStyle;
```

The <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> element allows authors to link their document to other resources&#46;

The address of the link&#40;s&#41; is given by the <a id="attr&#45;link&#45;href"></a><strong><code>href</code></strong> attribute&#46; If the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;href&#45;2"></a><code>[href](#attr-link-href)</code> attribute is present&#44; then its value must be a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;valid&#45;non&#45;empty&#45;url&#45;potentially&#45;surrounded&#45;by&#45;spaces"></a>[valid non&#45;empty URL potentially surrounded by spaces](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#valid-non-empty-url-potentially-surrounded-by-spaces)&#46; One or both of the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;href&#45;3"></a><code>[href](#attr-link-href)</code> or <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;imagesrcset&#45;2"></a><code>[imagesrcset](#attr-link-imagesrcset)</code> attributes must be present&#46;

If both the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;href&#45;4"></a><code>[href](#attr-link-href)</code> and <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;imagesrcset&#45;3"></a><code>[imagesrcset](#attr-link-imagesrcset)</code> attributes are absent&#44; then the element does not define a link&#46;

The types of link indicated &#40;the relationships&#41; are given by the value of the <a id="attr&#45;link&#45;rel"></a><strong><code>rel</code></strong> attribute&#44; which&#44; if present&#44; must have a value that is a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;unordered&#45;set&#45;of&#45;unique&#45;space&#45;separated&#45;tokens"></a>[unordered set of unique space&#45;separated tokens](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#unordered-set-of-unique-space-separated-tokens)&#46; The [allowed keywords and their meanings](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#linkTypes) are defined in a later section&#46; If the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel&#45;9"></a><code>[rel](#attr-link-rel)</code> attribute is absent&#44; has no keywords&#44; or if none of the keywords used are allowed according to the definitions in this specification&#44; then the element does not create any links&#46;

<a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel&#45;10"></a><code>[rel](#attr-link-rel)</code>&#39;s <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;supported&#45;tokens"></a>[supported tokens](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-supported-tokens) are the keywords defined in [HTML link types](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#linkTypes) which are allowed on <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;2"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> elements&#44; impact the processing model&#44; and are supported by the user agent&#46; The possible <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;supported&#45;tokens&#45;2"></a>[supported tokens](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-supported-tokens) are <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;rel&#45;alternate"></a><code>[alternate](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#rel-alternate)</code>&#44; <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;dns&#45;prefetch"></a><code>[dns&#45;prefetch](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-dns-prefetch)</code>&#44; <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;expect"></a><code>[expect](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-expect)</code>&#44; <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;rel&#45;icon&#45;2"></a><code>[icon](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#rel-icon)</code>&#44; <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;manifest"></a><code>[manifest](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-manifest)</code>&#44; <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;modulepreload&#45;2"></a><code>[modulepreload](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-modulepreload)</code>&#44; <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;next"></a><code>[next](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-next)</code>&#44; <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;pingback"></a><code>[pingback](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-pingback)</code>&#44; <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;preconnect"></a><code>[preconnect](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-preconnect)</code>&#44; <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;prefetch"></a><code>[prefetch](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-prefetch)</code>&#44; <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;preload&#45;4"></a><code>[preload](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-preload)</code>&#44; <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;search"></a><code>[search](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-search)</code>&#44; and <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;stylesheet"></a><code>[stylesheet](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-stylesheet)</code>&#46; <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel&#45;11"></a><code>[rel](#attr-link-rel)</code>&#39;s <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;supported&#45;tokens&#45;3"></a>[supported tokens](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-supported-tokens) must only include the tokens from this list that the user agent implements the processing model for&#46;

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

Theoretically a user agent could support the processing model for the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;canonical"></a><code>[canonical](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-canonical)</code> keyword — if it were a search engine that executed JavaScript&#46; But in practice that&#39;s quite unlikely&#46; So in most cases&#44; <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;canonical&#45;2"></a><code>[canonical](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-canonical)</code> ought not be included in <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel&#45;12"></a><code>[rel](#attr-link-rel)</code>&#39;s <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;supported&#45;tokens&#45;4"></a>[supported tokens](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-supported-tokens)&#46;

A <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;3"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> element must have either a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel&#45;13"></a><code>[rel](#attr-link-rel)</code> attribute or an <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;names&#58;&#45;the&#45;itemprop&#45;attribute"></a><code>[itemprop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#names:-the-itemprop-attribute)</code> attribute&#44; but not both&#46;

If a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;4"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> element has an <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;names&#58;&#45;the&#45;itemprop&#45;attribute&#45;2"></a><code>[itemprop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#names:-the-itemprop-attribute)</code> attribute&#44; or has a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel&#45;14"></a><code>[rel](#attr-link-rel)</code> attribute that contains only keywords that are <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;body&#45;ok"></a>[body&#45;ok](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#body-ok)&#44; then the element is said to be <a id="allowed&#45;in&#45;the&#45;body"></a><strong>allowed in the body</strong>&#46; This means that the element can be used where <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;phrasing&#45;content&#45;2&#45;3"></a>[phrasing content](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#phrasing-content-2) is expected&#46;

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

If the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel&#45;15"></a><code>[rel](#attr-link-rel)</code> attribute is used&#44; the element can only sometimes be used in the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;body&#45;element"></a><code>[body](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#the-body-element)</code> of the page&#46; When used with the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;names&#58;&#45;the&#45;itemprop&#45;attribute&#45;3"></a><code>[itemprop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#names:-the-itemprop-attribute)</code> attribute&#44; the element can be used both in the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;head&#45;element&#45;2"></a><code>[head](#the-head-element)</code> element and in the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;body&#45;element&#45;2"></a><code>[body](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#the-body-element)</code> of the page&#44; subject to the constraints of the microdata model&#46;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

Two categories of links can be created using the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;5"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> element&#58; <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;external&#45;resource&#45;link"></a>[links to external resources](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#external-resource-link) and <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;hyperlink&#45;3"></a>[hyperlinks](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#hyperlink)&#46; The [link types section](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#linkTypes) defines whether a particular link type is an external resource or a hyperlink&#46; One <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;6"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> element can create multiple links &#40;of which some might be <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;external&#45;resource&#45;link&#45;2"></a>[external resource links](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#external-resource-link) and some might be <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;hyperlink&#45;4"></a>[hyperlinks](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#hyperlink)&#41;&#59; exactly which and how many links are created depends on the keywords given in the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel&#45;16"></a><code>[rel](#attr-link-rel)</code> attribute&#46; User agents must process the links on a per&#45;link basis&#44; not a per&#45;element basis&#46;

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

Each link created for a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;7"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> element is handled separately&#46; For instance&#44; if there are two <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;8"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> elements with <code>rel&#61;&#34;stylesheet&#34;</code>&#44; they each count as a separate external resource&#44; and each is affected by its own attributes independently&#46; Similarly&#44; if a single <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;9"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> element has a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel&#45;17"></a><code>[rel](#attr-link-rel)</code> attribute with the value <code>next stylesheet</code>&#44; it creates both a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;hyperlink&#45;5"></a>[hyperlink](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#hyperlink) &#40;for the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;next&#45;2"></a><code>[next](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-next)</code> keyword&#41; and an <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;external&#45;resource&#45;link&#45;3"></a>[external resource link](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#external-resource-link) &#40;for the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;stylesheet&#45;2"></a><code>[stylesheet](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-stylesheet)</code> keyword&#41;&#44; and they are affected by other attributes &#40;such as <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;media&#45;2"></a><code>[media](#attr-link-media)</code> or <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;title&#45;3"></a><code>[title](#attr-link-title)</code>&#41; differently&#46;

<strong>Example&#58;</strong>

For example&#44; the following <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;10"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> element creates two <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;hyperlink&#45;6"></a>[hyperlinks](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#hyperlink) &#40;to the same page&#41;&#58;

``` text
<link rel="author license" href="/about">
```

The two links created by this element are one whose semantic is that the target page has information about the current page&#39;s author&#44; and one whose semantic is that the target page has information regarding the license under which the current page is provided&#46;

<a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;hyperlink&#45;7"></a>[Hyperlinks](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#hyperlink) created with the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;11"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> element and its <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel&#45;18"></a><code>[rel](#attr-link-rel)</code> attribute apply to the whole document&#46; This contrasts with the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;hyperlink&#45;rel"></a><code>[rel](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#attr-hyperlink-rel)</code> attribute of <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;a&#45;element"></a><code>[a](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#the-a-element)</code> and <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;area&#45;element"></a><code>[area](html-language-elements-embedded--877d6146487f--48a1a13e5cfe.md#the-area-element)</code> elements&#44; which indicates the type of a link whose context is given by the link&#39;s location within the document&#46;

Unlike those created by <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;a&#45;element&#45;2"></a><code>[a](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#the-a-element)</code> and <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;area&#45;element&#45;2"></a><code>[area](html-language-elements-embedded--877d6146487f--48a1a13e5cfe.md#the-area-element)</code> elements&#44; <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;hyperlink&#45;8"></a>[hyperlinks](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#hyperlink) created by <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;12"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> elements are not displayed as part of the document by default&#44; in user agents that [support the suggested default rendering](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#renderingUA)&#46; And even if they are force&#45;displayed using CSS&#44; they have no <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;activation&#45;behaviour"></a>[activation behavior](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#eventtarget-activation-behavior)&#46; Instead&#44; they primarily provide semantic information which might be used by the page or by other software that consumes the page&#39;s contents&#46; Additionally&#44; the user agent can [provide its own UI for following such hyperlinks](#providing-users-with-a-means-to-follow-hyperlinks-created-using-the-link-element)&#46;

The exact behavior for <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;external&#45;resource&#45;link&#45;4"></a>[links to external resources](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#external-resource-link) depends on the exact relationship&#44; as defined for the relevant [link type](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#linkTypes)&#46;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

The <a id="attr&#45;link&#45;crossorigin"></a><strong><code>crossorigin</code></strong> attribute is a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;cors&#45;settings&#45;attribute"></a>[CORS settings attribute](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#cors-settings-attribute)&#46; It is intended for use with <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;external&#45;resource&#45;link&#45;5"></a>[external resource links](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#external-resource-link)&#46;

The <a id="attr&#45;link&#45;media"></a><strong><code>media</code></strong> attribute says which media the resource applies to&#46; The value must be a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;valid&#45;media&#45;query&#45;list"></a>[valid media query list](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#valid-media-query-list)&#46;

<strong>✔</strong>MDN

[Subresource&#95;Integrity](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/Security/Subresource_Integrity)

Support in all current engines&#46;

Firefox43&#43;Safari11&#46;1&#43;Chrome45&#43;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

Opera&#63;Edge79&#43;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

Edge &#40;Legacy&#41;17&#43;Internet ExplorerNo

------------------------------------------------------------------------

Firefox Android&#63;Safari iOS&#63;Chrome Android&#63;WebView Android&#63;Samsung Internet&#63;Opera Android&#63;

The <a id="attr&#45;link&#45;integrity"></a><strong><code>integrity</code></strong> attribute represents the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;request&#45;integrity&#45;metadata"></a>[integrity metadata](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-integrity-metadata) for requests which this element is responsible for&#46; The value is text&#46; The attribute must only be specified on <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;13"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> elements that have a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel&#45;19"></a><code>[rel](#attr-link-rel)</code> attribute that contains the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;stylesheet&#45;3"></a><code>[stylesheet](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-stylesheet)</code>&#44; <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;preload&#45;5"></a><code>[preload](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-preload)</code>&#44; or <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;modulepreload&#45;3"></a><code>[modulepreload](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-modulepreload)</code> keyword&#46; [&#91;SRI&#93;](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#refsSRI)

The <a id="attr&#45;link&#45;hreflang"></a><strong><code>hreflang</code></strong> attribute on the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;14"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> element has the same semantics as the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;hyperlink&#45;hreflang"></a>[<code>hreflang</code> attribute on the <code>a</code> element](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#attr-hyperlink-hreflang)&#46;

The <a id="attr&#45;link&#45;type"></a><strong><code>type</code></strong> attribute gives the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;mime&#45;type"></a>[MIME type](https://mimesniff.spec.whatwg.org/#mime-type) of the linked resource&#46; It is purely advisory&#46; The value must be a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;valid&#45;mime&#45;type&#45;string"></a>[valid MIME type string](https://mimesniff.spec.whatwg.org/#valid-mime-type)&#46;

For <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;external&#45;resource&#45;link&#45;6"></a>[external resource links](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#external-resource-link)&#44; the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;type&#45;2"></a><code>[type](#attr-link-type)</code> attribute is used as a hint to user agents so that they can avoid fetching resources they do not support&#46;

The <a id="attr&#45;link&#45;referrerpolicy"></a><strong><code>referrerpolicy</code></strong> attribute is a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;referrer&#45;policy&#45;attribute"></a>[referrer policy attribute](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#referrer-policy-attribute)&#46; It is intended for use with <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;external&#45;resource&#45;link&#45;7"></a>[external resource links](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#external-resource-link)&#44; where it helps set the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;referrer&#45;policy&#45;2"></a>[referrer policy](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-referrer-policy/#referrer-policy) used when <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;fetch&#45;and&#45;process&#45;the&#45;linked&#45;resource"></a>[fetching and processing the linked resource](#fetch-and-process-the-linked-resource)&#46; [&#91;REFERRERPOLICY&#93;](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#refsREFERRERPOLICY)

The <a id="attr&#45;link&#45;title"></a><strong><code>title</code></strong> attribute gives the title of the link&#46; With one exception&#44; it is purely advisory&#46; The value is text&#46; The exception is for style sheet links that are <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;in&#45;a&#45;document&#45;tree"></a>[in a document tree](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#in-a-document-tree)&#44; for which the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;title&#45;4"></a><code>[title](#attr-link-title)</code> attribute defines <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;css&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;set"></a>[CSS style sheet sets](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#css-style-sheet-set)&#46;

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

The <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;title&#45;5"></a><code>[title](#attr-link-title)</code> attribute on <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;15"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> elements differs from the global <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;title"></a><code>[title](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#attr-title)</code> attribute of most other elements in that a link without a title does not inherit the title of the parent element&#58; it merely has no title&#46;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

The <a id="attr&#45;link&#45;imagesrcset"></a><strong><code>imagesrcset</code></strong> attribute may be present&#44; and is a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;srcset&#45;attribute"></a>[srcset attribute](html-language-elements-embedded--877d6146487f--48a1a13e5cfe.md#srcset-attribute)&#46;

The <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;imagesrcset&#45;4"></a><code>[imagesrcset](#attr-link-imagesrcset)</code> and <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;href&#45;5"></a><code>[href](#attr-link-href)</code> attributes &#40;if <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;width&#45;descriptor"></a>[width descriptors](html-language-elements-embedded--877d6146487f--48a1a13e5cfe.md#width-descriptor) are not used&#41; together contribute the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;image&#45;source"></a>[image sources](html-language-elements-embedded--877d6146487f--48a1a13e5cfe.md#image-source) to the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;source&#45;set"></a>[source set](html-language-elements-embedded--877d6146487f--48a1a13e5cfe.md#source-set)&#46;

If the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;imagesrcset&#45;5"></a><code>[imagesrcset](#attr-link-imagesrcset)</code> attribute is present and has any <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;image&#45;candidate&#45;string"></a>[image candidate strings](html-language-elements-embedded--877d6146487f--48a1a13e5cfe.md#image-candidate-string) using a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;width&#45;descriptor&#45;2"></a>[width descriptor](html-language-elements-embedded--877d6146487f--48a1a13e5cfe.md#width-descriptor)&#44; the <a id="attr&#45;link&#45;imagesizes"></a><strong><code>imagesizes</code></strong> attribute must also be present&#44; and is a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;sizes&#45;attribute"></a>[sizes attribute](html-language-elements-embedded--877d6146487f--48a1a13e5cfe.md#sizes-attribute)&#46; The <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;imagesizes&#45;2"></a><code>[imagesizes](#attr-link-imagesizes)</code> attribute contributes the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;source&#45;size&#45;2"></a>[source size](html-language-elements-embedded--877d6146487f--48a1a13e5cfe.md#source-size-2) to the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;source&#45;set&#45;2"></a>[source set](html-language-elements-embedded--877d6146487f--48a1a13e5cfe.md#source-set)&#46;

The <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;imagesrcset&#45;6"></a><code>[imagesrcset](#attr-link-imagesrcset)</code> and <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;imagesizes&#45;3"></a><code>[imagesizes](#attr-link-imagesizes)</code> attributes must only be specified on <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;16"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> elements that have both a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel&#45;20"></a><code>[rel](#attr-link-rel)</code> attribute that specifies the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;preload&#45;6"></a><code>[preload](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-preload)</code> keyword&#44; as well as an <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;as&#45;2"></a><code>[as](#attr-link-as)</code> attribute in the &#34;<code>image</code>&#34; state&#46;

<strong>Example&#58;</strong>

These attributes allow preloading the appropriate resource that is later used by an <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;img&#45;element"></a><code>[img](html-language-elements-embedded--877d6146487f--48a1a13e5cfe.md#the-img-element)</code> element that has the corresponding values for its <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;img&#45;srcset"></a><code>[srcset](html-language-elements-embedded--877d6146487f--48a1a13e5cfe.md#attr-img-srcset)</code> and <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;img&#45;sizes"></a><code>[sizes](html-language-elements-embedded--877d6146487f--48a1a13e5cfe.md#attr-img-sizes)</code> attributes&#58;

``` text
<link rel="preload" as="image"
      imagesrcset="wolf_400px.jpg 400w, wolf_800px.jpg 800w, wolf_1600px.jpg 1600w"
      imagesizes="50vw">

<!-- ... later, or perhaps inserted dynamically ... -->
<img src="wolf.jpg" alt="A rad wolf"
     srcset="wolf_400px.jpg 400w, wolf_800px.jpg 800w, wolf_1600px.jpg 1600w"
     sizes="50vw">
```

Note how we omit the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;href&#45;6"></a><code>[href](#attr-link-href)</code> attribute&#44; as it would only be relevant for browsers that do not support <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;imagesrcset&#45;7"></a><code>[imagesrcset](#attr-link-imagesrcset)</code>&#44; and in those cases it would likely cause the incorrect image to be preloaded&#46;

<strong>Example&#58;</strong>

The <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;imagesrcset&#45;8"></a><code>[imagesrcset](#attr-link-imagesrcset)</code> attribute can be combined with the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;media&#45;3"></a><code>[media](#attr-link-media)</code> attribute to preload the appropriate resource selected from a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;picture&#45;element"></a><code>[picture](html-language-elements-embedded--877d6146487f--48a1a13e5cfe.md#the-picture-element)</code> element&#39;s sources&#44; for <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;art&#45;direction"></a>[art direction](html-language-elements-embedded--877d6146487f--48a1a13e5cfe.md#art-direction)&#58;

``` text
<link rel="preload" as="image"
      imagesrcset="dog-cropped-1x.jpg, dog-cropped-2x.jpg 2x"
      media="(max-width: 800px)">
<link rel="preload" as="image"
      imagesrcset="dog-wide-1x.jpg, dog-wide-2x.jpg 2x"
      media="(min-width: 801px)">

<!-- ... later, or perhaps inserted dynamically ... -->
<picture>
  <source srcset="dog-cropped-1x.jpg, dog-cropped-2x.jpg 2x"
          media="(max-width: 800px)">
  <img src="dog-wide-1x.jpg" srcset="dog-wide-2x.jpg 2x"
       alt="An awesome dog">
</picture>
```

------------------------------------------------------------------------

The <a id="attr&#45;link&#45;sizes"></a><strong><code>sizes</code></strong> attribute gives the sizes of icons for visual media&#46; Its value&#44; if present&#44; is merely advisory&#46; User agents may use the value to decide which icon&#40;s&#41; to use if multiple icons are available&#46; If specified&#44; the attribute must have a value that is an <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;unordered&#45;set&#45;of&#45;unique&#45;space&#45;separated&#45;tokens&#45;2"></a>[unordered set of unique space&#45;separated tokens](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#unordered-set-of-unique-space-separated-tokens) which are <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;ascii&#45;case&#45;insensitive"></a>[ASCII case&#45;insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-case-insensitive)&#46; Each value must be either an <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;ascii&#45;case&#45;insensitive&#45;2"></a>[ASCII case&#45;insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-case-insensitive) match for the string &#34;<a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;sizes&#45;any"></a><code>[any](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#attr-link-sizes-any)</code>&#34;&#44; or a value that consists of two <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;valid&#45;non&#45;negative&#45;integer"></a>[valid non&#45;negative integers](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#valid-non-negative-integer) that do not have a leading U&#43;0030 DIGIT ZERO &#40;0&#41; character and that are separated by a single U&#43;0078 LATIN SMALL LETTER X or U&#43;0058 LATIN CAPITAL LETTER X character&#46; The attribute must only be specified on <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;17"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> elements that have a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel&#45;21"></a><code>[rel](#attr-link-rel)</code> attribute that specifies the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;rel&#45;icon&#45;3"></a><code>[icon](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#rel-icon)</code> keyword or the <code>apple&#45;touch&#45;icon</code> keyword&#46;

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

The <code>apple&#45;touch&#45;icon</code> keyword is a registered <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;rel&#45;extensions"></a>[extension to the predefined set of link types](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#concept-rel-extensions)&#44; but user agents are not required to support it in any way&#46;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

The <a id="attr&#45;link&#45;as"></a><strong><code>as</code></strong> attribute specifies either a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;preload&#45;destination"></a>[preload destination](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#preload-destination) or a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;module&#45;preload&#45;destination"></a>[module preload destination](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#module-preload-destination) for a preload request for the resource given by the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;href&#45;7"></a><code>[href](#attr-link-href)</code> attribute&#46; It is an <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;enumerated&#45;attribute"></a>[enumerated attribute](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#enumerated-attribute)&#46; Each of the union of <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;preload&#45;destination&#45;2"></a>[preload destinations](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#preload-destination) and <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;module&#45;preload&#45;destination&#45;2"></a>[module preload destinations](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#module-preload-destination) is a keyword for this attribute&#44; mapping to a state of the same name&#46; The attribute must be specified on <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;18"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> elements that have a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel&#45;22"></a><code>[rel](#attr-link-rel)</code> attribute that contains the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;preload&#45;7"></a><code>[preload](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-preload)</code> keyword&#59; in such cases it must have a value which is a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;preload&#45;destination&#45;3"></a>[preload destination](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#preload-destination)&#46; It may be specified on <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;19"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> elements that have a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel&#45;23"></a><code>[rel](#attr-link-rel)</code> attribute that contains the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;modulepreload&#45;4"></a><code>[modulepreload](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-modulepreload)</code> keyword&#59; in such cases it must have a value which is a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;module&#45;preload&#45;destination&#45;3"></a>[module preload destination](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#module-preload-destination)&#46; For other <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;20"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> elements&#44; it must not be specified&#46;

The processing model for how the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;as&#45;3"></a><code>[as](#attr-link-as)</code> attribute is used is given in an individual link type&#39;s <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;fetch&#45;and&#45;process&#45;the&#45;linked&#45;resource&#45;2"></a>[fetch and process the linked resource](#fetch-and-process-the-linked-resource) algorithm&#46;

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

The attribute does not have a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;missing&#45;value&#45;default"></a><em>[missing value default](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#missing-value-default)</em> or <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;invalid&#45;value&#45;default"></a><em>[invalid value default](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#invalid-value-default)</em>&#44; meaning that invalid or missing values for the attribute map to no state&#46; This is accounted for in the processing model&#46; For <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;preload&#45;8"></a><code>[preload](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-preload)</code> links&#44; both conditions are an error&#59; for <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;modulepreload&#45;5"></a><code>[modulepreload](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-modulepreload)</code> links&#44; a missing value will be treated as &#34;<code>script</code>&#34;&#46;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

The <a id="attr&#45;link&#45;blocking"></a><strong><code>blocking</code></strong> attribute is a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;blocking&#45;attribute"></a>[blocking attribute](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#blocking-attribute)&#46; It is used by link types <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;stylesheet&#45;4"></a><code>[stylesheet](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-stylesheet)</code> and <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;expect&#45;2"></a><code>[expect](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-expect)</code>&#44; and it must only be specified on link elements that have a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel&#45;24"></a><code>[rel](#attr-link-rel)</code> attribute containing those keywords&#46;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

The <a id="attr&#45;link&#45;color"></a><strong><code>color</code></strong> attribute is used with the <code>mask&#45;icon</code> link type&#46; The attribute must only be specified on <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;21"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> elements that have a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel&#45;25"></a><code>[rel](#attr-link-rel)</code> attribute that contains the <code>mask&#45;icon</code> keyword&#46; The value must be a string that matches the CSS <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;color"></a>[&#60;color&#62;](https://drafts.csswg.org/css-color/#typedef-color) production&#44; defining a suggested color that user agents can use to customize the display of the icon that the user sees when they pin your site&#46;

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

This specification does not have any user agent requirements for the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;color&#45;2"></a><code>[color](#attr-link-color)</code> attribute&#46;

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

The <code>mask&#45;icon</code> keyword is a registered <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;rel&#45;extensions&#45;2"></a>[extension to the predefined set of link types](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#concept-rel-extensions)&#44; but user agents are not required to support it in any way&#46;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

<a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;22"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> elements have an associated <a id="explicitly&#45;enabled"></a><strong>explicitly enabled</strong> boolean&#46; It is initially false&#46;

The <a id="attr&#45;link&#45;disabled"></a><strong><code>disabled</code></strong> attribute is a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;boolean&#45;attribute"></a>[boolean attribute](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#boolean-attribute) that is used with the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;stylesheet&#45;5"></a><code>[stylesheet](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-stylesheet)</code> link type&#46; The attribute must only be specified on <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;23"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> elements that have a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel&#45;26"></a><code>[rel](#attr-link-rel)</code> attribute that contains the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;stylesheet&#45;6"></a><code>[stylesheet](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-stylesheet)</code> keyword&#46;

Whenever the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;disabled&#45;2"></a><code>[disabled](#attr-link-disabled)</code> attribute is removed&#44; set the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;24"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> element&#39;s <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;explicitly&#45;enabled"></a>[explicitly enabled](#explicitly-enabled) attribute to true&#46;

<strong>Example&#58;</strong>

Removing the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;disabled&#45;3"></a><code>[disabled](#attr-link-disabled)</code> attribute dynamically&#44; e&#46;g&#46;&#44; using <code>document&#46;querySelector&#40;&#34;link&#34;&#41;&#46;removeAttribute&#40;&#34;disabled&#34;&#41;</code>&#44; will fetch and apply the style sheet&#58;

``` text
<link disabled rel="alternate stylesheet" href="css/pooh">
```

MDN

[HTMLLinkElement&#47;fetchPriority](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/HTMLLinkElement/fetchPriority)

The <a id="attr&#45;link&#45;fetchpriority"></a><strong><code>fetchpriority</code></strong> attribute is a <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;fetch&#45;priority&#45;attribute"></a>[fetch priority attribute](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#fetch-priority-attribute) that is intended for use with <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;external&#45;resource&#45;link&#45;8"></a>[external resource links](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#external-resource-link)&#44; where it is used to set the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;request&#45;priority&#45;2"></a>[priority](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-priority) used when <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;fetch&#45;and&#45;process&#45;the&#45;linked&#45;resource&#45;3"></a>[fetching and processing the linked resource](#fetch-and-process-the-linked-resource)&#46;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

There is no reflecting IDL attribute for the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;color&#45;3"></a><code>[color](#attr-link-color)</code> attribute&#44; but this might be added later&#46;

<strong>✔</strong>MDN

[HTMLLinkElement&#47;as](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/HTMLLinkElement/as)

Support in all current engines&#46;

Firefox Android&#63;Safari iOS&#63;Chrome Android&#63;WebView Android&#63;Samsung Internet&#63;Opera Android&#63;

The <a id="dom&#45;link&#45;as"></a><strong><code>as</code></strong> IDL attribute must <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;reflect"></a>[reflect](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#reflect) the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;as&#45;4"></a><code>[as](#attr-link-as)</code> content attribute&#44; <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;limited&#45;to&#45;only&#45;known&#45;values"></a>[limited to only known values](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#limited-to-only-known-values)&#46;

The <a id="dom&#45;link&#45;crossorigin"></a><strong><code>crossOrigin</code></strong> IDL attribute must <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;reflect&#45;2"></a>[reflect](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#reflect) the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;crossorigin&#45;2"></a><code>[crossorigin](#attr-link-crossorigin)</code> content attribute&#44; <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;limited&#45;to&#45;only&#45;known&#45;values&#45;2"></a>[limited to only known values](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#limited-to-only-known-values)&#46;

<strong>✔</strong>MDN

Support in all current engines&#46;

Firefox50&#43;Safari14&#46;1&#43;Chrome58&#43;
