------------------------------------------------------------------------

Firefox Android&#63;Safari iOS&#63;Chrome Android&#63;WebView Android&#63;Samsung Internet&#63;Opera Android&#63;

The <a id="dom&#45;link&#45;referrerpolicy"></a><strong><code>referrerPolicy</code></strong> IDL attribute must <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;reflect&#45;3"></a>[reflect](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#reflect) the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;referrerpolicy&#45;2"></a><code>[referrerpolicy](#attr-link-referrerpolicy)</code> content attribute&#44; <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;limited&#45;to&#45;only&#45;known&#45;values&#45;3"></a>[limited to only known values](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#limited-to-only-known-values)&#46;

The <a id="dom&#45;link&#45;fetchpriority"></a><strong><code>fetchPriority</code></strong> IDL attribute must <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;reflect&#45;4"></a>[reflect](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#reflect) the <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;fetchpriority&#45;2"></a><code>[fetchpriority](#attr-link-fetchpriority)</code> content attribute&#44; <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;limited&#45;to&#45;only&#45;known&#45;values&#45;4"></a>[limited to only known values](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#limited-to-only-known-values)&#46;

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

The <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;dom&#45;link&#45;rellist"></a><code>[relList](#dom-link-rellist)</code> attribute can be used for feature detection&#44; by calling its <a id="the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;dom&#45;domtokenlist&#45;supports"></a><code>[supports&#40;&#41;](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#dom-domtokenlist-supports)</code> method to check which [types of links](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#linkTypes) are supported&#46;

##### <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;media&#45;attribute"></a>4&#46;2&#46;4&#46;1 Processing the <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;media&#45;attribute&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;media"></a><code>[media](#attr-link-media)</code> attribute[](#processing-the-media-attribute)

If the link is a <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;media&#45;attribute&#58;hyperlink"></a>[hyperlink](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#hyperlink) then the <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;media&#45;attribute&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;media&#45;2"></a><code>[media](#attr-link-media)</code> attribute is purely advisory&#44; and describes for which media the document in question was designed&#46;

However&#44; if the link is an <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;media&#45;attribute&#58;external&#45;resource&#45;link"></a>[external resource link](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#external-resource-link)&#44; then the <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;media&#45;attribute&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;media&#45;3"></a><code>[media](#attr-link-media)</code> attribute is prescriptive&#46; The user agent must apply the external resource when the <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;media&#45;attribute&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;media&#45;4"></a><code>[media](#attr-link-media)</code> attribute&#39;s value <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;media&#45;attribute&#58;matches&#45;the&#45;environment"></a>[matches the environment](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#matches-the-environment) and the other relevant conditions apply&#44; and must not apply it otherwise&#46;

<a id="default&#45;media"></a>The default&#44; if the <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;media&#45;attribute&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;media&#45;5"></a><code>[media](#attr-link-media)</code> attribute is omitted&#44; is &#34;<code>all</code>&#34;&#44; meaning that by default links apply to all media&#46;

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

The external resource might have further restrictions defined within that limit its applicability&#46; For example&#44; a CSS style sheet might have some <code>&#64;media</code> blocks&#46; This specification does not override such further restrictions or requirements&#46;

##### <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute"></a>4&#46;2&#46;4&#46;2 Processing the <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;type"></a><code>[type](#attr-link-type)</code> attribute[](#processing-the-type-attribute)

If the <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;type&#45;2"></a><code>[type](#attr-link-type)</code> attribute is present&#44; then the user agent must assume that the resource is of the given type &#40;even if that is not a <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute&#58;valid&#45;mime&#45;type&#45;string"></a>[valid MIME type string](https://mimesniff.spec.whatwg.org/#valid-mime-type)&#44; e&#46;g&#46; the empty string&#41;&#46; If the attribute is omitted&#44; but the <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute&#58;external&#45;resource&#45;link"></a>[external resource link](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#external-resource-link) type has a default type defined&#44; then the user agent must assume that the resource is of that type&#46; If the UA does not support the given <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute&#58;mime&#45;type"></a>[MIME type](https://mimesniff.spec.whatwg.org/#mime-type) for the given link relationship&#44; then the UA should not <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute&#58;fetch&#45;and&#45;process&#45;the&#45;linked&#45;resource"></a>[fetch and process the linked resource](#fetch-and-process-the-linked-resource)&#59; if the UA does support the given <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute&#58;mime&#45;type&#45;2"></a>[MIME type](https://mimesniff.spec.whatwg.org/#mime-type) for the given link relationship&#44; then the UA should <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute&#58;fetch&#45;and&#45;process&#45;the&#45;linked&#45;resource&#45;2"></a>[fetch and process the linked resource](#fetch-and-process-the-linked-resource) at the appropriate time as specified for the <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute&#58;external&#45;resource&#45;link&#45;2"></a>[external resource link](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#external-resource-link)&#39;s particular type&#46; If the attribute is omitted&#44; and the <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute&#58;external&#45;resource&#45;link&#45;3"></a>[external resource link](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#external-resource-link) type does not have a default type defined&#44; but the user agent would <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute&#58;fetch&#45;and&#45;process&#45;the&#45;linked&#45;resource&#45;3"></a>[fetch and process the linked resource](#fetch-and-process-the-linked-resource) if the type was known and supported&#44; then the user agent should <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute&#58;fetch&#45;and&#45;process&#45;the&#45;linked&#45;resource&#45;4"></a>[fetch and process the linked resource](#fetch-and-process-the-linked-resource) under the assumption that it will be supported&#46;

User agents must not consider the <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;type&#45;3"></a><code>[type](#attr-link-type)</code> attribute authoritative — upon fetching the resource&#44; user agents must not use the <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;type&#45;4"></a><code>[type](#attr-link-type)</code> attribute to determine its actual type&#46; Only the actual type &#40;as defined in the next paragraph&#41; is used to determine whether to <em>apply</em> the resource&#44; not the aforementioned assumed type&#46;

<a id="concept&#45;link&#45;type&#45;sniffing"></a>If the <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute&#58;external&#45;resource&#45;link&#45;4"></a>[external resource link](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#external-resource-link) type defines rules for processing the resource&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute&#58;content&#45;type"></a>[Content&#45;Type metadata](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#content-type)&#44; then those rules apply&#46; Otherwise&#44; if the resource is expected to be an image&#44; user agents may apply the <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute&#58;content&#45;type&#45;sniffing&#58;&#45;image"></a>[image sniffing rules](https://mimesniff.spec.whatwg.org/#rules-for-sniffing-images-specifically)&#44; with the <var>official type</var> being the type determined from the resource&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute&#58;content&#45;type&#45;2"></a>[Content&#45;Type metadata](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#content-type)&#44; and use the resulting <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute&#58;content&#45;type&#45;sniffing&#45;2"></a>[computed type of the resource](https://mimesniff.spec.whatwg.org/#computed-mime-type) as if it was the actual type&#46; Otherwise&#44; if neither of these conditions apply or if the user agent opts not to apply the image sniffing rules&#44; then the user agent must use the resource&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute&#58;content&#45;type&#45;3"></a>[Content&#45;Type metadata](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#content-type) to determine the type of the resource&#46; If there is no type metadata&#44; but the <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute&#58;external&#45;resource&#45;link&#45;5"></a>[external resource link](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#external-resource-link) type has a default type defined&#44; then the user agent must assume that the resource is of that type&#46;

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

The <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute&#58;link&#45;type&#45;stylesheet"></a><code>[stylesheet](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-stylesheet)</code> link type defines rules for processing the resource&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute&#58;content&#45;type&#45;4"></a>[Content&#45;Type metadata](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#content-type)&#46;

Once the user agent has established the type of the resource&#44; the user agent must apply the resource if it is of a supported type and the other relevant conditions apply&#44; and must ignore the resource otherwise&#46;

<strong>Example&#58;</strong>

If a document contains style sheet links labeled as follows&#58;

``` text
<link rel="stylesheet" href="A" type="text/plain">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="B" type="text/css">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="C">
```

&#46;&#46;&#46;then a compliant UA that supported only CSS style sheets would fetch the B and C files&#44; and skip the A file &#40;since <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute&#58;text&#47;plain"></a><code>[text&#47;plain](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc2046#section-4.1.3)</code> is not the <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute&#58;mime&#45;type&#45;3"></a>[MIME type](https://mimesniff.spec.whatwg.org/#mime-type) for CSS style sheets&#41;&#46;

For files B and C&#44; it would then check the actual types returned by the server&#46; For those that are sent as <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute&#58;text&#47;css"></a><code>[text&#47;css](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#text/css)</code>&#44; it would apply the styles&#44; but for those labeled as <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute&#58;text&#47;plain&#45;2"></a><code>[text&#47;plain](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc2046#section-4.1.3)</code>&#44; or any other type&#44; it would not&#46;

If one of the two files was returned without a <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute&#58;content&#45;type&#45;5"></a>[Content&#45;Type](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#content-type) metadata&#44; or with a syntactically incorrect type like <code>Content&#45;Type&#58; &#34;null&#34;</code>&#44; then the default type for <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute&#58;link&#45;type&#45;stylesheet&#45;2"></a><code>[stylesheet](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-stylesheet)</code> links would kick in&#46; Since that default type is <a id="processing&#45;the&#45;type&#45;attribute&#58;text&#47;css&#45;2"></a><code>[text&#47;css](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#text/css)</code>&#44; the style sheet <em>would</em> nonetheless be applied&#46;

##### <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element"></a>4&#46;2&#46;4&#46;3 <a id="obtaining&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element"></a>Fetching and processing a resource from a <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> element[](#fetching-and-processing-a-resource-from-a-link-element)

<a id="concept&#45;link&#45;obtain"></a>All <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;external&#45;resource&#45;link"></a>[external resource links](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#external-resource-link) have a <a id="fetch&#45;and&#45;process&#45;the&#45;linked&#45;resource"></a><strong>fetch and process the linked resource</strong> algorithm&#44; which takes a <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;2"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> element <var>el</var>&#46; They also have <a id="linked&#45;resource&#45;fetch&#45;setup&#45;steps"></a><strong>linked resource fetch setup steps</strong> which take a <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;3"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> element <var>el</var> and <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;request"></a>[request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request) <var>request</var>&#46; Individual link types may provide their own <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;fetch&#45;and&#45;process&#45;the&#45;linked&#45;resource"></a>[fetch and process the linked resource](#fetch-and-process-the-linked-resource) algorithm&#44; but unless explicitly stated&#44; they use the <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;default&#45;fetch&#45;and&#45;process&#45;the&#45;linked&#45;resource"></a>[default fetch and process the linked resource](#default-fetch-and-process-the-linked-resource) algorithm&#46; Similarly&#44; individual link types may provide their own <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;linked&#45;resource&#45;fetch&#45;setup&#45;steps"></a>[linked resource fetch setup steps](#linked-resource-fetch-setup-steps)&#44; but unless explicitly stated&#44; these steps just return true&#46;

The <a id="default&#45;fetch&#45;and&#45;process&#45;the&#45;linked&#45;resource"></a><strong>default fetch and process the linked resource</strong>&#44; given a <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;4"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> element <var>el</var>&#44; is as follows&#58;

1.  Let <var>options</var> be the result of <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;create&#45;link&#45;options&#45;from&#45;element"></a>[creating link options](#create-link-options-from-element) from <var>el</var>&#46;

2.  Let <var>request</var> be the result of <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;create&#45;a&#45;link&#45;request"></a>[creating a link request](#create-a-link-request) given <var>options</var>&#46;

3.  If <var>request</var> is null&#44; then return&#46;

4.  Run the <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;linked&#45;resource&#45;fetch&#45;setup&#45;steps&#45;2"></a>[linked resource fetch setup steps](#linked-resource-fetch-setup-steps)&#44; given <var>el</var> and <var>request</var>&#46; If the result is false&#44; then return&#46;

5.  Set <var>request</var>&#39;s <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;request&#45;initiator&#45;type"></a>[initiator type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-initiator-type) to &#34;<code>css</code>&#34; if <var>el</var>&#39;s <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel"></a><code>[rel](#attr-link-rel)</code> attribute contains the keyword <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;type&#45;stylesheet"></a><code>[stylesheet](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-stylesheet)</code>&#59; &#34;<code>link</code>&#34; otherwise&#46;

6.  <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;fetch"></a>[Fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch) <var>request</var> with <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;processresponseconsumebody"></a><em>[processResponseConsumeBody](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#process-response-end-of-body)</em> set to the following steps given <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;response"></a>[response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response) <var>response</var> and null&#44; failure&#44; or a <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;byte&#45;sequence"></a>[byte sequence](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-sequence) <var>bodyBytes</var>&#58;

    1.  Let <var>success</var> be true&#46;

    2.  If any of the following are true&#58;

        - <var>bodyBytes</var> is null or failure&#59; or

        - <var>response</var>&#39;s <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;response&#45;status"></a>[status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-status) is not an <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;ok&#45;status"></a>[ok status](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#ok-status)&#44;

        then set <var>success</var> to false&#46;

        <strong>Note&#58;</strong>

        Note that content&#45;specific errors&#44; e&#46;g&#46;&#44; CSS parse errors or PNG decoding errors&#44; do not affect <var>success</var>&#46;

    3.  Otherwise&#44; wait for the <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;external&#45;resource&#45;link&#45;2"></a>[link resource](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#external-resource-link)&#39;s <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;critical&#45;subresources"></a>[critical subresources](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#critical-subresources) to finish loading&#46;

        The specification that defines a link type&#39;s <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;critical&#45;subresources&#45;2"></a>[critical subresources](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#critical-subresources) &#40;e&#46;g&#46;&#44; CSS&#41; is expected to describe how these subresources are fetched and processed&#46; However&#44; since this is not currently explicit&#44; this specification describes waiting for a <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;external&#45;resource&#45;link&#45;3"></a>[link resource](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#external-resource-link)&#39;s <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;critical&#45;subresources&#45;3"></a>[critical subresources](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#critical-subresources) to be fetched and processed&#44; with the expectation that this will be done correctly&#46;

    4.  <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;process&#45;the&#45;linked&#45;resource"></a>[Process the linked resource](#process-the-linked-resource) given <var>el</var>&#44; <var>success</var>&#44; <var>response</var>&#44; and <var>bodyBytes</var>&#46;

To <a id="create&#45;a&#45;link&#45;request"></a><strong>create a link request</strong> given a <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;processing&#45;options"></a>[link processing options](#link-processing-options) <var>options</var>&#58;

1.  <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;assert"></a>[Assert](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert)&#58; <var>options</var>&#39;s <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;options&#45;href"></a>[href](#link-options-href) is not the empty string&#46;

2.  Let <var>url</var> be the result of <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;encoding&#45;parsing&#45;a&#45;url"></a>[encoding&#45;parsing a URL](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#encoding-parsing-a-url) given <var>options</var>&#39;s <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;options&#45;href&#45;2"></a>[href](#link-options-href)&#44; relative to <var>options</var>&#39;s <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;options&#45;base&#45;url"></a>[base URL](#link-options-base-url)&#46;

    Passing the base URL instead of a document or environment is tracked by [issue &#35;9715](https://github.com/whatwg/html/issues/9715)&#46;

3.  If <var>url</var> is failure&#44; then return null&#46;

4.  Let <var>request</var> be the result of <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;create&#45;a&#45;potential&#45;cors&#45;request"></a>[creating a potential&#45;CORS request](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#create-a-potential-cors-request) given <var>url</var>&#44; <var>options</var>&#39;s <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;options&#45;destination"></a>[destination](#link-options-destination)&#44; and <var>options</var>&#39;s <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;options&#45;crossorigin"></a>[crossorigin](#link-options-crossorigin)&#46;

5.  Set <var>request</var>&#39;s <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;request&#45;policy&#45;container"></a>[policy container](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-policy-container) to <var>options</var>&#39;s <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;options&#45;policy&#45;container"></a>[policy container](#link-options-policy-container)&#46;

6.  Set <var>request</var>&#39;s <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;request&#45;integrity&#45;metadata"></a>[integrity metadata](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-integrity-metadata) to <var>options</var>&#39;s <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;options&#45;integrity"></a>[integrity](#link-options-integrity)&#46;

7.  Set <var>request</var>&#39;s <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;request&#45;nonce&#45;metadata"></a>[cryptographic nonce metadata](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-nonce-metadata) to <var>options</var>&#39;s <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;options&#45;nonce"></a>[cryptographic nonce metadata](#link-options-nonce)&#46;

8.  Set <var>request</var>&#39;s <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;request&#45;referrer&#45;policy"></a>[referrer policy](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-referrer-policy) to <var>options</var>&#39;s <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;options&#45;referrer&#45;policy"></a>[referrer policy](#link-options-referrer-policy)&#46;

9.  Set <var>request</var>&#39;s <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;request&#45;client"></a>[client](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request-client) to <var>options</var>&#39;s <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;options&#45;environment"></a>[environment](#link-options-environment)&#46;

10. Set <var>request</var>&#39;s <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;request&#45;priority"></a>[priority](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-priority) to <var>options</var>&#39;s <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;link&#45;options&#45;fetch&#45;priority"></a>[fetch priority](#link-options-fetch-priority)&#46;

11. Return <var>request</var>&#46;

User agents may opt to only try to <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;fetch&#45;and&#45;process&#45;the&#45;linked&#45;resource&#45;2"></a>[fetch and process](#fetch-and-process-the-linked-resource) such resources when they are needed&#44; instead of pro&#45;actively fetching all the <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;external&#45;resource&#45;link&#45;4"></a>[external resources](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#external-resource-link) that are not applied&#46;

Similar to the <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;fetch&#45;and&#45;process&#45;the&#45;linked&#45;resource&#45;3"></a>[fetch and process the linked resource](#fetch-and-process-the-linked-resource) algorithm&#44; all <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;external&#45;resource&#45;link&#45;5"></a>[external resource links](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#external-resource-link) have a <a id="process&#45;the&#45;linked&#45;resource"></a><strong>process the linked resource</strong> algorithm which takes a <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;5"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> element <var>el</var>&#44; boolean <var>success</var>&#44; a <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;response&#45;2"></a>[response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response) <var>response</var>&#44; and a <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;byte&#45;sequence&#45;2"></a>[byte sequence](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#byte-sequence) <var>bodyBytes</var>&#46; Individual link types may provide their own <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;process&#45;the&#45;linked&#45;resource&#45;2"></a>[process the linked resource](#process-the-linked-resource) algorithm&#44; but unless explicitly stated&#44; that algorithm does nothing&#46;

Unless otherwise specified for a given <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel&#45;2"></a><code>[rel](#attr-link-rel)</code> keyword&#44; the element must <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;delay&#45;the&#45;load&#45;event"></a>[delay the load event](html-language-syntax-parser--877d6146487f--37a9ba51eed8.md#delay-the-load-event) of the element&#39;s <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;node&#45;document"></a>[node document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-node-document) until all the attempts to <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;fetch&#45;and&#45;process&#45;the&#45;linked&#45;resource&#45;4"></a>[fetch and process the linked resource](#fetch-and-process-the-linked-resource) and its <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;critical&#45;subresources&#45;4"></a>[critical subresources](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#critical-subresources) are complete&#46; &#40;Resources that the user agent has not yet attempted to fetch and process&#44; e&#46;g&#46;&#44; because it is waiting for the resource to be needed&#44; do not <a id="fetching&#45;and&#45;processing&#45;a&#45;resource&#45;from&#45;a&#45;link&#45;element&#58;delay&#45;the&#45;load&#45;event&#45;2"></a>[delay the load event](html-language-syntax-parser--877d6146487f--37a9ba51eed8.md#delay-the-load-event)&#46;&#41;

##### <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers"></a>4&#46;2&#46;4&#46;4 Processing &#96;<a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;http&#45;link"></a><code>[Link](https://httpwg.org/specs/rfc8288.html#header)</code>&#96; headers[](#processing-link-headers)

All link types that can be <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;external&#45;resource&#45;link"></a>[external resource links](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#external-resource-link) define a <a id="process&#45;a&#45;link&#45;header"></a><strong>process a link header</strong> algorithm&#44; which takes a <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;processing&#45;options"></a>[link processing options](#link-processing-options)&#46; This algorithm defines whether and how they react to appearing in an HTTP &#96;<a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;http&#45;link&#45;2"></a><code>[Link](https://httpwg.org/specs/rfc8288.html#header)</code>&#96; response header&#46;

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

For most link types&#44; this algorithm does nothing&#46; The [summary table](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#table-link-relations) is a good reference to quickly know whether a link type has defined <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;process&#45;a&#45;link&#45;header"></a>[process a link header](#process-a-link-header) steps&#46;

A <a id="link&#45;processing&#45;options"></a><strong>link processing options</strong> is a <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;struct"></a>[struct](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct)&#46; It has the following <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;struct&#45;item"></a>[items](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct-item)&#58;

<strong><a id="link&#45;options&#45;href"></a><strong>href</strong> &#40;default the empty string&#41;</strong>

<strong><a id="link&#45;options&#45;initiator"></a><strong>initiator</strong> &#40;default &#34;<code>link</code>&#34;&#41;</strong>

<strong><a id="link&#45;options&#45;integrity"></a><strong>integrity</strong> &#40;default the empty string&#41;</strong>

<strong><a id="link&#45;options&#45;type"></a><strong>type</strong> &#40;default the empty string&#41;</strong>

<strong><a id="link&#45;options&#45;nonce"></a><strong>cryptographic nonce metadata</strong> &#40;default the empty string&#41;</strong>

A string

<strong><a id="link&#45;options&#45;destination"></a><strong>destination</strong> &#40;default the empty string&#41;</strong>

A <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;destination&#45;type"></a>[destination type](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#destination-type)&#46;

<strong><a id="link&#45;options&#45;crossorigin"></a><strong>crossorigin</strong> &#40;default <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;crossorigin&#45;none"></a>[No CORS](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#attr-crossorigin-none)&#41;</strong>

A <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;cors&#45;settings&#45;attribute"></a>[CORS settings attribute](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#cors-settings-attribute) state

<strong><a id="link&#45;options&#45;referrer&#45;policy"></a><strong>referrer policy</strong> &#40;default the empty string&#41;</strong>

A <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;referrer&#45;policy"></a>[referrer policy](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-referrer-policy/#referrer-policy)

<strong><a id="link&#45;options&#45;source&#45;set"></a><strong>source set</strong> &#40;default null&#41;</strong>

Null or a <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;source&#45;set"></a>[source set](html-language-elements-embedded--877d6146487f--48a1a13e5cfe.md#source-set)

<strong><a id="link&#45;options&#45;base&#45;url"></a><strong>base URL</strong></strong>

A <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;url"></a>[URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url)

<strong><a id="link&#45;options&#45;origin"></a><strong>origin</strong></strong>

An <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;concept&#45;origin"></a>[origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#concept-origin)

<strong><a id="link&#45;options&#45;environment"></a><strong>environment</strong></strong>

An <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;environment"></a>[environment](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#environment)

<strong><a id="link&#45;options&#45;policy&#45;container"></a><strong>policy container</strong></strong>

A <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;policy&#45;container"></a>[policy container](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#policy-container)

<strong><a id="link&#45;options&#45;document"></a><strong>document</strong> &#40;default null&#41;</strong>

Null or a <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;document"></a><code>[Document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document)</code>

<strong><a id="link&#45;options&#45;on&#45;document&#45;ready"></a><strong>on document ready</strong> &#40;default null&#41;</strong>

Null or an algorithm accepting a <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;document&#45;2"></a><code>[Document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document)</code>

<strong><a id="link&#45;options&#45;fetch&#45;priority"></a><strong>fetch priority</strong> &#40;default <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;fetchpriority&#45;auto&#45;state"></a><code>[Auto](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#attr-fetchpriority-auto-state)</code>&#41;</strong>

A <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;fetch&#45;priority&#45;attribute"></a>[fetch priority attribute](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#fetch-priority-attribute) state

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

A <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;processing&#45;options&#45;2"></a>[link processing options](#link-processing-options) has a <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;base&#45;url"></a>[base URL](#link-options-base-url) and an <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;href"></a>[href](#link-options-href) rather than a parsed URL because the URL could be a result of the options&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;source&#45;set"></a>[source set](#link-options-source-set)&#46;

To <a id="create&#45;link&#45;options&#45;from&#45;element"></a><strong>create link options from element</strong> given a <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> element <var>el</var>&#58;

1.  Let <var>document</var> be <var>el</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;node&#45;document"></a>[node document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-node-document)&#46;

2.  Let <var>options</var> be a new <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;processing&#45;options&#45;3"></a>[link processing options](#link-processing-options) with

    <strong><a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;crossorigin"></a>[crossorigin](#link-options-crossorigin)</strong>

    the state of <var>el</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;crossorigin"></a><code>[crossorigin](#attr-link-crossorigin)</code> content attribute
    <strong><a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;referrer&#45;policy"></a>[referrer policy](#link-options-referrer-policy)</strong>

    the state of <var>el</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;referrerpolicy"></a><code>[referrerpolicy](#attr-link-referrerpolicy)</code> content attribute
    <strong><a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;source&#45;set&#45;2"></a>[source set](#link-options-source-set)</strong>

    <var>el</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;source&#45;set&#45;2"></a>[source set](html-language-elements-embedded--877d6146487f--48a1a13e5cfe.md#source-set)
    <strong><a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;base&#45;url&#45;2"></a>[base URL](#link-options-base-url)</strong>

    <var>document</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;document&#45;base&#45;url"></a>[document base URL](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document-base-url)
    <strong><a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;origin"></a>[origin](#link-options-origin)</strong>

    <var>document</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;concept&#45;document&#45;origin"></a>[origin](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document-origin)
    <strong><a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;environment"></a>[environment](#link-options-environment)</strong>

    <var>document</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;relevant&#45;settings&#45;object"></a>[relevant settings object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#relevant-settings-object)
    <strong><a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;policy&#45;container"></a>[policy container](#link-options-policy-container)</strong>

    <var>document</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;concept&#45;document&#45;policy&#45;container"></a>[policy container](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-document-policy-container)
    <strong><a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;document"></a>[document](#link-options-document)</strong>

    <var>document</var>
    <strong><a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;nonce"></a>[cryptographic nonce metadata](#link-options-nonce)</strong>

    the current value of <var>el</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;cryptographicnonce"></a>[&#91;&#91;CryptographicNonce&#93;&#93;](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#cryptographicnonce) internal slot
    <strong><a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;fetch&#45;priority"></a>[fetch priority](#link-options-fetch-priority)</strong>

    the state of <var>el</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;fetchpriority"></a><code>[fetchpriority](#attr-link-fetchpriority)</code> content attribute

3.  If <var>el</var> has an <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;href"></a><code>[href](#attr-link-href)</code> attribute&#44; then set <var>options</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;href&#45;2"></a>[href](#link-options-href) to the value of <var>el</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;href&#45;2"></a><code>[href](#attr-link-href)</code> attribute&#46;

4.  If <var>el</var> has an <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;integrity"></a><code>[integrity](#attr-link-integrity)</code> attribute&#44; then set <var>options</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;integrity"></a>[integrity](#link-options-integrity) to the value of <var>el</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;integrity&#45;2"></a><code>[integrity](#attr-link-integrity)</code> content attribute&#46;

5.  If <var>el</var> has a <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;type"></a><code>[type](#attr-link-type)</code> attribute&#44; then set <var>options</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;type"></a>[type](#link-options-type) to the value of <var>el</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;type&#45;2"></a><code>[type](#attr-link-type)</code> attribute&#46;

6.  <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;assert"></a>[Assert](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert)&#58; <var>options</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;href&#45;3"></a>[href](#link-options-href) is not the empty string&#44; or <var>options</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;source&#45;set&#45;3"></a>[source set](#link-options-source-set) is not null&#46;

    A <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;2"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> element with neither an <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;href&#45;3"></a><code>[href](#attr-link-href)</code> or an <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;imagesrcset"></a><code>[imagesrcset](#attr-link-imagesrcset)</code> does not represent a link&#46;

7.  Return <var>options</var>&#46;

To <a id="extract&#45;links&#45;from&#45;headers"></a><strong>extract links from headers</strong> given a <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;concept&#45;header&#45;list"></a>[header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list) <var>headers</var>&#58;

1.  Let <var>links</var> be a new <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;list"></a>[list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list)&#46;

2.  Let <var>rawLinkHeaders</var> be the result of <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;concept&#45;header&#45;list&#45;get&#45;decode&#45;split"></a>[getting&#44; decoding&#44; and splitting](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-header-list-get-decode-split) &#96;<a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;3"></a><code>[Link](#the-link-element)</code>&#96; from <var>headers</var>&#46;

3.  <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;list&#45;iterate"></a>[For each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) <var>linkHeader</var> of <var>rawLinkHeaders</var>&#58;

    1.  Let <var>linkObject</var> be the result of <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;parsing&#45;a&#45;link&#45;field&#45;value"></a>[parsing](https://httpwg.org/specs/rfc8288.html#parse-fv) <var>linkHeader</var>&#46; [&#91;WEBLINK&#93;](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#refsWEBLINK)

    2.  If <var>linkObject</var>&#91;&#34;<code>target&#95;uri</code>&#34;&#93; does not <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;map&#45;exists"></a>[exist](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists)&#44; then <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;continue"></a>[continue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-continue)&#46;

    3.  <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;list&#45;append"></a>[Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append) <var>linkObject</var> to <var>links</var>&#46;

4.  Return <var>links</var>&#46;

To <a id="process&#45;link&#45;headers"></a><strong>process link headers</strong> given a <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;document&#45;3"></a><code>[Document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document)</code> <var>doc</var>&#44; a <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;concept&#45;response"></a>[response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response) <var>response</var>&#44; and a &#34;<code>pre&#45;media</code>&#34; or &#34;<code>media</code>&#34; <var>phase</var>&#58;

1.  Let <var>links</var> be the result of <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;extract&#45;links&#45;from&#45;headers"></a>[extracting links](#extract-links-from-headers) from <var>response</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;concept&#45;response&#45;header&#45;list"></a>[header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list)&#46;

2.  <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;list&#45;iterate&#45;2"></a>[For each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) <var>linkObject</var> in <var>links</var>&#58;

    1.  Let <var>rel</var> be <var>linkObject</var>&#91;&#34;<code>relation&#95;type</code>&#34;&#93;&#46;

    2.  Let <var>attribs</var> be <var>linkObject</var>&#91;&#34;<code>target&#95;attributes</code>&#34;&#93;&#46;

    3.  Let <var>expectedPhase</var> be &#34;<code>media</code>&#34; if either &#34;<a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;img&#45;srcset"></a><code>[srcset](html-language-elements-embedded--877d6146487f--48a1a13e5cfe.md#attr-img-srcset)</code>&#34;&#44; &#34;<a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;imagesrcset&#45;2"></a><code>[imagesrcset](#attr-link-imagesrcset)</code>&#34;&#44; or &#34;<a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;media"></a><code>[media](#attr-link-media)</code>&#34; <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;map&#45;exists&#45;2"></a>[exist](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists) in <var>attribs</var>&#59; otherwise &#34;<code>pre&#45;media</code>&#34;&#46;

    4.  If <var>expectedPhase</var> is not <var>phase</var>&#44; then <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;continue&#45;2"></a>[continue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-continue)&#46;

    5.  If <var>attribs</var>&#91;&#34;<a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;media&#45;2"></a><code>[media](#attr-link-media)</code>&#34;&#93; <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;map&#45;exists&#45;3"></a>[exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists) and <var>attribs</var>&#91;&#34;<a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;media&#45;3"></a><code>[media](#attr-link-media)</code>&#34;&#93; does not <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;matches&#45;the&#45;environment"></a>[match the environment](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#matches-the-environment)&#44; then <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;continue&#45;3"></a>[continue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-continue)&#46;

    6.  Let <var>options</var> be a new <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;processing&#45;options&#45;4"></a>[link processing options](#link-processing-options) with

        <strong><a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;href&#45;4"></a>[href](#link-options-href)</strong>

        <var>linkObject</var>&#91;&#34;<code>target&#95;uri</code>&#34;&#93;
        <strong><a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;base&#45;url&#45;3"></a>[base URL](#link-options-base-url)</strong>

        <var>doc</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;document&#45;base&#45;url&#45;2"></a>[document base URL](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document-base-url)
        <strong><a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;origin&#45;2"></a>[origin](#link-options-origin)</strong>

        <var>doc</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;concept&#45;document&#45;origin&#45;2"></a>[origin](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document-origin)
        <strong><a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;environment&#45;2"></a>[environment](#link-options-environment)</strong>

        <var>doc</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;relevant&#45;settings&#45;object&#45;2"></a>[relevant settings object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#relevant-settings-object)
        <strong><a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;policy&#45;container&#45;2"></a>[policy container](#link-options-policy-container)</strong>

        <var>doc</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;concept&#45;document&#45;policy&#45;container&#45;2"></a>[policy container](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-document-policy-container)
        <strong><a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;document&#45;2"></a>[document](#link-options-document)</strong>

        <var>doc</var>

    7.  <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;apply&#45;link&#45;options&#45;from&#45;parsed&#45;header&#45;attributes"></a>[Apply link options from parsed header attributes](#apply-link-options-from-parsed-header-attributes) to <var>options</var> given <var>attribs</var> and <var>rel</var>&#46; If that returned false&#44; then return&#46;

    8.  If <var>attribs</var>&#91;&#34;<a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;imagesrcset&#45;3"></a><code>[imagesrcset](#attr-link-imagesrcset)</code>&#34;&#93; <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;map&#45;exists&#45;4"></a>[exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists) and <var>attribs</var>&#91;&#34;<a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;imagesizes"></a><code>[imagesizes](#attr-link-imagesizes)</code>&#34;&#93; <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;map&#45;exists&#45;5"></a>[exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists)&#44; then set <var>options</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;source&#45;set&#45;4"></a>[source set](#link-options-source-set) to the result of <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;create&#45;a&#45;source&#45;set"></a>[creating a source set](html-language-elements-embedded--877d6146487f--48a1a13e5cfe.md#create-a-source-set) given <var>linkObject</var>&#91;&#34;<code>target&#95;uri</code>&#34;&#93;&#44; <var>attribs</var>&#91;&#34;<a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;imagesrcset&#45;4"></a><code>[imagesrcset](#attr-link-imagesrcset)</code>&#34;&#93;&#44; <var>attribs</var>&#91;&#34;<a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;imagesizes&#45;2"></a><code>[imagesizes](#attr-link-imagesizes)</code>&#34;&#93;&#44; and null&#46;

    9.  Run the <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;process&#45;a&#45;link&#45;header&#45;2"></a>[process a link header](#process-a-link-header) steps for <var>rel</var> given <var>options</var>&#46;

To <a id="apply&#45;link&#45;options&#45;from&#45;parsed&#45;header&#45;attributes"></a><strong>apply link options from parsed header attributes</strong> to a <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;processing&#45;options&#45;5"></a>[link processing options](#link-processing-options) <var>options</var> given <var>attribs</var> and a string <var>rel</var>&#58;

1.  If <var>rel</var> is &#34;<a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;type&#45;preload"></a><code>[preload](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-preload)</code>&#34;&#58;

    1.  If <var>attribs</var>&#91;&#34;<a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;as"></a><code>[as](#attr-link-as)</code>&#34;&#93; does not <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;map&#45;exists&#45;6"></a>[exist](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists)&#44; then return false&#46;

    2.  Let <var>destination</var> be the result of <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;translate&#45;a&#45;preload&#45;destination"></a>[translating](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#translate-a-preload-destination) <var>attribs</var>&#91;&#34;<a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;as&#45;2"></a><code>[as](#attr-link-as)</code>&#34;&#93;&#46;

    3.  If <var>destination</var> is null&#44; then return false&#46;

    4.  Set <var>options</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;destination"></a>[destination](#link-options-destination) to <var>destination</var>&#46;

2.  If <var>attribs</var>&#91;&#34;<a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;crossorigin&#45;2"></a><code>[crossorigin](#attr-link-crossorigin)</code>&#34;&#93; <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;map&#45;exists&#45;7"></a>[exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists) and is an <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;ascii&#45;case&#45;insensitive"></a>[ASCII case&#45;insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-case-insensitive) match for one of the <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;cors&#45;settings&#45;attribute&#45;2"></a>[CORS settings attribute](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#cors-settings-attribute) <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;enumerated&#45;attribute"></a>[keywords](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#enumerated-attribute)&#44; then set <var>options</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;crossorigin&#45;2"></a>[crossorigin](#link-options-crossorigin) to the <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;cors&#45;settings&#45;attribute&#45;3"></a>[CORS settings attribute](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#cors-settings-attribute) state corresponding to that keyword&#46;

3.  If <var>attribs</var>&#91;&#34;<a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;integrity&#45;3"></a><code>[integrity](#attr-link-integrity)</code>&#34;&#93; <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;map&#45;exists&#45;8"></a>[exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists)&#44; then set <var>options</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;integrity&#45;2"></a>[integrity](#link-options-integrity) to <var>attribs</var>&#91;&#34;<a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;integrity&#45;4"></a><code>[integrity](#attr-link-integrity)</code>&#34;&#93;&#46;

4.  If <var>attribs</var>&#91;&#34;<a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;referrerpolicy&#45;2"></a><code>[referrerpolicy](#attr-link-referrerpolicy)</code>&#34;&#93; <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;map&#45;exists&#45;9"></a>[exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists) and is an <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;ascii&#45;case&#45;insensitive&#45;2"></a>[ASCII case&#45;insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-case-insensitive) match for some <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;referrer&#45;policy&#45;2"></a>[referrer policy](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-referrer-policy/#referrer-policy)&#44; then set <var>options</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;referrer&#45;policy&#45;2"></a>[referrer policy](#link-options-referrer-policy) to that <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;referrer&#45;policy&#45;3"></a>[referrer policy](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-referrer-policy/#referrer-policy)&#46;

5.  If <var>attribs</var>&#91;&#34;<a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;nonce"></a><code>[nonce](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#attr-nonce)</code>&#34;&#93; <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;map&#45;exists&#45;10"></a>[exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists)&#44; then set <var>options</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;nonce&#45;2"></a>[nonce](#link-options-nonce) to <var>attribs</var>&#91;&#34;<a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;nonce&#45;2"></a><code>[nonce](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#attr-nonce)</code>&#34;&#93;&#46;

6.  If <var>attribs</var>&#91;&#34;<a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;type&#45;3"></a><code>[type](#attr-link-type)</code>&#34;&#93; <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;map&#45;exists&#45;11"></a>[exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists)&#44; then set <var>options</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;type&#45;2"></a>[type](#link-options-type) to <var>attribs</var>&#91;&#34;<a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;type&#45;4"></a><code>[type](#attr-link-type)</code>&#34;&#93;&#46;

7.  If <var>attribs</var>&#91;&#34;<a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;fetchpriority&#45;2"></a><code>[fetchpriority](#attr-link-fetchpriority)</code>&#34;&#93; <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;map&#45;exists&#45;12"></a>[exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#map-exists) and is an <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;ascii&#45;case&#45;insensitive&#45;3"></a>[ASCII case&#45;insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-case-insensitive) match for a <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;fetch&#45;priority&#45;attribute&#45;2"></a>[fetch priority attribute](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#fetch-priority-attribute) keyword&#44; then set <var>options</var>&#39;s <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;link&#45;options&#45;fetch&#45;priority&#45;2"></a>[fetch priority](#link-options-fetch-priority) to that <a id="processing&#45;link&#45;headers&#58;fetch&#45;priority&#45;attribute&#45;3"></a>[fetch priority attribute](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#fetch-priority-attribute) keyword&#46;

8.  Return true&#46;

##### <a id="early&#45;hints"></a>4&#46;2&#46;4&#46;5 Early hints[](#early-hints)

MDN

[Status&#47;103](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTTP/Status/103)

Firefoxpreview&#43;SafariNoChrome103&#43;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

OperaNoEdge103&#43;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

Edge &#40;Legacy&#41;&#63;Internet ExplorerNo

------------------------------------------------------------------------

Firefox Android&#63;Safari iOS&#63;Chrome Android&#63;WebView Android&#63;Samsung Internet&#63;Opera Android&#63;

<a id="early&#45;hints&#45;2"></a><strong>Early hints</strong> allow user&#45;agents to perform some operations&#44; such as to speculatively load resources that are likely to be used by the document&#44; before the navigation request is fully handled by the server and a response code is served&#46; Servers can indicate early hints by serving a <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;concept&#45;response"></a>[response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response) with a 103 status code before serving the final <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;concept&#45;response&#45;2"></a>[response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response)&#46;[&#91;RFC8297&#93;](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#refsRFC8297)

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

For compatibility reasons [early hints are typically delivered over HTTP&#47;2 or above](https://httpwg.org/specs/rfc8297.html#security-considerations)&#44; but for readability we use HTTP&#47;1&#46;1&#45;style notation below&#46;

<strong>Example&#58;</strong>

For example&#44; given the following sequence of responses&#58;

<a id="early&#45;hints&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel"></a><a id="early&#45;hints&#58;link&#45;type&#45;preload"></a><a id="early&#45;hints&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;as"></a>

``` text
103 Early Hint
Link: </image.png>; rel=preload; as=image
```

``` text
200 OK
Content-Type: text/html

<!DOCTYPE html>
...
<img src="/image.png">
```

the image will start loading before the HTML content arrives&#46;

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

Only the first early hint response served during the navigation is handled&#44; and it is discarded if it is succeeded by a cross&#45;origin redirect&#46;

In addition to the &#96;<a id="early&#45;hints&#58;http&#45;link"></a><code>[Link](https://httpwg.org/specs/rfc8288.html#header)</code>&#96; headers&#44; it is possible that the 103 response contains a <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;content&#45;security&#45;policy"></a>[Content Security Policy](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-csp/#content-security-policy-object) header&#44; which is enforced when processing the early hint&#46;

<strong>Example&#58;</strong>

For example&#44; given the following sequence of responses&#58;

<a id="early&#45;hints&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel&#45;2"></a><a id="early&#45;hints&#58;link&#45;type&#45;preload&#45;2"></a><a id="early&#45;hints&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;as&#45;2"></a>

``` text
103 Early Hint
Content-Security-Policy: style-src: self;
Link: </style.css>; rel=preload; as=style
```

<a id="early&#45;hints&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel&#45;3"></a><a id="early&#45;hints&#58;link&#45;type&#45;preload&#45;3"></a><a id="early&#45;hints&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;as&#45;3"></a>

``` text
103 Early Hint
Link: </image.png>; rel=preload; as=image
```

``` text
302 Redirect
Location: /alternate.html
```

<a id="early&#45;hints&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel&#45;4"></a><a id="early&#45;hints&#58;link&#45;type&#45;preload&#45;4"></a><a id="early&#45;hints&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;as&#45;4"></a>

``` text
200 OK
Content-Security-Policy: style-src: none;
Link: </font.ttf>; rel=preload; as=font
```

The font and style would be loaded&#44; and the image will be discarded&#44; as only the first early hint response in the final redirect chain is respected&#46; The late <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;content&#45;security&#45;policy&#45;2"></a>[Content Security Policy](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-csp/#content-security-policy-object) header comes after the request to fetch the style has already been performed&#44; but the style will not be accessible to the document&#46;

To <a id="process&#45;early&#45;hint&#45;headers"></a><strong>process early hint headers</strong> given a <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;concept&#45;response&#45;3"></a>[response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response) <var>response</var> and an <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;environment"></a>[environment](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#environment) <var>reservedEnvironment</var>&#58;

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

Early&#45;hint &#96;<a id="early&#45;hints&#58;http&#45;link&#45;2"></a><code>[Link](https://httpwg.org/specs/rfc8288.html#header)</code>&#96; headers are always processed before &#96;<a id="early&#45;hints&#58;http&#45;link&#45;3"></a><code>[Link](https://httpwg.org/specs/rfc8288.html#header)</code>&#96; headers from the final <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;concept&#45;response&#45;4"></a>[response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response)&#44; followed by <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> elements&#46; This is equivalent to prepending the contents of the early and final &#96;<a id="early&#45;hints&#58;http&#45;link&#45;4"></a><code>[Link](https://httpwg.org/specs/rfc8288.html#header)</code>&#96; headers to the <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;document"></a><code>[Document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document)</code>&#39;s <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;the&#45;head&#45;element"></a><code>[head](#the-head-element)</code> element&#44; in respective order&#46;

1.  Let <var>earlyPolicyContainer</var> be the result of <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;creating&#45;a&#45;policy&#45;container&#45;from&#45;a&#45;fetch&#45;response"></a>[creating a policy container from a fetch response](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#creating-a-policy-container-from-a-fetch-response) given <var>response</var> and <var>reservedEnvironment</var>&#46;

    <strong>Note&#58;</strong>

    This allows the early hint <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;concept&#45;response&#45;5"></a>[response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response) to include a <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;content&#45;security&#45;policy&#45;3"></a>[Content Security Policy](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-csp/#content-security-policy-object) which would be <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;enforce&#45;the&#45;policy"></a>[enforced](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-csp/#enforced) when fetching the early hint <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;concept&#45;request"></a>[request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request)&#46;

2.  Let <var>links</var> be the result of <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;extract&#45;links&#45;from&#45;headers"></a>[extracting links](#extract-links-from-headers) from <var>response</var>&#39;s <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;concept&#45;response&#45;header&#45;list"></a>[header list](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-header-list)&#46;

3.  Let <var>earlyHints</var> be an empty <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;list"></a>[list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list)&#46;

4.  <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;list&#45;iterate"></a>[For each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) <var>linkObject</var> in <var>links</var>&#58;

    <strong>Note&#58;</strong>

    The moment we receive the early hint link header&#44; we begin <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;concept&#45;fetch"></a>[fetching](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch) <var>earlyRequest</var>&#46; If it comes back before the <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;document&#45;2"></a><code>[Document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document)</code> is created&#44; we set <var>earlyResponse</var> to the <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;concept&#45;response&#45;6"></a>[response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response) of that <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;concept&#45;fetch&#45;2"></a>[fetch](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-fetch) and once the <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;document&#45;3"></a><code>[Document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document)</code> is created we commit it &#40;by making it available in the <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;map&#45;of&#45;preloaded&#45;resources"></a>[map of preloaded resources](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#map-of-preloaded-resources) as if it was a <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;2"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> element&#41;&#46; If the <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;document&#45;4"></a><code>[Document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document)</code> is created first&#44; the <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;concept&#45;response&#45;7"></a>[response](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response) is committed as soon as it becomes available&#46;

    1.  Let <var>rel</var> be <var>linkObject</var>&#91;&#34;<code>relation&#95;type</code>&#34;&#93;&#46;

    2.  Let <var>options</var> be a new <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;link&#45;processing&#45;options"></a>[link processing options](#link-processing-options) with

        <strong><a id="early&#45;hints&#58;link&#45;options&#45;href"></a>[href](#link-options-href)</strong>

        <var>linkObject</var>&#91;&#34;<code>target&#95;uri</code>&#34;&#93;
        <strong><a id="early&#45;hints&#58;link&#45;options&#45;initiator"></a>[initiator](#link-options-initiator)</strong>

        &#34;<code>early&#45;hint</code>&#34;
        <strong><a id="early&#45;hints&#58;link&#45;options&#45;base&#45;url"></a>[base URL](#link-options-base-url)</strong>

        <var>response</var>&#39;s <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;concept&#45;response&#45;url"></a>[URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-url)
        <strong><a id="early&#45;hints&#58;link&#45;options&#45;origin"></a>[origin](#link-options-origin)</strong>

        <var>response</var>&#39;s <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;concept&#45;response&#45;url&#45;2"></a>[URL](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-response-url)&#39;s <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;concept&#45;url&#45;origin"></a>[origin](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-origin)
        <strong><a id="early&#45;hints&#58;link&#45;options&#45;environment"></a>[environment](#link-options-environment)</strong>

        <var>reservedEnvironment</var>
        <strong><a id="early&#45;hints&#58;link&#45;options&#45;policy&#45;container"></a>[policy container](#link-options-policy-container)</strong>

        <var>earlyPolicyContainer</var>

    3.  Let <var>attribs</var> be <var>linkObject</var>&#91;&#34;<code>target&#95;attributes</code>&#34;&#93;&#46;

        <strong>Note&#58;</strong>

        Only the <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;as&#45;5"></a><code>[as](#attr-link-as)</code>&#44; <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;crossorigin"></a><code>[crossorigin](#attr-link-crossorigin)</code>&#44; <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;integrity"></a><code>[integrity](#attr-link-integrity)</code>&#44; and <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;type"></a><code>[type](#attr-link-type)</code> attributes are handled as part of early hint processing&#46; The other ones&#44; in particular <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;blocking"></a><code>[blocking](#attr-link-blocking)</code>&#44; <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;imagesrcset"></a><code>[imagesrcset](#attr-link-imagesrcset)</code>&#44; <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;imagesizes"></a><code>[imagesizes](#attr-link-imagesizes)</code>&#44; and <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;media"></a><code>[media](#attr-link-media)</code> are only applicable once a <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;document&#45;5"></a><code>[Document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document)</code> is created&#46;

    4.  <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;apply&#45;link&#45;options&#45;from&#45;parsed&#45;header&#45;attributes"></a>[Apply link options from parsed header attributes](#apply-link-options-from-parsed-header-attributes) to <var>options</var> given <var>attribs</var> and <var>rel</var>&#46; If that returned false&#44; then return&#46;

    5.  Run the <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;process&#45;a&#45;link&#45;header"></a>[process a link header](#process-a-link-header) steps for <var>rel</var> given <var>options</var>&#46;

    6.  <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;list&#45;append"></a>[Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-append) <var>options</var> to <var>earlyHints</var>&#46;

5.  Return the following substeps given <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;document&#45;6"></a><code>[Document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document)</code> <var>doc</var>&#58; <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;list&#45;iterate&#45;2"></a>[for each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-iterate) <var>options</var> in <var>earlyHints</var>&#58;

    1.  If <var>options</var>&#39;s <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;link&#45;options&#45;on&#45;document&#45;ready"></a>[on document ready](#link-options-on-document-ready) is null&#44; then set <var>options</var>&#39;s <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;link&#45;options&#45;document"></a>[document](#link-options-document) to <var>doc</var>&#46;

    2.  Otherwise&#44; call <var>options</var>&#39;s <a id="early&#45;hints&#58;link&#45;options&#45;on&#45;document&#45;ready&#45;2"></a>[on document ready](#link-options-on-document-ready) with <var>doc</var>&#46;

##### <a id="providing&#45;users&#45;with&#45;a&#45;means&#45;to&#45;follow&#45;hyperlinks&#45;created&#45;using&#45;the&#45;link&#45;element"></a>4&#46;2&#46;4&#46;6 Providing users with a means to follow hyperlinks created using the <a id="providing&#45;users&#45;with&#45;a&#45;means&#45;to&#45;follow&#45;hyperlinks&#45;created&#45;using&#45;the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> element[](#providing-users-with-a-means-to-follow-hyperlinks-created-using-the-link-element)

<a id="linkui"></a>Interactive user agents may provide users with a means to <a id="providing&#45;users&#45;with&#45;a&#45;means&#45;to&#45;follow&#45;hyperlinks&#45;created&#45;using&#45;the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;following&#45;hyperlinks&#45;2"></a>[follow the hyperlinks](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#following-hyperlinks-2) created using the <a id="providing&#45;users&#45;with&#45;a&#45;means&#45;to&#45;follow&#45;hyperlinks&#45;created&#45;using&#45;the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;2"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> element&#44; somewhere within their user interface&#46; Such invocations of the <a id="providing&#45;users&#45;with&#45;a&#45;means&#45;to&#45;follow&#45;hyperlinks&#45;created&#45;using&#45;the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;following&#45;hyperlinks&#45;2&#45;2"></a>[follow the hyperlink](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#following-hyperlinks-2) algorithm must set the <a id="providing&#45;users&#45;with&#45;a&#45;means&#45;to&#45;follow&#45;hyperlinks&#45;created&#45;using&#45;the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;following&#45;userinvolvement"></a><em>[userInvolvement](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#following-userinvolvement)</em> argument to &#34;<a id="providing&#45;users&#45;with&#45;a&#45;means&#45;to&#45;follow&#45;hyperlinks&#45;created&#45;using&#45;the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;uni&#45;browser&#45;ui"></a><code>[browser UI](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#uni-browser-ui)</code>&#34;&#46; The exact interface is not defined by this specification&#44; but it could include the following information &#40;obtained from the element&#39;s attributes&#44; again as defined below&#41;&#44; in some form or another &#40;possibly simplified&#41;&#44; for each <a id="providing&#45;users&#45;with&#45;a&#45;means&#45;to&#45;follow&#45;hyperlinks&#45;created&#45;using&#45;the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;hyperlink"></a>[hyperlink](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#hyperlink) created with each <a id="providing&#45;users&#45;with&#45;a&#45;means&#45;to&#45;follow&#45;hyperlinks&#45;created&#45;using&#45;the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element&#45;3"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> element in the document&#58;

- The relationship between this document and the resource &#40;given by the <a id="providing&#45;users&#45;with&#45;a&#45;means&#45;to&#45;follow&#45;hyperlinks&#45;created&#45;using&#45;the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;rel"></a><code>[rel](#attr-link-rel)</code> attribute&#41;
- The title of the resource &#40;given by the <a id="providing&#45;users&#45;with&#45;a&#45;means&#45;to&#45;follow&#45;hyperlinks&#45;created&#45;using&#45;the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;title"></a><code>[title](#attr-link-title)</code> attribute&#41;&#46;
- The address of the resource &#40;given by the <a id="providing&#45;users&#45;with&#45;a&#45;means&#45;to&#45;follow&#45;hyperlinks&#45;created&#45;using&#45;the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;href"></a><code>[href](#attr-link-href)</code> attribute&#41;&#46;
- The language of the resource &#40;given by the <a id="providing&#45;users&#45;with&#45;a&#45;means&#45;to&#45;follow&#45;hyperlinks&#45;created&#45;using&#45;the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;hreflang"></a><code>[hreflang](#attr-link-hreflang)</code> attribute&#41;&#46;
- The optimum media for the resource &#40;given by the <a id="providing&#45;users&#45;with&#45;a&#45;means&#45;to&#45;follow&#45;hyperlinks&#45;created&#45;using&#45;the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;media"></a><code>[media](#attr-link-media)</code> attribute&#41;&#46;

User agents could also include other information&#44; such as the type of the resource &#40;as given by the <a id="providing&#45;users&#45;with&#45;a&#45;means&#45;to&#45;follow&#45;hyperlinks&#45;created&#45;using&#45;the&#45;link&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;type"></a><code>[type](#attr-link-type)</code> attribute&#41;&#46;

#### <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element"></a>4&#46;2&#46;5 The <a id="meta"></a><strong><code>meta</code></strong> element[](#the-meta-element)

<strong><a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;categories"></a>[Categories](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-categories)&#58;</strong>

<a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;metadata&#45;content&#45;2"></a>[Metadata content](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#metadata-content-2)&#46;

If the <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;names&#58;&#45;the&#45;itemprop&#45;attribute"></a><code>[itemprop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#names:-the-itemprop-attribute)</code> attribute is present&#58; <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;flow&#45;content&#45;2"></a>[flow content](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#flow-content-2)&#46;

If the <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;names&#58;&#45;the&#45;itemprop&#45;attribute&#45;2"></a><code>[itemprop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#names:-the-itemprop-attribute)</code> attribute is present&#58; <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;phrasing&#45;content&#45;2"></a>[phrasing content](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#phrasing-content-2)&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;contexts"></a>[Contexts in which this element can be used](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-contexts)&#58;</strong>

If the <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;charset"></a><code>[charset](#attr-meta-charset)</code> attribute is present&#44; or if the element&#39;s <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv"></a><code>[http&#45;equiv](#attr-meta-http-equiv)</code> attribute is in the <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;content&#45;type"></a>[Encoding declaration state](#attr-meta-http-equiv-content-type)&#58; in a <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;the&#45;head&#45;element"></a><code>[head](#the-head-element)</code> element&#46;
