If the <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;2"></a><code>[http&#45;equiv](#attr-meta-http-equiv)</code> attribute is present but not in the <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;content&#45;type&#45;2"></a>[Encoding declaration state](#attr-meta-http-equiv-content-type)&#58; in a <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;the&#45;head&#45;element&#45;2"></a><code>[head](#the-head-element)</code> element&#46;

If the <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;3"></a><code>[http&#45;equiv](#attr-meta-http-equiv)</code> attribute is present but not in the <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;content&#45;type&#45;3"></a>[Encoding declaration state](#attr-meta-http-equiv-content-type)&#58; in a <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;the&#45;noscript&#45;element"></a><code>[noscript](html-language-elements-interactive-scripting--877d6146487f--05bb1d85cc89.md#the-noscript-element)</code> element that is a child of a <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;the&#45;head&#45;element&#45;3"></a><code>[head](#the-head-element)</code> element&#46;

If the <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;name"></a><code>[name](#attr-meta-name)</code> attribute is present&#58; where <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;metadata&#45;content&#45;2&#45;2"></a>[metadata content](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#metadata-content-2) is expected&#46;

If the <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;names&#58;&#45;the&#45;itemprop&#45;attribute&#45;3"></a><code>[itemprop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#names:-the-itemprop-attribute)</code> attribute is present&#58; where <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;metadata&#45;content&#45;2&#45;3"></a>[metadata content](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#metadata-content-2) is expected&#46;

If the <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;names&#58;&#45;the&#45;itemprop&#45;attribute&#45;4"></a><code>[itemprop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#names:-the-itemprop-attribute)</code> attribute is present&#58; where <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;phrasing&#45;content&#45;2&#45;2"></a>[phrasing content](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#phrasing-content-2) is expected&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;content&#45;model"></a>[Content model](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-content-model)&#58;</strong>

<a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;content&#45;nothing"></a>[Nothing](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-content-nothing)&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;tag&#45;omission"></a>[Tag omission in text&#47;html](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-tag-omission)&#58;</strong>

No <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;syntax&#45;end&#45;tag"></a>[end tag](html-language-syntax-parser--877d6146487f--37a9ba51eed8.md#syntax-end-tag)&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;attributes"></a>[Content attributes](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-attributes)&#58;</strong>

<a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;global&#45;attributes"></a>[Global attributes](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#global-attributes)

<a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;name&#45;2"></a><code>[name](#attr-meta-name)</code> — Metadata name

<a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;4"></a><code>[http&#45;equiv](#attr-meta-http-equiv)</code> — Pragma directive

<a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;content"></a><code>[content](#attr-meta-content)</code> — Value of the element

<a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;charset&#45;2"></a><code>[charset](#attr-meta-charset)</code> — <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;character&#45;encoding&#45;declaration"></a>[Character encoding declaration](#character-encoding-declaration)

<a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;media"></a><code>[media](#attr-meta-media)</code> — Applicable media

<strong><a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;accessibility&#45;considerations"></a>[Accessibility considerations](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-accessibility-considerations)&#58;</strong>

[For authors](https://w3c.github.io/html-aria/#el-meta)&#46;

[For implementers](https://w3c.github.io/html-aam/#el-meta)&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;sanitization"></a>[Sanitization](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-sanitization)&#58;</strong>

<a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;sanitizer&#45;uncategorized"></a>[Uncategorized](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#sanitizer-uncategorized)&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;dom"></a>[DOM interface](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-dom)&#58;</strong>

<a id="htmlmetaelement"></a><a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;htmlelement"></a><a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;htmlconstructor"></a><a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;cereactions"></a><a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;xattr&#45;reflect"></a><a id="dom&#45;meta&#45;name"></a><a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;cereactions&#45;2"></a><a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;xattr&#45;reflect&#45;2"></a><a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;5"></a><a id="dom&#45;meta&#45;httpequiv"></a><a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;cereactions&#45;3"></a><a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;xattr&#45;reflect&#45;3"></a><a id="dom&#45;meta&#45;content"></a><a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;cereactions&#45;4"></a><a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;xattr&#45;reflect&#45;4"></a><a id="dom&#45;meta&#45;media"></a>

``` text
[Exposed=Window]
interface HTMLMetaElement : HTMLElement {
  [HTMLConstructor] constructor();

  [CEReactions, Reflect] attribute DOMString name;
  [CEReactions, Reflect="http-equiv"] attribute DOMString httpEquiv;
  [CEReactions, Reflect] attribute DOMString content;
  [CEReactions, Reflect] attribute DOMString media;

  // also has obsolete members
};
```

The <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;represents"></a>[represents](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#represents) various kinds of metadata that cannot be expressed using the <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;the&#45;title&#45;element"></a><code>[title](#the-title-element)</code>&#44; <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;the&#45;base&#45;element"></a><code>[base](#the-base-element)</code>&#44; <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code>&#44; <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;the&#45;style&#45;element"></a><code>[style](#the-style-element)</code>&#44; and <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;the&#45;script&#45;element"></a><code>[script](html-language-elements-interactive-scripting--877d6146487f--05bb1d85cc89.md#the-script-element)</code> elements&#46;

The <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;2"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element can represent document&#45;level metadata with the <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;name&#45;3"></a><code>[name](#attr-meta-name)</code> attribute&#44; pragma directives with the <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;6"></a><code>[http&#45;equiv](#attr-meta-http-equiv)</code> attribute&#44; and the file&#39;s <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;character&#45;encoding&#45;declaration&#45;2"></a>[character encoding declaration](#character-encoding-declaration) when an HTML document is serialized to string form &#40;e&#46;g&#46; for transmission over the network or for disk storage&#41; with the <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;charset&#45;3"></a><code>[charset](#attr-meta-charset)</code> attribute&#46;

Exactly one of the <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;name&#45;4"></a><code>[name](#attr-meta-name)</code>&#44; <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;7"></a><code>[http&#45;equiv](#attr-meta-http-equiv)</code>&#44; <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;charset&#45;4"></a><code>[charset](#attr-meta-charset)</code>&#44; and <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;names&#58;&#45;the&#45;itemprop&#45;attribute&#45;5"></a><code>[itemprop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#names:-the-itemprop-attribute)</code> attributes must be specified&#46;

If either <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;name&#45;5"></a><code>[name](#attr-meta-name)</code>&#44; <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;8"></a><code>[http&#45;equiv](#attr-meta-http-equiv)</code>&#44; or <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;names&#58;&#45;the&#45;itemprop&#45;attribute&#45;6"></a><code>[itemprop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#names:-the-itemprop-attribute)</code> is specified&#44; then the <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;content&#45;2"></a><code>[content](#attr-meta-content)</code> attribute must also be specified&#46; Otherwise&#44; it must be omitted&#46;

The <a id="attr&#45;meta&#45;charset"></a><strong><code>charset</code></strong> attribute specifies the <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;encoding"></a>[character encoding](https://encoding.spec.whatwg.org/#encoding) used by the document&#46; This is a <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;character&#45;encoding&#45;declaration&#45;3"></a>[character encoding declaration](#character-encoding-declaration)&#46; If the attribute is present&#44; its value must be an <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;ascii&#45;case&#45;insensitive"></a>[ASCII case&#45;insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-case-insensitive) match for the string &#34;<code>utf&#45;8</code>&#34;&#46;

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

The <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;charset&#45;5"></a><code>[charset](#attr-meta-charset)</code> attribute on the <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;3"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element has no effect in XML documents&#44; but is allowed in XML documents in order to facilitate migration to and from XML&#46;

There must not be more than one <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;4"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element with a <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;charset&#45;6"></a><code>[charset](#attr-meta-charset)</code> attribute per document&#46;

The <a id="attr&#45;meta&#45;content"></a><strong><code>content</code></strong> attribute gives the value of the document metadata or pragma directive when the element is used for those purposes&#46; The allowed values depend on the exact context&#44; as described in subsequent sections of this specification&#46;

If a <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;5"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element has a <a id="attr&#45;meta&#45;name"></a><strong><code>name</code></strong> attribute&#44; it sets document metadata&#46; Document metadata is expressed in terms of name&#45;value pairs&#44; the <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;name&#45;6"></a><code>[name](#attr-meta-name)</code> attribute on the <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;6"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element giving the name&#44; and the <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;content&#45;3"></a><code>[content](#attr-meta-content)</code> attribute on the same element giving the value&#46; The name specifies what aspect of metadata is being set&#59; valid names and the meaning of their values are described in the following sections&#46; If a <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;7"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element has no <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;content&#45;4"></a><code>[content](#attr-meta-content)</code> attribute&#44; then the value part of the metadata name&#45;value pair is the empty string&#46;

The <a id="attr&#45;meta&#45;media"></a><strong><code>media</code></strong> attribute says which media the metadata applies to&#46; The value must be a <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;valid&#45;media&#45;query&#45;list"></a>[valid media query list](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#valid-media-query-list)&#46; Unless the <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;name&#45;7"></a><code>[name](#attr-meta-name)</code> is <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;meta&#45;theme&#45;color"></a><code>[theme&#45;color](#meta-theme-color)</code>&#44; the <a id="the&#45;meta&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;media&#45;2"></a><code>[media](#attr-meta-media)</code> attribute has no effect on the processing model and must not be used by authors&#46;

##### <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names"></a>4&#46;2&#46;5&#46;1 Standard metadata names[](#standard-metadata-names)

<strong>✔</strong>MDN

[Element&#47;meta&#47;name](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTML/Element/meta/name)

Support in all current engines&#46;

Firefox1&#43;Safari4&#43;Chrome1&#43;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

Opera12&#46;1&#43;Edge79&#43;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

Edge &#40;Legacy&#41;12&#43;Internet Explorer6&#43;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

Firefox Android&#63;Safari iOS&#63;Chrome Android&#63;WebView Android&#63;Samsung Internet&#63;Opera Android12&#46;1&#43;

This specification defines a few names for the <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;name"></a><code>[name](#attr-meta-name)</code> attribute of the <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element&#46;

Names are case&#45;insensitive&#44; and must be compared in an <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;ascii&#45;case&#45;insensitive"></a>[ASCII case&#45;insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-case-insensitive) manner&#46;

<strong><a id="meta&#45;application&#45;name"></a><strong><code>application&#45;name</code></strong></strong>

The value must be a short free&#45;form string giving the name of the web application that the page represents&#46; If the page is not a web application&#44; the <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;meta&#45;application&#45;name"></a><code>[application&#45;name](#meta-application-name)</code> metadata name must not be used&#46; Translations of the web application&#39;s name may be given&#44; using the <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;lang"></a><code>[lang](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#attr-lang)</code> attribute to specify the language of each name&#46;

There must not be more than one <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;2"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element with a given <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;language"></a>[language](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#language) and where the <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;name&#45;2"></a><code>[name](#attr-meta-name)</code> attribute value is an <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;ascii&#45;case&#45;insensitive&#45;2"></a>[ASCII case&#45;insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-case-insensitive) match for <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;meta&#45;application&#45;name&#45;2"></a><code>[application&#45;name](#meta-application-name)</code> per document&#46;

User agents may use the application name in UI in preference to the page&#39;s <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;the&#45;title&#45;element"></a><code>[title](#the-title-element)</code>&#44; since the title might include status messages and the like relevant to the status of the page at a particular moment in time instead of just being the name of the application&#46;

To find the application name to use given an ordered list of languages &#40;e&#46;g&#46; British English&#44; American English&#44; and English&#41;&#44; user agents must run the following steps&#58;

1.  Let <var>languages</var> be the list of languages&#46;

2.  Let <var>default language</var> be the <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;language&#45;2"></a>[language](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#language) of the <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;document"></a><code>[Document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document)</code>&#39;s <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;document&#45;element"></a>[document element](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#document-element)&#44; if any&#44; and if that language is not unknown&#46;

3.  If there is a <var>default language</var>&#44; and if it is not the same language as any of the languages in <var>languages</var>&#44; append it to <var>languages</var>&#46;

4.  Let <var>winning language</var> be the first language in <var>languages</var> for which there is a <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;3"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element in the <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;document&#45;2"></a><code>[Document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document)</code> where the <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;name&#45;3"></a><code>[name](#attr-meta-name)</code> attribute value is an <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;ascii&#45;case&#45;insensitive&#45;3"></a>[ASCII case&#45;insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-case-insensitive) match for <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;meta&#45;application&#45;name&#45;3"></a><code>[application&#45;name](#meta-application-name)</code> and whose <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;language&#45;3"></a>[language](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#language) is the language in question&#46;

    If none of the languages have such a <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;4"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element&#44; then return&#59; there&#39;s no given application name&#46;

5.  Return the value of the <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;content"></a><code>[content](#attr-meta-content)</code> attribute of the first <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;5"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element in the <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;document&#45;3"></a><code>[Document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document)</code> in <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;tree&#45;order"></a>[tree order](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-tree-order) where the <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;name&#45;4"></a><code>[name](#attr-meta-name)</code> attribute value is an <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;ascii&#45;case&#45;insensitive&#45;4"></a>[ASCII case&#45;insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-case-insensitive) match for <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;meta&#45;application&#45;name&#45;4"></a><code>[application&#45;name](#meta-application-name)</code> and whose <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;language&#45;4"></a>[language](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#language) is <var>winning language</var>&#46;

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

This algorithm would be used by a browser when it needs a name for the page&#44; for instance&#44; to label a bookmark&#46; The languages it would provide to the algorithm would be the user&#39;s preferred languages&#46;

<strong><a id="meta&#45;author"></a><strong><code>author</code></strong></strong>

The value must be a free&#45;form string giving the name of one of the page&#39;s authors&#46;

<strong><a id="meta&#45;description"></a><strong><code>description</code></strong></strong>

The value must be a free&#45;form string that describes the page&#46; The value must be appropriate for use in a directory of pages&#44; e&#46;g&#46; in a search engine&#46; There must not be more than one <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;6"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element where the <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;name&#45;5"></a><code>[name](#attr-meta-name)</code> attribute value is an <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;ascii&#45;case&#45;insensitive&#45;5"></a>[ASCII case&#45;insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-case-insensitive) match for <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;meta&#45;description"></a><code>[description](#meta-description)</code> per document&#46;

<strong><a id="meta&#45;generator"></a><strong><code>generator</code></strong></strong>

The value must be a free&#45;form string that identifies one of the software packages used to generate the document&#46; This value must not be used on pages whose markup is not generated by software&#44; e&#46;g&#46; pages whose markup was written by a user in a text editor&#46;

<strong>Example&#58;</strong>

Here is what a tool called &#34;Frontweaver&#34; could include in its output&#44; in the page&#39;s <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;the&#45;head&#45;element"></a><code>[head](#the-head-element)</code> element&#44; to identify itself as the tool used to generate the page&#58;

``` text
<meta name=generator content="Frontweaver 8.2">
```

<strong><a id="meta&#45;keywords"></a><strong><code>keywords</code></strong></strong>

The value must be a <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;set&#45;of&#45;comma&#45;separated&#45;tokens"></a>[set of comma&#45;separated tokens](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#set-of-comma-separated-tokens)&#44; each of which is a keyword relevant to the page&#46;

<strong>Example&#58;</strong>

This page about typefaces on British motorways uses a <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;7"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element to specify some keywords that users might use to look for the page&#58;

``` text
<!DOCTYPE HTML>
<html lang="en-GB">
 <head>
  <title>Typefaces on UK motorways</title>
  <meta name="keywords" content="british,type face,font,fonts,highway,highways">
 </head>
 <body>
  ...
```

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

Many search engines do not consider such keywords&#44; because this feature has historically been used unreliably and even misleadingly as a way to spam search engine results in a way that is not helpful for users&#46;

To obtain the list of keywords that the author has specified as applicable to the page&#44; the user agent must run the following steps&#58;

1.  Let <var>keywords</var> be an empty list&#46;

2.  For each <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;8"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element with a <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;name&#45;6"></a><code>[name](#attr-meta-name)</code> attribute and a <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;content&#45;2"></a><code>[content](#attr-meta-content)</code> attribute and where the <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;name&#45;7"></a><code>[name](#attr-meta-name)</code> attribute value is an <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;ascii&#45;case&#45;insensitive&#45;6"></a>[ASCII case&#45;insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-case-insensitive) match for <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;meta&#45;keywords"></a><code>[keywords](#meta-keywords)</code>&#58;

    1.  <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;split&#45;a&#45;string&#45;on&#45;commas"></a>[Split the value of the element&#39;s <code>content</code> attribute on commas](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#split-on-commas)&#46;

    2.  Add the resulting tokens&#44; if any&#44; to <var>keywords</var>&#46;

3.  Remove any duplicates from <var>keywords</var>&#46;

4.  Return <var>keywords</var>&#46; This is the list of keywords that the author has specified as applicable to the page&#46;

User agents should not use this information when there is insufficient confidence in the reliability of the value&#46;

<strong>Example&#58;</strong>

For instance&#44; it would be reasonable for a content management system to use the keyword information of pages within the system to populate the index of a site&#45;specific search engine&#44; but a large&#45;scale content aggregator that used this information would likely find that certain users would try to game its ranking mechanism through the use of inappropriate keywords&#46;

<strong><a id="meta&#45;referrer"></a><strong><code>referrer</code></strong></strong>

The value must be a <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;referrer&#45;policy"></a>[referrer policy](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-referrer-policy/#referrer-policy)&#44; which defines the default <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;referrer&#45;policy&#45;2"></a>[referrer policy](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-referrer-policy/#referrer-policy) for the <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;document&#45;4"></a><code>[Document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document)</code>&#46; [&#91;REFERRERPOLICY&#93;](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#refsREFERRERPOLICY)

If any <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;9"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element <var>element</var> is <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;insert&#45;an&#45;element&#45;into&#45;a&#45;document"></a>[inserted into the document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#insert-an-element-into-a-document)&#44; or has its <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;name&#45;8"></a><code>[name](#attr-meta-name)</code> or <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;content&#45;3"></a><code>[content](#attr-meta-content)</code> attributes changed&#44; user agents must run the following algorithm&#58;

1.  If <var>element</var> is not <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;in&#45;a&#45;document&#45;tree"></a>[in a document tree](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#in-a-document-tree)&#44; then return&#46;

2.  If <var>element</var> does not have a <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;name&#45;9"></a><code>[name](#attr-meta-name)</code> attribute whose value is an <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;ascii&#45;case&#45;insensitive&#45;7"></a>[ASCII case&#45;insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-case-insensitive) match for &#34;<a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;meta&#45;referrer"></a><code>[referrer](#meta-referrer)</code>&#34;&#44; then return&#46;

3.  If <var>element</var> does not have a <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;content&#45;4"></a><code>[content](#attr-meta-content)</code> attribute&#44; or that attribute&#39;s value is the empty string&#44; then return&#46;

4.  Let <var>value</var> be the value of <var>element</var>&#39;s <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;content&#45;5"></a><code>[content](#attr-meta-content)</code> attribute&#44; <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;converted&#45;to&#45;ascii&#45;lowercase"></a>[converted to ASCII lowercase](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-lowercase)&#46;

5.  If <var>value</var> is one of the values given in the first column of the following table&#44; then set <var>value</var> to the value given in the second column&#58;

    <table><thead><tr><th>Legacy value          </th><th>Referrer policy        </th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>never</code>          </td><td><code id="standard-metadata-names:referrer-policy-no-referrer"><a data-x-internal="referrer-policy-no-referrer" href="https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-referrer-policy/#referrer-policy-no-referrer">no&#45;referrer</a></code>         </td></tr><tr><td><code>default</code>          </td><td>the <a id="standard-metadata-names:default-referrer-policy" href="https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-referrer-policy/#default-referrer-policy" data-x-internal="default-referrer-policy">default referrer policy</a>         </td></tr><tr><td><code>always</code>          </td><td><code id="standard-metadata-names:referrer-policy-unsafe-url"><a data-x-internal="referrer-policy-unsafe-url" href="https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-referrer-policy/#referrer-policy-unsafe-url">unsafe&#45;url</a></code>         </td></tr><tr><td><code>origin&#45;when&#45;crossorigin</code>          </td><td><code id="standard-metadata-names:referrer-policy-origin-when-cross-origin"><a data-x-internal="referrer-policy-origin-when-cross-origin" href="https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-referrer-policy/#referrer-policy-origin-when-cross-origin">origin&#45;when&#45;cross&#45;origin</a></code>       </td></tr></tbody></table>

6.  If <var>value</var> is a <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;referrer&#45;policy&#45;3"></a>[referrer policy](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-referrer-policy/#referrer-policy)&#44; then set <var>element</var>&#39;s <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;node&#45;document"></a>[node document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-node-document)&#39;s <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;concept&#45;document&#45;policy&#45;container"></a>[policy container](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-document-policy-container)&#39;s <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;policy&#45;container&#45;referrer&#45;policy"></a>[referrer policy](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#policy-container-referrer-policy) to <var>policy</var>&#46;

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

For historical reasons&#44; unlike other standard metadata names&#44; the processing model for <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;meta&#45;referrer&#45;2"></a><code>[referrer](#meta-referrer)</code> is not responsive to element removals&#44; and does not use <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;tree&#45;order&#45;2"></a>[tree order](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-tree-order)&#46; Only the most&#45;recently&#45;inserted or most&#45;recently&#45;modified <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;10"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element in this state has an effect&#46;

<strong><a id="meta&#45;theme&#45;color"></a><strong><code>theme&#45;color</code></strong></strong>

MDN

[Element&#47;meta&#47;name&#47;theme&#45;color](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTML/Element/meta/name/theme-color)

FirefoxNoSafari15&#43;Chrome🔰 73&#43;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

OperaNoEdge🔰 79&#43;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

Edge &#40;Legacy&#41;&#63;Internet ExplorerNo

------------------------------------------------------------------------

Firefox Android&#63;Safari iOS&#63;Chrome Android80&#43;WebView AndroidNoSamsung Internet6&#46;2&#43;Opera AndroidNo

The value must be a string that matches the CSS <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;color"></a>[&#60;color&#62;](https://drafts.csswg.org/css-color/#typedef-color) production&#44; defining a suggested color that user agents should use to customize the display of the page or of the surrounding user interface&#46; For example&#44; a browser might color the page&#39;s title bar with the specified value&#44; or use it as a color highlight in a tab bar or task switcher&#46;

Within an HTML document&#44; the <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;media"></a><code>[media](#attr-meta-media)</code> attribute value must be unique amongst all the <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;11"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> elements with their <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;name&#45;10"></a><code>[name](#attr-meta-name)</code> attribute value set to an <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;ascii&#45;case&#45;insensitive&#45;8"></a>[ASCII case&#45;insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-case-insensitive) match for <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;meta&#45;theme&#45;color"></a><code>[theme&#45;color](#meta-theme-color)</code>&#46;

<strong>Example&#58;</strong>

This standard itself uses &#34;WHATWG green&#34; as its theme color&#58;

``` text
<!DOCTYPE HTML>
<title>HTML Standard</title>
<meta name="theme-color" content="#3c790a">
...
```

The <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;media&#45;2"></a><code>[media](#attr-meta-media)</code> attribute may be used to describe the context in which the provided color should be used&#46;

<strong>Example&#58;</strong>

If we only wanted to use &#34;WHATWG green&#34; as this standard&#39;s theme color in dark mode&#44; we could use the <code>prefers&#45;color&#45;scheme</code> media feature&#58;

``` text
<!DOCTYPE HTML>
<title>HTML Standard</title>
<meta name="theme-color" content="#3c790a" media="(prefers-color-scheme: dark)">
...
```

To obtain a page&#39;s theme color&#44; user agents must run the following steps&#58;

1.  Let <var>candidate elements</var> be the list of all <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;12"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> elements that meet the following criteria&#44; in <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;tree&#45;order&#45;3"></a>[tree order](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-tree-order)&#58;

    - the element is <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;in&#45;a&#45;document&#45;tree&#45;2"></a>[in a document tree](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#in-a-document-tree)&#59;

    - the element has a <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;name&#45;11"></a><code>[name](#attr-meta-name)</code> attribute&#44; whose value is an <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;ascii&#45;case&#45;insensitive&#45;9"></a>[ASCII case&#45;insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-case-insensitive) match for <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;meta&#45;theme&#45;color&#45;2"></a><code>[theme&#45;color](#meta-theme-color)</code>&#59; and

    - the element has a <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;content&#45;6"></a><code>[content](#attr-meta-content)</code> attribute&#46;

2.  For each <var>element</var> in <var>candidate elements</var>&#58;

    1.  If <var>element</var> has a <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;media"></a><code>[media](#attr-link-media)</code> attribute and the value of <var>element</var>&#39;s <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;media&#45;3"></a><code>[media](#attr-meta-media)</code> attribute does not <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;matches&#45;the&#45;environment"></a>[match the environment](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#matches-the-environment)&#44; then <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;continue"></a>[continue](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-continue)&#46;

    2.  Let <var>value</var> be the result of <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;strip&#45;leading&#45;and&#45;trailing&#45;ascii&#45;whitespace"></a>[stripping leading and trailing ASCII whitespace](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#strip-leading-and-trailing-ascii-whitespace) from the value of <var>element</var>&#39;s <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;content&#45;7"></a><code>[content](#attr-meta-content)</code> attribute&#46;

    3.  Let <var>color</var> be the result of <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;parsed&#45;as&#45;a&#45;css&#45;color&#45;value"></a>[parsing](https://drafts.csswg.org/css-color/#parse-a-css-color-value) <var>value</var>&#46;

    4.  If <var>color</var> is not failure&#44; then return <var>color</var>&#46;

3.  Return nothing &#40;the page has no theme color&#41;&#46;

If any <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;13"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> elements are <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;insert&#45;an&#45;element&#45;into&#45;a&#45;document&#45;2"></a>[inserted into the document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#insert-an-element-into-a-document) or <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;remove&#45;an&#45;element&#45;from&#45;a&#45;document"></a>[removed from the document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#remove-an-element-from-a-document)&#44; or existing <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;14"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> elements have their <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;name&#45;12"></a><code>[name](#attr-meta-name)</code>&#44; <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;content&#45;8"></a><code>[content](#attr-meta-content)</code>&#44; or <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;media&#45;2"></a><code>[media](#attr-link-media)</code> attributes changed&#44; or if the environment changes such that any <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;15"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element&#39;s <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;media&#45;3"></a><code>[media](#attr-link-media)</code> attribute&#39;s value may now or may no longer <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;matches&#45;the&#45;environment&#45;2"></a>[match the environment](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#matches-the-environment)&#44; user agents must re&#45;run the above algorithm and apply the result to any affected UI&#46;

When using the theme color in UI&#44; user agents may adjust it in implementation&#45;specific ways to make it more suitable for the UI in question&#46; For example&#44; if a user agent intends to use the theme color as a background and display white text over it&#44; it might use a darker variant of the theme color in that part of the UI&#44; to ensure adequate contrast&#46;

<strong><a id="meta&#45;color&#45;scheme"></a><strong><code>color&#45;scheme</code></strong></strong>

To aid user agents in rendering the page background with the desired color scheme immediately &#40;rather than waiting for all CSS in the page to load&#41;&#44; a <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;&#39;color&#45;scheme&#39;"></a>[&#39;color&#45;scheme&#39;](https://drafts.csswg.org/css-color-adjust/#color-scheme-prop) value can be provided in a <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;16"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element&#46;

The value must be a string that matches the syntax for the CSS <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;&#39;color&#45;scheme&#39;&#45;2"></a>[&#39;color&#45;scheme&#39;](https://drafts.csswg.org/css-color-adjust/#color-scheme-prop) property value&#46; It determines the <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;page&#39;s&#45;supported&#45;color&#45;schemes"></a>[page&#39;s supported color&#45;schemes](https://drafts.csswg.org/css-color-adjust/#pages-supported-color-schemes)&#46;

There must not be more than one <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;17"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element with its <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;name&#45;13"></a><code>[name](#attr-meta-name)</code> attribute value set to an <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;ascii&#45;case&#45;insensitive&#45;10"></a>[ASCII case&#45;insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-case-insensitive) match for <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;meta&#45;color&#45;scheme"></a><code>[color&#45;scheme](#meta-color-scheme)</code> per document&#46;

<strong>Example&#58;</strong>

The following declaration indicates that the page is aware of and can handle a color scheme with dark background colors and light foreground colors&#58;

``` text
<meta name="color-scheme" content="dark">
```

To obtain a <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;page&#39;s&#45;supported&#45;color&#45;schemes&#45;2"></a>[page&#39;s supported color&#45;schemes](https://drafts.csswg.org/css-color-adjust/#pages-supported-color-schemes)&#44; user agents must run the following steps&#58;

1.  Let <var>candidate elements</var> be the list of all <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;18"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> elements that meet the following criteria&#44; in <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;tree&#45;order&#45;4"></a>[tree order](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-tree-order)&#58;

    - the element is <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;in&#45;a&#45;document&#45;tree&#45;3"></a>[in a document tree](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#in-a-document-tree)&#59;

    - the element has a <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;name&#45;14"></a><code>[name](#attr-meta-name)</code> attribute&#44; whose value is an <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;ascii&#45;case&#45;insensitive&#45;11"></a>[ASCII case&#45;insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-case-insensitive) match for <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;meta&#45;color&#45;scheme&#45;2"></a><code>[color&#45;scheme](#meta-color-scheme)</code>&#59; and

    - the element has a <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;content&#45;9"></a><code>[content](#attr-meta-content)</code> attribute&#46;

2.  For each <var>element</var> in <var>candidate elements</var>&#58;

    1.  Let <var>parsed</var> be the result of <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;parse&#45;a&#45;list&#45;of&#45;component&#45;values"></a>[parsing a list of component values](https://drafts.csswg.org/css-syntax/#parse-a-list-of-component-values) given the value of <var>element</var>&#39;s <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;content&#45;10"></a><code>[content](#attr-meta-content)</code> attribute&#46;
    2.  If <var>parsed</var> is a valid CSS <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;&#39;color&#45;scheme&#39;&#45;3"></a>[&#39;color&#45;scheme&#39;](https://drafts.csswg.org/css-color-adjust/#color-scheme-prop) property value&#44; then return <var>parsed</var>&#46;

3.  Return null&#46;

If any <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;19"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> elements are <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;insert&#45;an&#45;element&#45;into&#45;a&#45;document&#45;3"></a>[inserted into the document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#insert-an-element-into-a-document) or <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;remove&#45;an&#45;element&#45;from&#45;a&#45;document&#45;2"></a>[removed from the document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#remove-an-element-from-a-document)&#44; or existing <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;20"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> elements have their <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;name&#45;15"></a><code>[name](#attr-meta-name)</code> or <a id="standard&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;content&#45;11"></a><code>[content](#attr-meta-content)</code> attributes changed&#44; user agents must re&#45;run the above algorithm&#46;

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

Because these rules check successive elements until they find a match&#44; an author can provide multiple such values to handle fallback for legacy user agents&#46; Opposite to how CSS fallback works for properties&#44; the multiple meta elements needs to be arranged with the legacy values after the newer values&#46;

##### <a id="other&#45;metadata&#45;names"></a>4&#46;2&#46;5&#46;2 Other metadata names[](#other-metadata-names)

Anyone can create and use their own <a id="concept&#45;meta&#45;extensions"></a><strong>extensions to the predefined set of metadata names</strong>&#46; There is no requirement to register such extensions&#46;

However&#44; a new metadata name should not be created in any of the following cases&#58;

- If either the name is a <a id="other&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;url"></a>[URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url)&#44; or the value of its accompanying <a id="other&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;content"></a><code>[content](#attr-meta-content)</code> attribute is a <a id="other&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;url&#45;2"></a>[URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url)&#59; in those cases&#44; registering it as an <a id="other&#45;metadata&#45;names&#58;concept&#45;rel&#45;extensions"></a>[extension to the predefined set of link types](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#concept-rel-extensions) is encouraged &#40;rather than creating a new metadata name&#41;&#46;

- If the name is for something expected to have processing requirements in user agents&#59; in that case it ought to be standardized&#46;

Also&#44; before creating and using a new metadata name&#44; consulting the [WHATWG Wiki MetaExtensions page](https://wiki.whatwg.org/wiki/MetaExtensions) is encouraged — to avoid choosing a metadata name that&#39;s already in use&#44; and to avoid duplicating the purpose of any metadata names that are already in use&#44; and to avoid new standardized names clashing with your chosen name&#46; [&#91;WHATWGWIKI&#93;](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#refsWHATWGWIKI)

Anyone is free to edit the WHATWG Wiki MetaExtensions page at any time to add a metadata name&#46; New metadata names can be specified with the following information&#58;

<strong>Keyword</strong>

The actual name being defined&#46; The name should not be confusingly similar to any other defined name &#40;e&#46;g&#46; differing only in case&#41;&#46;

<strong>Brief description</strong>

A short non&#45;normative description of what the metadata name&#39;s meaning is&#44; including the format the value is required to be in&#46;

<strong>Specification</strong>

A link to a more detailed description of the metadata name&#39;s semantics and requirements&#46; It could be another page on the wiki&#44; or a link to an external page&#46;

<strong>Synonyms</strong>

A list of other names that have exactly the same processing requirements&#46; Authors should not use the names defined to be synonyms &#40;they are only intended to allow user agents to support legacy content&#41;&#46; Anyone may remove synonyms that are not used in practice&#59; only names that need to be processed as synonyms for compatibility with legacy content are to be registered in this way&#46;

<strong>Status</strong>

One of the following&#58;

<strong>Proposed</strong>

The name has not received wide peer review and approval&#46; Someone has proposed it and is&#44; or soon will be&#44; using it&#46;

<strong>Ratified</strong>

The name has received wide peer review and approval&#46; It has a specification that unambiguously defines how to handle pages that use the name&#44; including when they use it in incorrect ways&#46;

<strong>Discontinued</strong>

The metadata name has received wide peer review and it has been found wanting&#46; Existing pages are using this metadata name&#44; but new pages should avoid it&#46; The &#34;brief description&#34; and &#34;specification&#34; entries will give details of what authors should use instead&#44; if anything&#46;

If a metadata name is found to be redundant with existing values&#44; it should be removed and listed as a synonym for the existing value&#46;

If a metadata name is added in the &#34;proposed&#34; state for a period of a month or more without being used or specified&#44; then it may be removed from the WHATWG Wiki MetaExtensions page&#46;

If a metadata name is added with the &#34;proposed&#34; status and found to be redundant with existing values&#44; it should be removed and listed as a synonym for the existing value&#46; If a metadata name is added with the &#34;proposed&#34; status and found to be harmful&#44; then it should be changed to &#34;discontinued&#34; status&#46;

Anyone can change the status at any time&#44; but should only do so in accordance with the definitions above&#46;

##### <a id="pragma&#45;directives"></a>4&#46;2&#46;5&#46;3 Pragma directives[](#pragma-directives)

When the <a id="attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv"></a><strong><code>http&#45;equiv</code></strong> attribute is specified on a <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element&#44; the element is a pragma directive&#46;

<a id="warning&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;unrelated&#45;to&#45;http&#45;headers"></a>

<strong>Warning&#58;</strong>

Despite the name <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv"></a><code>[http&#45;equiv](#attr-meta-http-equiv)</code>&#44; pragma directives are almost entirely unrelated to HTTP headers&#46; Implementers and web developers are best off thinking of them as entirely separate&#44; and the name as being a historical accident&#46;

In more detail&#44; although the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;refresh"></a><code>[refresh](#attr-meta-http-equiv-refresh)</code> keyword has the same processing model as the corresponding &#96;<a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;refresh"></a><code>[Refresh](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#refresh)</code>&#96; header&#44; every other standardized pragma directive has at least slightly different behavior than the similarly&#45;named header&#46; &#40;And usually dramatically&#45;different behavior&#46;&#41;

Implementers or specification writers contemplating adding new document&#45;level pragmas or HTTP header&#45;controlled switches should be cautious about this mismatch&#44; and avoid perpetuating the existing confusion by adding the same or similar behavior to both an HTTP header and an <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;2"></a><code>[http&#45;equiv](#attr-meta-http-equiv)</code> pragma&#46; Instead&#44; consider providing only an HTTP header&#44; or if an in&#45;document pragma is needed&#44; consider adding a new attribute to <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;2"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> similar to the model used by the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;charset"></a><code>[charset](#attr-meta-charset)</code> attribute&#46; &#40;Note that avoiding in&#45;document pragmas is often the better choice&#44; since the DOM is mutable&#46; Thus&#44; even in the simple case where the developer does not add&#44; remove&#44; or mutate <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;3"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> elements&#44; the policy will go from un&#45;applied to applied during parsing&#44; which can have complex implications&#46;&#41;

The <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;3"></a><code>[http&#45;equiv](#attr-meta-http-equiv)</code> attribute is an <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;enumerated&#45;attribute"></a>[enumerated attribute](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#enumerated-attribute) with the following keywords and states&#58;

<table id="table-http-equiv" class="yesno"><thead><tr><th>Keyword      </th><th>Conforming      </th><th>State      </th><th>Brief description    </th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><dfn data-dfn-for="meta/http-equiv" id="attr-meta-http-equiv-keyword-content-language" data-dfn-type="attr-value"><code>content&#45;language</code></dfn>      </td><td class="no">No      </td><td><a href="#attr-meta-http-equiv-content-language" id="pragma-directives:attr-meta-http-equiv-content-language">Content language</a>      </td><td>Sets the <a href="#pragma-set-default-language" id="pragma-directives:pragma-set-default-language">pragma&#45;set default language</a>&#46;     </td></tr><tr><td><dfn data-dfn-for="meta/http-equiv" id="attr-meta-http-equiv-keyword-content-type" data-dfn-type="attr-value"><code>content&#45;type</code></dfn>      </td><td>      </td><td><a href="#attr-meta-http-equiv-content-type" id="pragma-directives:attr-meta-http-equiv-content-type">Encoding declaration</a>      </td><td>An alternative form of setting the <code id="pragma-directives:attr-meta-charset-2"><a href="#attr-meta-charset">charset</a></code>&#46;     </td></tr><tr><td><dfn data-dfn-for="meta/http-equiv" id="attr-meta-http-equiv-keyword-default-style" data-dfn-type="attr-value"><code>default&#45;style</code></dfn>      </td><td>      </td><td><a href="#attr-meta-http-equiv-default-style" id="pragma-directives:attr-meta-http-equiv-default-style">Default style</a>      </td><td>Sets the <a href="https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#css-style-sheet-set-name" id="pragma-directives:css-style-sheet-set-name" data-x-internal="css-style-sheet-set-name">name</a> of the default      <a id="pragma-directives:css-style-sheet-set" href="https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#css-style-sheet-set" data-x-internal="css-style-sheet-set">CSS style sheet set</a>&#46;     </td></tr><tr><td><dfn data-dfn-for="meta/http-equiv" id="attr-meta-http-equiv-keyword-refresh" data-dfn-type="attr-value"><code>refresh</code></dfn>      </td><td>      </td><td><a href="#attr-meta-http-equiv-refresh" id="pragma-directives:attr-meta-http-equiv-refresh-2">Refresh</a>      </td><td>Acts as a timed redirect&#46;     </td></tr><tr><td><dfn data-dfn-for="meta/http-equiv" id="attr-meta-http-equiv-keyword-set-cookie" data-dfn-type="attr-value"><code>set&#45;cookie</code></dfn>      </td><td class="no">No      </td><td><a href="#attr-meta-http-equiv-set-cookie" id="pragma-directives:attr-meta-http-equiv-set-cookie">Set&#45;Cookie</a>      </td><td>Has no effect&#46;     </td></tr><tr><td><dfn data-dfn-for="meta/http-equiv" id="attr-meta-http-equiv-keyword-x-ua-compatible" data-dfn-type="attr-value"><code>x&#45;ua&#45;compatible</code></dfn>      </td><td>      </td><td><a href="#attr-meta-http-equiv-x-ua-compatible" id="pragma-directives:attr-meta-http-equiv-x-ua-compatible">X&#45;UA&#45;Compatible</a>      </td><td>In practice&#44; encourages Internet Explorer to more closely follow the specifications&#46;     </td></tr><tr><td><dfn data-dfn-for="meta/http-equiv" id="attr-meta-http-equiv-keyword-content-security-policy" data-dfn-type="attr-value"><code>content&#45;security&#45;policy</code></dfn>      </td><td>      </td><td><a href="#attr-meta-http-equiv-content-security-policy" id="pragma-directives:attr-meta-http-equiv-content-security-policy">Content security policy</a>      </td><td><a href="https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-csp/#enforced" id="pragma-directives:enforce-the-policy" data-x-internal="enforce-the-policy">Enforces</a> a <a id="pragma-directives:content-security-policy" href="https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-csp/#content-security-policy-object" data-x-internal="content-security-policy">Content Security      Policy</a> on a <code id="pragma-directives:document"><a href="html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document">Document</a></code>&#46;   </td></tr></tbody></table>

When a <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;4"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element is <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;insert&#45;an&#45;element&#45;into&#45;a&#45;document"></a>[inserted into the document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#insert-an-element-into-a-document)&#44; if its <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;4"></a><code>[http&#45;equiv](#attr-meta-http-equiv)</code> attribute is present and represents one of the above states&#44; then the user agent must run the algorithm appropriate for that state&#44; as described in the following list&#58;

<strong><a id="attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;content&#45;language"></a><strong>Content language state</strong> &#40;<code>http&#45;equiv&#61;&#34;<a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;keyword&#45;content&#45;language"></a><code>[content&#45;language](#attr-meta-http-equiv-keyword-content-language)</code>&#34;</code>&#41;</strong>

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

This feature is non&#45;conforming&#46; Authors are encouraged to use the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;lang"></a><code>[lang](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#attr-lang)</code> attribute instead&#46;

This pragma sets the <a id="pragma&#45;set&#45;default&#45;language"></a><strong>pragma&#45;set default language</strong>&#46; Until such a pragma is successfully processed&#44; there is no <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;pragma&#45;set&#45;default&#45;language&#45;2"></a>[pragma&#45;set default language](#pragma-set-default-language)&#46;

1.  If the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;5"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element has no <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;content"></a><code>[content](#attr-meta-content)</code> attribute&#44; then return&#46;

2.  If the element&#39;s <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;content&#45;2"></a><code>[content](#attr-meta-content)</code> attribute contains a U&#43;002C COMMA character &#40;&#44;&#41;&#44; then return&#46;

3.  Let <var>input</var> be the value of the element&#39;s <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;content&#45;3"></a><code>[content](#attr-meta-content)</code> attribute&#46;

4.  Let <var>position</var> point at the first character of <var>input</var>&#46;

5.  <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;skip&#45;ascii&#45;whitespace"></a>[Skip ASCII whitespace](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#skip-ascii-whitespace) within <var>input</var> given <var>position</var>&#46;

6.  <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;collect&#45;a&#45;sequence&#45;of&#45;code&#45;points"></a>[Collect a sequence of code points](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#collect-a-sequence-of-code-points) that are not <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;space&#45;characters"></a>[ASCII whitespace](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-whitespace) from <var>input</var> given <var>position</var>&#46;

7.  Let <var>candidate</var> be the string that resulted from the previous step&#46;

8.  If <var>candidate</var> is the empty string&#44; return&#46;

9.  Set the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;pragma&#45;set&#45;default&#45;language&#45;3"></a>[pragma&#45;set default language](#pragma-set-default-language) to <var>candidate</var>&#46;

    <strong>Note&#58;</strong>

    If the value consists of multiple space&#45;separated tokens&#44; tokens after the first are ignored&#46;

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

This pragma is almost&#44; but not quite&#44; entirely unlike the HTTP &#96;<a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;http&#45;content&#45;language"></a><code>[Content&#45;Language](https://httpwg.org/specs/rfc7231.html#header.content-language)</code>&#96; header of the same name&#46; [&#91;HTTP&#93;](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#refsHTTP)

<strong><a id="attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;content&#45;type"></a><strong>Encoding declaration state</strong> &#40;<code>http&#45;equiv&#61;&#34;<a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;keyword&#45;content&#45;type"></a><code>[content&#45;type](#attr-meta-http-equiv-keyword-content-type)</code>&#34;</code>&#41;</strong>

The <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;content&#45;type&#45;2"></a>[Encoding declaration state](#attr-meta-http-equiv-content-type) is just an alternative form of setting the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;charset&#45;3"></a><code>[charset](#attr-meta-charset)</code> attribute&#58; it is a <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;character&#45;encoding&#45;declaration"></a>[character encoding declaration](#character-encoding-declaration)&#46; This state&#39;s user agent requirements are all handled by the parsing section of the specification&#46;

For <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;6"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> elements with an <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;5"></a><code>[http&#45;equiv](#attr-meta-http-equiv)</code> attribute in the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;content&#45;type&#45;3"></a>[Encoding declaration state](#attr-meta-http-equiv-content-type)&#44; the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;content&#45;4"></a><code>[content](#attr-meta-content)</code> attribute must have a value that is an <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;ascii&#45;case&#45;insensitive"></a>[ASCII case&#45;insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-case-insensitive) match for a string that consists of&#58; &#34;<code>text&#47;html&#59;</code>&#34;&#44; optionally followed by any number of <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;space&#45;characters&#45;2"></a>[ASCII whitespace](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-whitespace)&#44; followed by &#34;<code>charset&#61;utf&#45;8</code>&#34;&#46;

A document must not contain both a <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;7"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element with an <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;6"></a><code>[http&#45;equiv](#attr-meta-http-equiv)</code> attribute in the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;content&#45;type&#45;4"></a>[Encoding declaration state](#attr-meta-http-equiv-content-type) and a <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;8"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element with the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;charset&#45;4"></a><code>[charset](#attr-meta-charset)</code> attribute present&#46;

The <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;content&#45;type&#45;5"></a>[Encoding declaration state](#attr-meta-http-equiv-content-type) may be used in <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;html&#45;documents"></a>[HTML documents](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#html-document)&#44; but elements with an <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;7"></a><code>[http&#45;equiv](#attr-meta-http-equiv)</code> attribute in that state must not be used in <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;xml&#45;documents"></a>[XML documents](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#xml-document)&#46;

<strong><a id="attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;default&#45;style"></a><strong>Default style state</strong> &#40;<code>http&#45;equiv&#61;&#34;<a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;keyword&#45;default&#45;style"></a><code>[default&#45;style](#attr-meta-http-equiv-keyword-default-style)</code>&#34;</code>&#41;</strong>

<strong>⚠</strong>MDN

[Alternative&#95;style&#95;sheets](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/CSS/Alternative_style_sheets)

Support in one engine only&#46;

Firefox3&#43;Safari&#63;Chrome1–48

------------------------------------------------------------------------

OperaYesEdgeNo

------------------------------------------------------------------------

Edge &#40;Legacy&#41;&#63;Internet Explorer8&#43;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

Firefox Android&#63;Safari iOS&#63;Chrome Android&#63;WebView Android&#63;Samsung Internet&#63;Opera Android&#63;

This pragma sets the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;css&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;set&#45;name&#45;2"></a>[name](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#css-style-sheet-set-name) of the default <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;css&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;set&#45;2"></a>[CSS style sheet set](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#css-style-sheet-set)&#46;

1.  If the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;9"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element has no <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;content&#45;5"></a><code>[content](#attr-meta-content)</code> attribute&#44; or if that attribute&#39;s value is the empty string&#44; then return&#46;

2.  <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;change&#45;the&#45;preferred&#45;css&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;set&#45;name"></a>[Change the preferred CSS style sheet set name](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#change-the-preferred-css-style-sheet-set-name) with the name being the value of the element&#39;s <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;content&#45;6"></a><code>[content](#attr-meta-content)</code> attribute&#46; [&#91;CSSOM&#93;](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#refsCSSOM)

<strong><a id="attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;refresh"></a><strong>Refresh state</strong> &#40;<code>http&#45;equiv&#61;&#34;<a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;keyword&#45;refresh"></a><code>[refresh](#attr-meta-http-equiv-keyword-refresh)</code>&#34;</code>&#41;</strong>

This pragma acts as a timed redirect&#46;

A <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;document&#45;2"></a><code>[Document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document)</code> object has an associated <a id="will&#45;declaratively&#45;refresh"></a><strong>will declaratively refresh</strong> &#40;a boolean&#41;&#46; It is initially false&#46;

1.  If the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;10"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element has no <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;content&#45;7"></a><code>[content](#attr-meta-content)</code> attribute&#44; or if that attribute&#39;s value is the empty string&#44; then return&#46;

2.  Let <var>input</var> be the value of the element&#39;s <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;content&#45;8"></a><code>[content](#attr-meta-content)</code> attribute&#46;

3.  Run the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;shared&#45;declarative&#45;refresh&#45;steps"></a>[shared declarative refresh steps](#shared-declarative-refresh-steps) with the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;11"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element&#39;s <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;node&#45;document"></a>[node document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-node-document)&#44; <var>input</var>&#44; and the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;12"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element&#46;

The <a id="shared&#45;declarative&#45;refresh&#45;steps"></a><strong>shared declarative refresh steps</strong>&#44; given a <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;document&#45;3"></a><code>[Document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document)</code> object <var>document</var>&#44; string <var>input</var>&#44; and optionally a <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;13"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element <var>meta</var>&#44; are as follows&#58;

1.  If <var>document</var>&#39;s <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;will&#45;declaratively&#45;refresh"></a>[will declaratively refresh](#will-declaratively-refresh) is true&#44; then return&#46;

2.  Let <var>position</var> point at the first <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;code&#45;point"></a>[code point](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point) of <var>input</var>&#46;

3.  <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;skip&#45;ascii&#45;whitespace&#45;2"></a>[Skip ASCII whitespace](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#skip-ascii-whitespace) within <var>input</var> given <var>position</var>&#46;

4.  Let <var>time</var> be 0&#46;

5.  <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;collect&#45;a&#45;sequence&#45;of&#45;code&#45;points&#45;2"></a>[Collect a sequence of code points](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#collect-a-sequence-of-code-points) that are <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;ascii&#45;digits"></a>[ASCII digits](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-digit) from <var>input</var> given <var>position</var>&#44; and let <var>timeString</var> be the result&#46;

6.  If <var>timeString</var> is the empty string&#58;

    1.  If the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;code&#45;point&#45;2"></a>[code point](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point) in <var>input</var> pointed to by <var>position</var> is not U&#43;002E &#40;&#46;&#41;&#44; then return&#46;

7.  Otherwise&#44; set <var>time</var> to the result of parsing <var>timeString</var> using the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;rules&#45;for&#45;parsing&#45;non&#45;negative&#45;integers"></a>[rules for parsing non&#45;negative integers](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#rules-for-parsing-non-negative-integers)&#46;

8.  <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;collect&#45;a&#45;sequence&#45;of&#45;code&#45;points&#45;3"></a>[Collect a sequence of code points](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#collect-a-sequence-of-code-points) that are <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;ascii&#45;digits&#45;2"></a>[ASCII digits](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-digit) and U&#43;002E FULL STOP characters &#40;&#46;&#41; from <var>input</var> given <var>position</var>&#46; Ignore any collected characters&#46;

9.  Let <var>urlRecord</var> be <var>document</var>&#39;s <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;the&#45;document&#39;s&#45;address"></a>[URL](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document-url)&#46;

10. If <var>position</var> is not past the end of <var>input</var>&#58;

    1.  If the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;code&#45;point&#45;3"></a>[code point](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point) in <var>input</var> pointed to by <var>position</var> is not U&#43;003B &#40;&#59;&#41;&#44; U&#43;002C &#40;&#44;&#41;&#44; or <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;space&#45;characters&#45;3"></a>[ASCII whitespace](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-whitespace)&#44; then return&#46;

    2.  <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;skip&#45;ascii&#45;whitespace&#45;3"></a>[Skip ASCII whitespace](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#skip-ascii-whitespace) within <var>input</var> given <var>position</var>&#46;

    3.  If the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;code&#45;point&#45;4"></a>[code point](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point) in <var>input</var> pointed to by <var>position</var> is U&#43;003B &#40;&#59;&#41; or U&#43;002C &#40;&#44;&#41;&#44; then advance <var>position</var> to the next <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;code&#45;point&#45;5"></a>[code point](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point)&#46;

    4.  <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;skip&#45;ascii&#45;whitespace&#45;4"></a>[Skip ASCII whitespace](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#skip-ascii-whitespace) within <var>input</var> given <var>position</var>&#46;

11. If <var>position</var> is not past the end of <var>input</var>&#58;

    1.  Let <var>urlString</var> be the substring of <var>input</var> from the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;code&#45;point&#45;6"></a>[code point](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point) at <var>position</var> to the end of the string&#46;

    2.  If the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;code&#45;point&#45;7"></a>[code point](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point) in <var>input</var> pointed to by <var>position</var> is U&#43;0055 &#40;U&#41; or U&#43;0075 &#40;u&#41;&#44; then advance <var>position</var> to the next <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;code&#45;point&#45;8"></a>[code point](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point)&#46; Otherwise&#44; jump to the step labeled <em>skip quotes</em>&#46;

    3.  If the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;code&#45;point&#45;9"></a>[code point](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point) in <var>input</var> pointed to by <var>position</var> is U&#43;0052 &#40;R&#41; or U&#43;0072 &#40;r&#41;&#44; then advance <var>position</var> to the next <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;code&#45;point&#45;10"></a>[code point](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point)&#46; Otherwise&#44; jump to the step labeled <em>parse</em>&#46;

    4.  If the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;code&#45;point&#45;11"></a>[code point](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point) in <var>input</var> pointed to by <var>position</var> is U&#43;004C &#40;L&#41; or U&#43;006C &#40;l&#41;&#44; then advance <var>position</var> to the next <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;code&#45;point&#45;12"></a>[code point](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point)&#46; Otherwise&#44; jump to the step labeled <em>parse</em>&#46;

    5.  <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;skip&#45;ascii&#45;whitespace&#45;5"></a>[Skip ASCII whitespace](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#skip-ascii-whitespace) within <var>input</var> given <var>position</var>&#46;

    6.  If the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;code&#45;point&#45;13"></a>[code point](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point) in <var>input</var> pointed to by <var>position</var> is U&#43;003D &#40;&#61;&#41;&#44; then advance <var>position</var> to the next <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;code&#45;point&#45;14"></a>[code point](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point)&#46; Otherwise&#44; jump to the step labeled <em>parse</em>&#46;

    7.  <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;skip&#45;ascii&#45;whitespace&#45;6"></a>[Skip ASCII whitespace](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#skip-ascii-whitespace) within <var>input</var> given <var>position</var>&#46;

    8.  <em>Skip quotes</em>&#58; If the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;code&#45;point&#45;15"></a>[code point](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point) in <var>input</var> pointed to by <var>position</var> is U&#43;0027 &#40;&#39;&#41; or U&#43;0022 &#40;&#34;&#41;&#44; then let <var>quote</var> be that <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;code&#45;point&#45;16"></a>[code point](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point)&#44; and advance <var>position</var> to the next <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;code&#45;point&#45;17"></a>[code point](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point)&#46; Otherwise&#44; let <var>quote</var> be the empty string&#46;

    9.  Set <var>urlString</var> to the substring of <var>input</var> from the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;code&#45;point&#45;18"></a>[code point](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point) at <var>position</var> to the end of the string&#46;

    10. If <var>quote</var> is not the empty string&#44; and there is a <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;code&#45;point&#45;19"></a>[code point](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point) in <var>urlString</var> equal to <var>quote</var>&#44; then truncate <var>urlString</var> at that <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;code&#45;point&#45;20"></a>[code point](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point)&#44; so that it and all subsequent <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;code&#45;point&#45;21"></a>[code points](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point) are removed&#46;

    11. <em>Parse</em>&#58; Set <var>urlRecord</var> to the result of <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;encoding&#45;parsing&#45;a&#45;url"></a>[encoding&#45;parsing a URL](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#encoding-parsing-a-url) given <var>urlString</var>&#44; relative to <var>document</var>&#46;

    12. If <var>urlRecord</var> is failure&#44; then return&#46;

    13. If <var>urlRecord</var>&#39;s <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;concept&#45;url&#45;scheme"></a>[scheme](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-scheme) is &#34;<a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;the&#45;javascript&#58;&#45;url&#45;special&#45;case"></a><code>[javascript](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#the-javascript:-url-special-case)</code>&#34;&#44; then return&#46;

12. Set <var>document</var>&#39;s <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;will&#45;declaratively&#45;refresh&#45;2"></a>[will declaratively refresh](#will-declaratively-refresh) to true&#46;

13. Perform one or more of the following steps&#58;

    - After the refresh has come due &#40;as defined below&#41;&#44; if the user has not canceled the redirect and&#44; if <var>meta</var> is given&#44; <var>document</var>&#39;s <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;active&#45;sandboxing&#45;flag&#45;set"></a>[active sandboxing flag set](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#active-sandboxing-flag-set) does not have the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;sandboxed&#45;automatic&#45;features&#45;browsing&#45;context&#45;flag"></a>[sandboxed automatic features browsing context flag](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#sandboxed-automatic-features-browsing-context-flag) set&#44; then <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;navigate"></a>[navigate](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#navigate) <var>document</var>&#39;s <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;node&#45;navigable"></a>[node navigable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#node-navigable) to <var>urlRecord</var> using <var>document</var>&#44; with <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;navigation&#45;hh"></a><em>[historyHandling](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#navigation-hh)</em> set to &#34;<a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;navigationhistorybehavior&#45;replace"></a><code>[replace](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#navigationhistorybehavior-replace)</code>&#34;&#46;

      For the purposes of the previous paragraph&#44; a refresh is said to have come due as soon as the <em>later</em> of the following two conditions occurs&#58;

      - At least <var>time</var> seconds have elapsed since <var>document</var>&#39;s <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;completely&#45;loaded&#45;time"></a>[completely loaded time](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#completely-loaded-time)&#44; adjusted to take into account user or user agent preferences&#46;
      - If <var>meta</var> is given&#44; at least <var>time</var> seconds have elapsed since <var>meta</var> was <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;insert&#45;an&#45;element&#45;into&#45;a&#45;document&#45;2"></a>[inserted into the document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#insert-an-element-into-a-document) <var>document</var>&#44; adjusted to take into account user or user agent preferences&#46;

      <strong>Note&#58;</strong>
