      It is important to use <var>document</var> here&#44; and not <var>meta</var>&#39;s <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;node&#45;document&#45;2"></a>[node document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-node-document)&#44; as that might have changed between the initial set of steps and the refresh coming due and <var>meta</var> is not always given &#40;in case of the HTTP &#96;<a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;refresh&#45;2"></a><code>[Refresh](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#refresh)</code>&#96; header&#41;&#46;

    - Provide the user with an interface that&#44; when selected&#44; <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;navigate&#45;2"></a>[navigates](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#navigate) <var>document</var>&#39;s <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;node&#45;navigable&#45;2"></a>[node navigable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#node-navigable) to <var>urlRecord</var> using <var>document</var>&#46;

    - Do nothing&#46;

    In addition&#44; the user agent may&#44; as with anything&#44; inform the user of any and all aspects of its operation&#44; including the state of any timers&#44; the destinations of any timed redirects&#44; and so forth&#46;

<a id="conformance&#45;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;refresh"></a>For <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;14"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> elements with an <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;8"></a><code>[http&#45;equiv](#attr-meta-http-equiv)</code> attribute in the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;refresh&#45;3"></a>[Refresh state](#attr-meta-http-equiv-refresh)&#44; the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;content&#45;9"></a><code>[content](#attr-meta-content)</code> attribute must have a value consisting either of&#58;

- just a <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;valid&#45;non&#45;negative&#45;integer"></a>[valid non&#45;negative integer](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#valid-non-negative-integer)&#44; or
- a <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;valid&#45;non&#45;negative&#45;integer&#45;2"></a>[valid non&#45;negative integer](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#valid-non-negative-integer)&#44; followed by a U&#43;003B SEMICOLON character &#40;&#59;&#41;&#44; followed by one or more <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;space&#45;characters&#45;4"></a>[ASCII whitespace](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-whitespace)&#44; followed by a substring that is an <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;ascii&#45;case&#45;insensitive&#45;2"></a>[ASCII case&#45;insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-case-insensitive) match for the string &#34;<code>URL</code>&#34;&#44; followed by a U&#43;003D EQUALS SIGN character &#40;&#61;&#41;&#44; followed by a <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;valid&#45;url&#45;string"></a>[valid URL string](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#valid-url-string) that does not start with a literal U&#43;0027 APOSTROPHE &#40;&#39;&#41; or U&#43;0022 QUOTATION MARK &#40;&#34;&#41; character&#46;

In the former case&#44; the integer represents a number of seconds before the page is to be reloaded&#59; in the latter case the integer represents a number of seconds before the page is to be replaced by the page at the given <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;url"></a>[URL](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url)&#46;

<strong>Example&#58;</strong>

A news organization&#39;s front page could include the following markup in the page&#39;s <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;the&#45;head&#45;element"></a><code>[head](#the-head-element)</code> element&#44; to ensure that the page automatically reloads from the server every five minutes&#58;

``` text
<meta http-equiv="Refresh" content="300">
```

<strong>Example&#58;</strong>

A sequence of pages could be used as an automated slide show by making each page refresh to the next page in the sequence&#44; using markup such as the following&#58;

``` text
<meta http-equiv="Refresh" content="20; URL=page4.html">
```

<strong><a id="attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;set&#45;cookie"></a><strong>Set&#45;Cookie state</strong> &#40;<code>http&#45;equiv&#61;&#34;<a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;keyword&#45;set&#45;cookie"></a><code>[set&#45;cookie](#attr-meta-http-equiv-keyword-set-cookie)</code>&#34;</code>&#41;</strong>

This pragma is non&#45;conforming and has no effect&#46;

User agents are required to ignore this pragma&#46;

<strong><a id="attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;x&#45;ua&#45;compatible"></a><strong>X&#45;UA&#45;Compatible state</strong> &#40;<code>http&#45;equiv&#61;&#34;<a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;keyword&#45;x&#45;ua&#45;compatible"></a><code>[x&#45;ua&#45;compatible](#attr-meta-http-equiv-keyword-x-ua-compatible)</code>&#34;</code>&#41;</strong>

In practice&#44; this pragma encourages Internet Explorer to more closely follow the specifications&#46;

For <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;15"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> elements with an <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;9"></a><code>[http&#45;equiv](#attr-meta-http-equiv)</code> attribute in the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;x&#45;ua&#45;compatible&#45;2"></a>[X&#45;UA&#45;Compatible state](#attr-meta-http-equiv-x-ua-compatible)&#44; the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;content&#45;10"></a><code>[content](#attr-meta-content)</code> attribute must have a value that is an <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;ascii&#45;case&#45;insensitive&#45;3"></a>[ASCII case&#45;insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-case-insensitive) match for the string &#34;<code>IE&#61;edge</code>&#34;&#46;

User agents are required to ignore this pragma&#46;

<strong><a id="attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;content&#45;security&#45;policy"></a><strong>Content security policy state</strong> &#40;<code>http&#45;equiv&#61;&#34;<a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;keyword&#45;content&#45;security&#45;policy"></a><code>[content&#45;security&#45;policy](#attr-meta-http-equiv-keyword-content-security-policy)</code>&#34;</code>&#41;</strong>

This pragma <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;enforce&#45;the&#45;policy&#45;2"></a>[enforces](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-csp/#enforced) a <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;content&#45;security&#45;policy&#45;2"></a>[Content Security Policy](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-csp/#content-security-policy-object) on a <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;document&#45;4"></a><code>[Document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document)</code>&#46; [&#91;CSP&#93;](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#refsCSP)

1.  If the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;16"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element is not a child of a <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;the&#45;head&#45;element&#45;2"></a><code>[head](#the-head-element)</code> element&#44; return&#46;

2.  If the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;17"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element has no <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;content&#45;11"></a><code>[content](#attr-meta-content)</code> attribute&#44; or if that attribute&#39;s value is the empty string&#44; then return&#46;

3.  Let <var>policy</var> be the result of executing Content Security Policy&#39;s <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;parse&#45;a&#45;serialized&#45;content&#45;security&#45;policy"></a>[parse a serialized Content Security Policy](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-csp/#parse-serialized-policy) algorithm on the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;18"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element&#39;s <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;content&#45;12"></a><code>[content](#attr-meta-content)</code> attribute&#39;s value&#44; with a source of &#34;meta&#34;&#44; and a disposition of &#34;enforce&#34;&#46;

4.  Remove all occurrences of the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;report&#45;uri&#45;directive"></a><code>[report&#45;uri](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-csp/#report-uri)</code>&#44; <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;frame&#45;ancestors&#45;directive"></a><code>[frame&#45;ancestors](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-csp/#frame-ancestors)</code>&#44; and <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;sandbox&#45;directive"></a><code>[sandbox](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-csp/#sandbox)</code> <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;content&#45;security&#45;policy&#45;directive"></a>[directives](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-csp/#directives) from <var>policy</var>&#46;

5.  <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;enforce&#45;the&#45;policy&#45;3"></a>[Enforce the policy](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-csp/#enforced) <var>policy</var>&#46;

For <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;19"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> elements with an <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;10"></a><code>[http&#45;equiv](#attr-meta-http-equiv)</code> attribute in the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;content&#45;security&#45;policy&#45;2"></a>[Content security policy state](#attr-meta-http-equiv-content-security-policy)&#44; the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;content&#45;13"></a><code>[content](#attr-meta-content)</code> attribute must have a value consisting of a <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;content&#45;security&#45;policy&#45;syntax"></a>[valid Content Security Policy](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-csp/#grammardef-serialized-policy)&#44; but must not contain any <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;report&#45;uri&#45;directive&#45;2"></a><code>[report&#45;uri](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-csp/#report-uri)</code>&#44; <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;frame&#45;ancestors&#45;directive&#45;2"></a><code>[frame&#45;ancestors](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-csp/#frame-ancestors)</code>&#44; or <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;sandbox&#45;directive&#45;2"></a><code>[sandbox](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-csp/#sandbox)</code> <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;content&#45;security&#45;policy&#45;directive&#45;2"></a>[directives](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-csp/#directives)&#46; The <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;content&#45;security&#45;policy&#45;3"></a>[Content Security Policy](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-csp/#content-security-policy-object) given in the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;content&#45;14"></a><code>[content](#attr-meta-content)</code> attribute will be <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;enforce&#45;the&#45;policy&#45;4"></a>[enforced](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-csp/#enforced) upon the current document&#46; [&#91;CSP&#93;](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#refsCSP)

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

At the time of inserting the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;20"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element to the document&#44; it is possible that some resources have already been fetched&#46; For example&#44; images might be stored in the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;list&#45;of&#45;available&#45;images"></a>[list of available images](html-language-elements-embedded--877d6146487f--48a1a13e5cfe.md#list-of-available-images) prior to dynamically inserting a <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;21"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element with an <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;11"></a><code>[http&#45;equiv](#attr-meta-http-equiv)</code> attribute in the <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;content&#45;security&#45;policy&#45;3"></a>[Content security policy state](#attr-meta-http-equiv-content-security-policy)&#46; Resources that have already been fetched are not guaranteed to be blocked by a <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;content&#45;security&#45;policy&#45;4"></a>[Content Security Policy](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-csp/#content-security-policy-object) that&#39;s <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;enforce&#45;the&#45;policy&#45;5"></a>[enforced](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-csp/#enforced) late&#46;

<strong>Example&#58;</strong>

A page might choose to mitigate the risk of cross&#45;site scripting attacks by preventing the execution of inline JavaScript&#44; as well as blocking all plugin content&#44; using a policy such as the following&#58;

``` text
<meta http-equiv="Content-Security-Policy" content="script-src 'self'; object-src 'none'">
```

There must not be more than one <a id="pragma&#45;directives&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;22"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element with any particular state in the document at a time&#46;

##### <a id="charset"></a>4&#46;2&#46;5&#46;4 Specifying the document&#39;s character encoding[](#charset)

A <a id="character&#45;encoding&#45;declaration"></a><strong>character encoding declaration</strong> is a mechanism by which the <a id="charset&#58;encoding"></a>[character encoding](https://encoding.spec.whatwg.org/#encoding) used to store or transmit a document is specified&#46;

The Encoding standard requires use of the <a id="charset&#58;utf&#45;8"></a>[UTF&#45;8](https://encoding.spec.whatwg.org/#utf-8) <a id="charset&#58;encoding&#45;2"></a>[character encoding](https://encoding.spec.whatwg.org/#encoding) and requires use of the &#34;<code>utf&#45;8</code>&#34; <a id="charset&#58;encoding&#45;label"></a>[encoding label](https://encoding.spec.whatwg.org/#label) to identify it&#46; Those requirements necessitate that the document&#39;s <a id="charset&#58;character&#45;encoding&#45;declaration"></a>[character encoding declaration](#character-encoding-declaration)&#44; if it exists&#44; specifies an <a id="charset&#58;encoding&#45;label&#45;2"></a>[encoding label](https://encoding.spec.whatwg.org/#label) using an <a id="charset&#58;ascii&#45;case&#45;insensitive"></a>[ASCII case&#45;insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-case-insensitive) match for &#34;<code>utf&#45;8</code>&#34;&#46; Regardless of whether a <a id="charset&#58;character&#45;encoding&#45;declaration&#45;2"></a>[character encoding declaration](#character-encoding-declaration) is present or not&#44; the actual <a id="charset&#58;document&#39;s&#45;character&#45;encoding"></a>[character encoding](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document-encoding) used to encode the document must be <a id="charset&#58;utf&#45;8&#45;2"></a>[UTF&#45;8](https://encoding.spec.whatwg.org/#utf-8)&#46; [&#91;ENCODING&#93;](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#refsENCODING)

To enforce the above rules&#44; authoring tools must default to using <a id="charset&#58;utf&#45;8&#45;3"></a>[UTF&#45;8](https://encoding.spec.whatwg.org/#utf-8) for newly&#45;created documents&#46;

The following restrictions also apply&#58;

- The character encoding declaration must be serialized without the use of <a id="charset&#58;syntax&#45;charref"></a>[character references](html-language-syntax-parser--877d6146487f--37a9ba51eed8.md#syntax-charref) or character escapes of any kind&#46;
- <a id="charset1024"></a><a id="charset512"></a>The element containing the character encoding declaration must be serialized completely within the first 1024 bytes of the document&#46;

In addition&#44; due to a number of restrictions on <a id="charset&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> elements&#44; there can only be one <a id="charset&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;2"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code>&#45;based character encoding declaration per document&#46;

If an <a id="charset&#58;html&#45;documents"></a>[HTML document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#html-document) does not start with a BOM&#44; and its <a id="charset&#58;encoding&#45;3"></a>[encoding](https://encoding.spec.whatwg.org/#encoding) is not explicitly given by <a id="charset&#58;content&#45;type"></a>[Content&#45;Type metadata](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#content-type)&#44; and the document is not <a id="charset&#58;an&#45;iframe&#45;srcdoc&#45;document"></a>[an <code>iframe</code> <code>srcdoc</code> document](html-language-elements-embedded--877d6146487f--48a1a13e5cfe.md#an-iframe-srcdoc-document)&#44; then the encoding must be specified using a <a id="charset&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;3"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element with a <a id="charset&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;charset"></a><code>[charset](#attr-meta-charset)</code> attribute or a <a id="charset&#58;the&#45;meta&#45;element&#45;4"></a><code>[meta](#the-meta-element)</code> element with an <a id="charset&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv"></a><code>[http&#45;equiv](#attr-meta-http-equiv)</code> attribute in the <a id="charset&#58;attr&#45;meta&#45;http&#45;equiv&#45;content&#45;type"></a>[Encoding declaration state](#attr-meta-http-equiv-content-type)&#46;

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

A character encoding declaration is required &#40;either in the <a id="charset&#58;content&#45;type&#45;2"></a>[Content&#45;Type metadata](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#content-type) or explicitly in the file&#41; even when all characters are in the ASCII range&#44; because a character encoding is needed to process non&#45;ASCII characters entered by the user in forms&#44; in URLs generated by scripts&#44; and so forth&#46;

Using non&#45;UTF&#45;8 encodings can have unexpected results on form submission and URL encodings&#44; which use the <a id="charset&#58;document&#39;s&#45;character&#45;encoding&#45;2"></a>[document&#39;s character encoding](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-document-encoding) by default&#46;

If the document is <a id="charset&#58;an&#45;iframe&#45;srcdoc&#45;document&#45;2"></a>[an <code>iframe</code> <code>srcdoc</code> document](html-language-elements-embedded--877d6146487f--48a1a13e5cfe.md#an-iframe-srcdoc-document)&#44; the document must not have a <a id="charset&#58;character&#45;encoding&#45;declaration&#45;3"></a>[character encoding declaration](#character-encoding-declaration)&#46; &#40;In this case&#44; the source is already decoded&#44; since it is part of the document that contained the <a id="charset&#58;the&#45;iframe&#45;element"></a><code>[iframe](html-language-elements-embedded--877d6146487f--48a1a13e5cfe.md#the-iframe-element)</code>&#46;&#41;

In XML&#44; the XML declaration should be used for inline character encoding information&#44; if necessary&#46;

<strong>Example&#58;</strong>

In HTML&#44; to declare that the character encoding is <a id="charset&#58;utf&#45;8&#45;4"></a>[UTF&#45;8](https://encoding.spec.whatwg.org/#utf-8)&#44; the author could include the following markup near the top of the document &#40;in the <a id="charset&#58;the&#45;head&#45;element"></a><code>[head](#the-head-element)</code> element&#41;&#58;

``` text
<meta charset="utf-8">
```

In XML&#44; the XML declaration would be used instead&#44; at the very top of the markup&#58;

``` text
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
```

<strong><a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;categories"></a>[Categories](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-categories)&#58;</strong>

<a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;metadata&#45;content&#45;2"></a>[Metadata content](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#metadata-content-2)&#46;

<a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;flow&#45;content&#45;2"></a>[Flow content](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#flow-content-2)&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;contexts"></a>[Contexts in which this element can be used](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-contexts)&#58;</strong>

Where <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;metadata&#45;content&#45;2&#45;2"></a>[metadata content](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#metadata-content-2) is expected&#46;

In a <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;the&#45;noscript&#45;element"></a><code>[noscript](html-language-elements-interactive-scripting--877d6146487f--05bb1d85cc89.md#the-noscript-element)</code> element that is a child of a <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;the&#45;head&#45;element"></a><code>[head](#the-head-element)</code> element&#46;

Where <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;flow&#45;content&#45;2&#45;2"></a>[flow content](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#flow-content-2) is expected&#44; as the first child of its parent&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;content&#45;model"></a>[Content model](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-content-model)&#58;</strong>

<a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;text&#45;content"></a>[Text](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#text-content) that gives a <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;conformant&#45;style&#45;sheet"></a>[conformant style sheet](https://drafts.csswg.org/css-syntax/#w3c-conformance-classes)&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;tag&#45;omission"></a>[Tag omission in text&#47;html](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-tag-omission)&#58;</strong>

Neither tag is omissible&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;attributes"></a>[Content attributes](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-attributes)&#58;</strong>

<a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;global&#45;attributes"></a>[Global attributes](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#global-attributes)

<a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;style&#45;media"></a><code>[media](#attr-style-media)</code> — Applicable media

<a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;style&#45;blocking"></a><code>[blocking](#attr-style-blocking)</code> — Whether the element is <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;potentially&#45;render&#45;blocking"></a>[potentially render&#45;blocking](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#potentially-render-blocking)

Also&#44; the <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;style&#45;title"></a><code>[title](#attr-style-title)</code> attribute <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;style&#45;title&#45;2"></a>[has special semantics](#attr-style-title) on this element&#58; <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;css&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;set&#45;name"></a>[CSS style sheet set name](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#css-style-sheet-set-name)

<strong><a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;accessibility&#45;considerations"></a>[Accessibility considerations](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-accessibility-considerations)&#58;</strong>

[For authors](https://w3c.github.io/html-aria/#el-style)&#46;

[For implementers](https://w3c.github.io/html-aam/#el-style)&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;sanitization"></a>[Sanitization](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-sanitization)&#58;</strong>

<a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;sanitizer&#45;uncategorized"></a>[Uncategorized](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#sanitizer-uncategorized)&#46;

<strong><a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;element&#45;dom"></a>[DOM interface](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#concept-element-dom)&#58;</strong>

<a id="htmlstyleelement"></a><a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;htmlelement"></a><a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;htmlconstructor"></a><a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;dom&#45;style&#45;disabled"></a><a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;cereactions"></a><a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;xattr&#45;reflect"></a><a id="dom&#45;style&#45;media"></a><a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;dom&#45;domtokenlist&#45;value"></a><a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;xattr&#45;reflect&#45;2"></a><a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;domtokenlist"></a><a id="dom&#45;style&#45;blocking"></a><a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;htmlstyleelement"></a><a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;linkstyle"></a>

``` text
[Exposed=Window]
interface HTMLStyleElement : HTMLElement {
  [HTMLConstructor] constructor();

  attribute boolean disabled;
  [CEReactions, Reflect] attribute DOMString media;
  [SameObject, PutForwards=value, Reflect] readonly attribute DOMTokenList blocking;

  // also has obsolete members
};
HTMLStyleElement includes LinkStyle;
```

The <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;the&#45;style&#45;element"></a><code>[style](#the-style-element)</code> element allows authors to embed CSS style sheets in their documents&#46; The <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;the&#45;style&#45;element&#45;2"></a><code>[style](#the-style-element)</code> element is one of several inputs to the styling processing model&#46; The element does not <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;represents"></a>[represent](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#represents) content for the user&#46;

<strong>✔</strong>MDN

[HTMLStyleElement&#47;disabled](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/HTMLStyleElement/disabled)

Support in all current engines&#46;

Firefox1&#43;Safari1&#43;Chrome1&#43;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

Opera12&#46;1&#43;Edge79&#43;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

Edge &#40;Legacy&#41;13&#43;Internet Explorer5&#46;5&#43;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

Firefox Android&#63;Safari iOS&#63;Chrome Android&#63;WebView Android&#63;Samsung Internet&#63;Opera Android12&#46;1&#43;

The <a id="dom&#45;style&#45;disabled"></a><strong><code>disabled</code></strong> getter steps are&#58;

1.  If <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;this"></a>[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this) does not have an <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;associated&#45;css&#45;style&#45;sheet"></a>[associated CSS style sheet](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#associated-css-style-sheet)&#44; return false&#46;

2.  If <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;this&#45;2"></a>[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)&#39;s <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;associated&#45;css&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;2"></a>[associated CSS style sheet](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#associated-css-style-sheet)&#39;s <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;css&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;disabled&#45;flag"></a>[disabled flag](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#concept-css-style-sheet-disabled-flag) is set&#44; return true&#46;

3.  Return false&#46;

The <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;dom&#45;style&#45;disabled&#45;2"></a><code>[disabled](#dom-style-disabled)</code> setter steps are&#58;

1.  If <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;this&#45;3"></a>[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this) does not have an <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;associated&#45;css&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;3"></a>[associated CSS style sheet](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#associated-css-style-sheet)&#44; return&#46;

2.  If the given value is true&#44; set <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;this&#45;4"></a>[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)&#39;s <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;associated&#45;css&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;4"></a>[associated CSS style sheet](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#associated-css-style-sheet)&#39;s <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;css&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;disabled&#45;flag&#45;2"></a>[disabled flag](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#concept-css-style-sheet-disabled-flag)&#46; Otherwise&#44; unset <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;this&#45;5"></a>[this](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#this)&#39;s <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;associated&#45;css&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;5"></a>[associated CSS style sheet](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#associated-css-style-sheet)&#39;s <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;css&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;disabled&#45;flag&#45;3"></a>[disabled flag](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#concept-css-style-sheet-disabled-flag)&#46;

<strong>Example&#58;</strong>

Importantly&#44; <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;dom&#45;style&#45;disabled&#45;3"></a><code>[disabled](#dom-style-disabled)</code> attribute assignments only take effect when the <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;the&#45;style&#45;element&#45;3"></a><code>[style](#the-style-element)</code> element has an <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;associated&#45;css&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;6"></a>[associated CSS style sheet](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#associated-css-style-sheet)&#58;

``` text
const style = document.createElement('style');
style.disabled = true;
style.textContent = 'body { background-color: red; }';
document.body.append(style);
console.log(style.disabled); // false
```

The <a id="attr&#45;style&#45;media"></a><strong><code>media</code></strong> attribute says which media the styles apply to&#46; The value must be a <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;valid&#45;media&#45;query&#45;list"></a>[valid media query list](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#valid-media-query-list)&#46; The user agent must apply the styles when the <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;style&#45;media&#45;2"></a><code>[media](#attr-style-media)</code> attribute&#39;s value <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;matches&#45;the&#45;environment"></a>[matches the environment](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#matches-the-environment) and the other relevant conditions apply&#44; and must not apply them otherwise&#46;

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

The styles might be further limited in scope&#44; e&#46;g&#46; in CSS with the use of <code>&#64;media</code> blocks&#46; This specification does not override such further restrictions or requirements&#46;

<a id="style&#45;default&#45;media"></a>The default&#44; if the <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;style&#45;media&#45;3"></a><code>[media](#attr-style-media)</code> attribute is omitted&#44; is &#34;<code>all</code>&#34;&#44; meaning that by default styles apply to all media&#46;

The <a id="attr&#45;style&#45;blocking"></a><strong><code>blocking</code></strong> attribute is a <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;blocking&#45;attribute"></a>[blocking attribute](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#blocking-attribute)&#46;

<strong>⚠</strong>MDN

[Alternative&#95;style&#95;sheets](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/CSS/Alternative_style_sheets)

Support in one engine only&#46;

Firefox3&#43;Safari&#63;Chrome1–48

------------------------------------------------------------------------

OperaYesEdgeNo

------------------------------------------------------------------------

Edge &#40;Legacy&#41;&#63;Internet Explorer8&#43;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

Firefox Android&#63;Safari iOS&#63;Chrome Android&#63;WebView Android&#63;Samsung Internet&#63;Opera Android&#63;

<a id="title&#45;on&#45;style"></a>The <a id="attr&#45;style&#45;title"></a><strong><code>title</code></strong> attribute on <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;the&#45;style&#45;element&#45;4"></a><code>[style](#the-style-element)</code> elements defines <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;css&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;set"></a>[CSS style sheet sets](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#css-style-sheet-set)&#46; If the <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;the&#45;style&#45;element&#45;5"></a><code>[style](#the-style-element)</code> element has no <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;style&#45;title&#45;3"></a><code>[title](#attr-style-title)</code> attribute&#44; then it has no title&#59; the <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;title"></a><code>[title](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#attr-title)</code> attribute of ancestors does not apply to the <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;the&#45;style&#45;element&#45;6"></a><code>[style](#the-style-element)</code> element&#46; If the <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;the&#45;style&#45;element&#45;7"></a><code>[style](#the-style-element)</code> element is not <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;in&#45;a&#45;document&#45;tree"></a>[in a document tree](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#in-a-document-tree)&#44; then the <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;style&#45;title&#45;4"></a><code>[title](#attr-style-title)</code> attribute is ignored&#46; [&#91;CSSOM&#93;](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#refsCSSOM)

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

The <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;style&#45;title&#45;5"></a><code>[title](#attr-style-title)</code> attribute on <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;the&#45;style&#45;element&#45;8"></a><code>[style](#the-style-element)</code> elements&#44; like the <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;link&#45;title"></a><code>[title](#attr-link-title)</code> attribute on <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> elements&#44; differs from the global <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;title&#45;2"></a><code>[title](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#attr-title)</code> attribute in that a <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;the&#45;style&#45;element&#45;9"></a><code>[style](#the-style-element)</code> block without a title does not inherit the title of the parent element&#58; it merely has no title&#46;

The <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;child&#45;text&#45;content"></a>[child text content](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-child-text-content) of a <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;the&#45;style&#45;element&#45;10"></a><code>[style](#the-style-element)</code> element must be that of a <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;conformant&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;2"></a>[conformant style sheet](https://drafts.csswg.org/css-syntax/#w3c-conformance-classes)&#46;

If a <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;the&#45;style&#45;element&#45;11"></a><code>[style](#the-style-element)</code> element is a descendant of a <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;the&#45;body&#45;element"></a><code>[body](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#the-body-element)</code> element&#44; then the style sheet given by its <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;child&#45;text&#45;content&#45;2"></a>[child text content](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-child-text-content) must not contain any <code>&#64;import</code> rules&#44; and each <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;style&#45;rule"></a>[style rule](https://drafts.csswg.org/css-syntax/#style-rule) and each <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;at&#45;scope"></a><code>[&#64;scope](https://drafts.csswg.org/css-cascade-6/#at-ruledef-scope)</code> rule with a <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;scope&#45;start"></a>[&#60;scope&#45;start&#62;](https://drafts.csswg.org/css-cascade-6/#typedef-scope-start) in it&#44; other than those inside an <code>&#64;mixin</code> or <code>&#64;supports&#45;condition</code> rule&#44; must be inside an <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;at&#45;scope&#45;2"></a><code>[&#64;scope](https://drafts.csswg.org/css-cascade-6/#at-ruledef-scope)</code> rule without a <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;scope&#45;start&#45;2"></a>[&#60;scope&#45;start&#62;](https://drafts.csswg.org/css-cascade-6/#typedef-scope-start)&#44; e&#46;g&#46;&#44; &#34;<code>&#64;scope &#123; &#46;&#46;&#46; &#125;</code>&#34; or &#34;<code>&#64;scope to &#40;&#46;x&#41; &#123; &#46;&#46;&#46; &#125;</code>&#34;&#46;

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

The <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;scoping&#45;root"></a>[scoping root](https://drafts.csswg.org/selectors/#scoping-root) of such an <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;at&#45;scope&#45;3"></a><code>[&#64;scope](https://drafts.csswg.org/css-cascade-6/#at-ruledef-scope)</code> rule is the <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;the&#45;style&#45;element&#45;12"></a><code>[style](#the-style-element)</code> element&#39;s parent&#44; so its style rules can only style the parent and the parent&#39;s descendants&#46; Since the <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;the&#45;style&#45;element&#45;13"></a><code>[style](#the-style-element)</code> element has to be the first child of its parent&#44; those descendants come after the <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;the&#45;style&#45;element&#45;14"></a><code>[style](#the-style-element)</code> element&#46; This prevents a <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;the&#45;style&#45;element&#45;15"></a><code>[style](#the-style-element)</code> element in the <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;the&#45;body&#45;element&#45;2"></a><code>[body](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#the-body-element)</code> from restyling content that the user agent might already have rendered&#44; other than its parent&#46;

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

At&#45;rules like <code>&#64;font&#45;face</code>&#44; <code>&#64;keyframes</code>&#44; and <code>&#64;property</code> define names for the whole document&#46; Using such names only within the <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;the&#45;style&#45;element&#45;16"></a><code>[style](#the-style-element)</code> element&#39;s parent&#44; and picking names that aren&#39;t used elsewhere&#44; avoids restyling content that the user agent might already have rendered&#46;

<strong>Example&#58;</strong>

In the following fragment&#44; the <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;the&#45;style&#45;element&#45;17"></a><code>[style](#the-style-element)</code> element only styles its parent <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;the&#45;article&#45;element"></a><code>[article](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#the-article-element)</code> element and that element&#39;s descendants&#58;

``` text
<article>
 <style>
  @scope {
    :scope { border: 1px solid; }
    img { max-width: 100%; }
  }
 </style>
 <h2>Kittens</h2>
 <img src="kittens.jpg" alt="Three kittens asleep in a basket.">
</article>
```

Adding <code>h2 &#123; margin&#58; 0&#59; &#125;</code> outside the <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;at&#45;scope&#45;4"></a><code>[&#64;scope](https://drafts.csswg.org/css-cascade-6/#at-ruledef-scope)</code> rule would make the style sheet non&#45;conforming&#44; even though the only <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;the&#45;h1&#44;&#45;h2&#44;&#45;h3&#44;&#45;h4&#44;&#45;h5&#44;&#45;and&#45;h6&#45;elements"></a><code>[h2](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#the-h1,-h2,-h3,-h4,-h5,-and-h6-elements)</code> element comes after the <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;the&#45;style&#45;element&#45;18"></a><code>[style](#the-style-element)</code> element&#46;

A <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;the&#45;style&#45;element&#45;19"></a><code>[style](#the-style-element)</code> element is <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;implicitly&#45;potentially&#45;render&#45;blocking"></a>[implicitly potentially render&#45;blocking](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#implicitly-potentially-render-blocking) if the element was created by its <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;node&#45;document"></a>[node document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-node-document)&#39;s parser&#46;

------------------------------------------------------------------------

The user agent must run the <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;update&#45;a&#45;style&#45;block"></a>[update a <code>style</code> block](#update-a-style-block) algorithm whenever any of the following conditions occur&#58;

- The element is popped off the <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;stack&#45;of&#45;open&#45;elements"></a>[stack of open elements](html-language-syntax-parser--877d6146487f--37a9ba51eed8.md#stack-of-open-elements) of an <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;html&#45;parser"></a>[HTML parser](html-language-syntax-parser--877d6146487f--37a9ba51eed8.md#html-parser) or <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;xml&#45;parser"></a>[XML parser](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#xml-parser)&#46;

- The element is not on the <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;stack&#45;of&#45;open&#45;elements&#45;2"></a>[stack of open elements](html-language-syntax-parser--877d6146487f--37a9ba51eed8.md#stack-of-open-elements) of an <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;html&#45;parser&#45;2"></a>[HTML parser](html-language-syntax-parser--877d6146487f--37a9ba51eed8.md#html-parser) or <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;xml&#45;parser&#45;2"></a>[XML parser](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#xml-parser)&#44; and it <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;becomes&#45;connected"></a>[becomes connected](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#becomes-connected) or <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;becomes&#45;disconnected"></a>[disconnected](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#becomes-disconnected)&#46;

- The element is not on the <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;stack&#45;of&#45;open&#45;elements&#45;3"></a>[stack of open elements](html-language-syntax-parser--877d6146487f--37a9ba51eed8.md#stack-of-open-elements) of an <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;html&#45;parser&#45;3"></a>[HTML parser](html-language-syntax-parser--877d6146487f--37a9ba51eed8.md#html-parser) or <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;xml&#45;parser&#45;3"></a>[XML parser](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#xml-parser)&#44; and its <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;children&#45;changed&#45;steps"></a>[children changed steps](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-node-children-changed-ext) run&#46;

The <a id="update&#45;a&#45;style&#45;block"></a><strong>update a <code>style</code> block</strong> algorithm is as follows&#58;

1.  Let <var>element</var> be the <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;the&#45;style&#45;element&#45;20"></a><code>[style](#the-style-element)</code> element&#46;

2.  If <var>element</var> has an <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;associated&#45;css&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;7"></a>[associated CSS style sheet](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#associated-css-style-sheet)&#44; <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;remove&#45;a&#45;css&#45;style&#45;sheet"></a>[remove the CSS style sheet](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#remove-a-css-style-sheet) in question&#46;

3.  If <var>element</var> is not <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;connected"></a>[connected](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#connected)&#44; then return&#46;

4.  If <var>element</var>&#39;s <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;style&#45;type"></a><code>[type](html-language-obsolete--877d6146487f--66870e9cdabe.md#attr-style-type)</code> attribute is present and its value is neither the empty string nor an <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;ascii&#45;case&#45;insensitive"></a>[ASCII case&#45;insensitive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-case-insensitive) match for &#34;<a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;text&#47;css"></a><code>[text&#47;css](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#text/css)</code>&#34;&#44; then return&#46;

    <strong>Note&#58;</strong>

    In particular&#44; a <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;style&#45;type&#45;2"></a><code>[type](html-language-obsolete--877d6146487f--66870e9cdabe.md#attr-style-type)</code> value with parameters&#44; such as &#34;<code>text&#47;css&#59; charset&#61;utf&#45;8</code>&#34;&#44; will cause this algorithm to return early&#46;

5.  If the <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;should&#45;element&#39;s&#45;inline&#45;behavior&#45;be&#45;blocked&#45;by&#45;content&#45;security&#45;policy"></a>[Should element&#39;s inline behavior be blocked by Content Security Policy&#63;](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec-csp/#should-block-inline) algorithm returns &#34;<code>Blocked</code>&#34; when executed upon <var>element</var>&#44; &#34;<code>style</code>&#34;&#44; and <var>element</var>&#39;s <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;child&#45;text&#45;content&#45;3"></a>[child text content](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-child-text-content)&#44; then return&#46; [&#91;CSP&#93;](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#refsCSP)

6.  <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;create&#45;a&#45;css&#45;style&#45;sheet"></a>[Create a CSS style sheet](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#create-a-css-style-sheet) with the following properties&#58;

    <strong><a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;css&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;type"></a>[type](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#concept-css-style-sheet-type)</strong>

    <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;text&#47;css&#45;2"></a><code>[text&#47;css](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#text/css)</code>

    <strong><a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;css&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;owner&#45;node"></a>[owner node](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#concept-css-style-sheet-owner-node)</strong>

    <var>element</var>

    <strong><a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;css&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;media"></a>[media](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#concept-css-style-sheet-media)</strong>

    The <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;style&#45;media&#45;4"></a><code>[media](#attr-style-media)</code> attribute of <var>element</var>&#46;

    <strong>Note&#58;</strong>

    This is a reference to the &#40;possibly absent at this time&#41; attribute&#44; rather than a copy of the attribute&#39;s current value&#46; CSSOM defines what happens when the attribute is dynamically set&#44; changed&#44; or removed&#46;
    <strong><a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;css&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;title"></a>[title](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#concept-css-style-sheet-title)</strong>

    The <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;style&#45;title&#45;6"></a><code>[title](#attr-style-title)</code> attribute of <var>element</var>&#44; if <var>element</var> is <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;in&#45;a&#45;document&#45;tree&#45;2"></a>[in a document tree](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#in-a-document-tree)&#44; or the empty string otherwise&#46;

    <strong>Note&#58;</strong>

    Again&#44; this is a <em>reference</em> to the attribute&#46;
    <strong><a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;css&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;alternate&#45;flag"></a>[alternate flag](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#concept-css-style-sheet-alternate-flag)</strong>

    Unset&#46;

    <strong><a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;css&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;origin&#45;clean&#45;flag"></a>[origin&#45;clean flag](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#concept-css-style-sheet-origin-clean-flag)</strong>

    Set&#46;

    <strong><a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;css&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;location"></a>[location](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#concept-css-style-sheet-location)</strong>

    <strong><a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;css&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;parent&#45;css&#45;style&#45;sheet"></a>[parent CSS style sheet](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#concept-css-style-sheet-parent-css-style-sheet)</strong>

    <strong><a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;css&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;owner&#45;css&#45;rule"></a>[owner CSS rule](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#concept-css-style-sheet-owner-css-rule)</strong>

    null

    <strong><a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;css&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;disabled&#45;flag&#45;4"></a>[disabled flag](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#concept-css-style-sheet-disabled-flag)</strong>

    Left at its default value&#46;

    <strong><a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;css&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;css&#45;rules"></a>[CSS rules](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#concept-css-style-sheet-css-rules)</strong>

    Left uninitialized&#46;

    This doesn&#39;t seem right&#46; Presumably we should be using the element&#39;s <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;child&#45;text&#45;content&#45;4"></a>[child text content](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-child-text-content)&#63; Tracked as [issue &#35;2997](https://github.com/whatwg/html/issues/2997)&#46;

7.  If <var>element</var> <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;contributes&#45;a&#45;script&#45;blocking&#45;style&#45;sheet"></a>[contributes a script&#45;blocking style sheet](#contributes-a-script-blocking-style-sheet)&#44; <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;set&#45;append"></a>[append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#set-append) <var>element</var> to its <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;node&#45;document&#45;2"></a>[node document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-node-document)&#39;s <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;script&#45;blocking&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;set"></a>[script&#45;blocking style sheet set](#script-blocking-style-sheet-set)&#46;

8.  If <var>element</var>&#39;s <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;attr&#45;style&#45;media&#45;5"></a><code>[media](#attr-style-media)</code> attribute&#39;s value <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;matches&#45;the&#45;environment&#45;2"></a>[matches the environment](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#matches-the-environment) and <var>element</var> is <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;potentially&#45;render&#45;blocking&#45;2"></a>[potentially render&#45;blocking](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#potentially-render-blocking)&#44; then <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;block&#45;rendering"></a>[block rendering](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#block-rendering) on <var>element</var>&#46;

Once the attempts to obtain the style sheet&#39;s <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;critical&#45;subresources"></a>[critical subresources](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#critical-subresources)&#44; if any&#44; are complete&#44; or&#44; if the style sheet has no <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;critical&#45;subresources&#45;2"></a>[critical subresources](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#critical-subresources)&#44; once the style sheet has been parsed and processed&#44; the user agent must run these steps&#58;

Fetching the <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;critical&#45;subresources&#45;3"></a>[critical subresources](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#critical-subresources) is not well&#45;defined&#59; probably [issue &#35;968](https://github.com/whatwg/html/issues/968) is the best resolution for that&#46; In the meantime&#44; any <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;critical&#45;subresources&#45;4"></a>[critical subresource](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#critical-subresources) <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;request"></a>[request](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-request) should have its <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;request&#45;render&#45;blocking"></a>[render&#45;blocking](https://fetch.spec.whatwg.org/#request-render-blocking) set to whether or not the <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;the&#45;style&#45;element&#45;21"></a><code>[style](#the-style-element)</code> element is currently <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;render&#45;blocking"></a>[render&#45;blocking](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#render-blocking)&#46;

1.  Let <var>element</var> be the <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;the&#45;style&#45;element&#45;22"></a><code>[style](#the-style-element)</code> element associated with the style sheet in question&#46;

2.  Let <var>success</var> be true&#46;

3.  If the attempts to obtain any of the style sheet&#39;s <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;critical&#45;subresources&#45;5"></a>[critical subresources](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#critical-subresources) failed for any reason &#40;e&#46;g&#46;&#44; DNS error&#44; HTTP 404 response&#44; a connection being prematurely closed&#44; unsupported Content&#45;Type&#41;&#44; set <var>success</var> to false&#46;

    <strong>Note&#58;</strong>

    Note that content&#45;specific errors&#44; e&#46;g&#46;&#44; CSS parse errors or PNG decoding errors&#44; do not affect <var>success</var>&#46;

4.  <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;queue&#45;an&#45;element&#45;task"></a>[Queue an element task](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#queue-an-element-task) on the <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;networking&#45;task&#45;source"></a>[networking task source](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#networking-task-source) given <var>element</var> and the following steps&#58;

    1.  If <var>success</var> is true&#44; <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;event&#45;fire"></a>[fire an event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-fire) named <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;event&#45;load"></a><code>[load](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#event-load)</code> at <var>element</var>&#46;

    2.  Otherwise&#44; <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;concept&#45;event&#45;fire&#45;2"></a>[fire an event](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-event-fire) named <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;event&#45;error"></a><code>[error](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#event-error)</code> at <var>element</var>&#46;

    3.  If <var>element</var> <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;contributes&#45;a&#45;script&#45;blocking&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;2"></a>[contributes a script&#45;blocking style sheet](#contributes-a-script-blocking-style-sheet)&#58;

        1.  <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;assert"></a>[Assert](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert)&#58; <var>element</var>&#39;s <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;node&#45;document&#45;3"></a>[node document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-node-document)&#39;s <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;script&#45;blocking&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;set&#45;2"></a>[script&#45;blocking style sheet set](#script-blocking-style-sheet-set) <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;list&#45;contains"></a>[contains](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-contain) <var>element</var>&#46;

        2.  <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;list&#45;remove"></a>[Remove](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-remove) <var>element</var> from its <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;node&#45;document&#45;4"></a>[node document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-node-document)&#39;s <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;script&#45;blocking&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;set&#45;3"></a>[script&#45;blocking style sheet set](#script-blocking-style-sheet-set)&#46;

    4.  <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;unblock&#45;rendering"></a>[Unblock rendering](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#unblock-rendering) on <var>element</var>&#46;

The element must <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;delay&#45;the&#45;load&#45;event"></a>[delay the load event](html-language-syntax-parser--877d6146487f--37a9ba51eed8.md#delay-the-load-event) of the element&#39;s <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;node&#45;document&#45;5"></a>[node document](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-node-document) until all the attempts to obtain the style sheet&#39;s <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;critical&#45;subresources&#45;6"></a>[critical subresources](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#critical-subresources)&#44; if any&#44; are complete&#46;

<strong>Note&#58;</strong>

This specification does not specify a style system&#44; but CSS is expected to be supported by most web browsers&#46; [&#91;CSS&#93;](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#refsCSS)

The <a id="the&#45;style&#45;element&#58;linkstyle&#45;2"></a><code>[LinkStyle](https://drafts.csswg.org/cssom/#the-linkstyle-interface)</code> interface is also implemented by this element&#46; [&#91;CSSOM&#93;](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#refsCSSOM)

<strong>Example&#58;</strong>

The following document has its stress emphasis styled as bright red text rather than italics text&#44; while leaving titles of works and Latin words in their default italics&#46; It shows how using appropriate elements enables easier restyling of documents&#46;

``` text
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en-US">
 <head>
  <title>My favorite book</title>
  <style>
   body { color: black; background: white; }
   em { font-style: normal; color: red; }
  </style>
 </head>
 <body>
  <p>My <em>favorite</em> book of all time has <em>got</em> to be
  <cite>A Cat's Life</cite>. It is a book by P. Rahmel that talks
  about the <i lang="la">Felis catus</i> in modern human society.</p>
 </body>
</html>
```

#### <a id="interactions&#45;of&#45;styling&#45;and&#45;scripting"></a>4&#46;2&#46;7 Interactions of styling and scripting[](#interactions-of-styling-and-scripting)

If the style sheet referenced no other resources &#40;e&#46;g&#46;&#44; it was an internal style sheet given by a <a id="interactions&#45;of&#45;styling&#45;and&#45;scripting&#58;the&#45;style&#45;element"></a><code>[style](#the-style-element)</code> element with no <code>&#64;import</code> rules&#41;&#44; then the style rules must be <a id="interactions&#45;of&#45;styling&#45;and&#45;scripting&#58;immediately"></a>[immediately](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#immediately) made available to script&#59; otherwise&#44; the style rules must only be made available to script once the <a id="interactions&#45;of&#45;styling&#45;and&#45;scripting&#58;event&#45;loop"></a>[event loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#event-loop) reaches its <a id="interactions&#45;of&#45;styling&#45;and&#45;scripting&#58;update&#45;the&#45;rendering"></a>[update the rendering](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#update-the-rendering) step&#46;

<a id="a&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;that&#45;is&#45;blocking&#45;scripts"></a>An element <var>el</var> in the context of a <a id="interactions&#45;of&#45;styling&#45;and&#45;scripting&#58;document"></a><code>[Document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document)</code> of an <a id="interactions&#45;of&#45;styling&#45;and&#45;scripting&#58;html&#45;parser"></a>[HTML parser](html-language-syntax-parser--877d6146487f--37a9ba51eed8.md#html-parser) or <a id="interactions&#45;of&#45;styling&#45;and&#45;scripting&#58;xml&#45;parser"></a>[XML parser](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#xml-parser) <a id="contributes&#45;a&#45;script&#45;blocking&#45;style&#45;sheet"></a><strong>contributes a script&#45;blocking style sheet</strong> if all of the following are true&#58;

- <var>el</var> was created by that <a id="interactions&#45;of&#45;styling&#45;and&#45;scripting&#58;document&#45;2"></a><code>[Document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document)</code>&#39;s parser&#46;

- <var>el</var> is either a <a id="interactions&#45;of&#45;styling&#45;and&#45;scripting&#58;the&#45;style&#45;element&#45;2"></a><code>[style](#the-style-element)</code> element or a <a id="interactions&#45;of&#45;styling&#45;and&#45;scripting&#58;the&#45;link&#45;element"></a><code>[link](#the-link-element)</code> element that was an <a id="interactions&#45;of&#45;styling&#45;and&#45;scripting&#58;link&#45;type&#45;stylesheet"></a>[external resource link that contributes to the styling processing model](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#link-type-stylesheet) when the <var>el</var> was created by the parser&#46;

- <var>el</var>&#39;s <code>media</code> attribute&#39;s value <a id="interactions&#45;of&#45;styling&#45;and&#45;scripting&#58;matches&#45;the&#45;environment"></a>[matches the environment](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#matches-the-environment)&#46;

- <var>el</var>&#39;s style sheet was enabled when the element was created by the parser&#46;

- The last time the <a id="interactions&#45;of&#45;styling&#45;and&#45;scripting&#58;event&#45;loop&#45;2"></a>[event loop](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#event-loop) reached [step 1](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#step1)&#44; <var>el</var>&#39;s <a id="interactions&#45;of&#45;styling&#45;and&#45;scripting&#58;root"></a>[root](https://dom.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-tree-root) was that <a id="interactions&#45;of&#45;styling&#45;and&#45;scripting&#58;document&#45;3"></a><code>[Document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document)</code>&#46;

- The user agent hasn&#39;t given up on loading that particular style sheet yet&#46; A user agent may give up on loading a style sheet at any time&#46;

  <strong>Note&#58;</strong>

  Giving up on a style sheet before the style sheet loads&#44; if the style sheet eventually does still load&#44; means that the script might end up operating with incorrect information&#46; For example&#44; if a style sheet sets the color of an element to green&#44; but a script that inspects the resulting style is executed before the sheet is loaded&#44; the script will find that the element is black &#40;or whatever the default color is&#41;&#44; and might thus make poor choices &#40;e&#46;g&#46;&#44; deciding to use black as the color elsewhere on the page&#44; instead of green&#41;&#46; Implementers have to balance the likelihood of a script using incorrect information with the performance impact of doing nothing while waiting for a slow network request to finish&#46;

It is expected that counterparts to the above rules also apply to <a id="interactions&#45;of&#45;styling&#45;and&#45;scripting&#58;xml&#45;stylesheet"></a><code>[&#60;&#63;xml&#45;stylesheet&#63;&#62;](https://www.w3.org/TR/xml-stylesheet/#the-xml-stylesheet-processing-instruction)</code> PIs&#46; However&#44; this has not yet been thoroughly investigated&#46;

A <a id="interactions&#45;of&#45;styling&#45;and&#45;scripting&#58;document&#45;4"></a><code>[Document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document)</code> has a <a id="script&#45;blocking&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;set"></a><strong>script&#45;blocking style sheet set</strong>&#44; which is an <a id="interactions&#45;of&#45;styling&#45;and&#45;scripting&#58;set"></a>[ordered set](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ordered-set)&#44; initially empty&#46;

A <a id="interactions&#45;of&#45;styling&#45;and&#45;scripting&#58;document&#45;5"></a><code>[Document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document)</code> <var>document</var> <a id="has&#45;a&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;that&#45;is&#45;blocking&#45;scripts"></a><strong>has a style sheet that is blocking scripts</strong> if the following steps return true&#58;

1.  If <var>document</var>&#39;s <a id="interactions&#45;of&#45;styling&#45;and&#45;scripting&#58;script&#45;blocking&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;set"></a>[script&#45;blocking style sheet set](#script-blocking-style-sheet-set) is not <a id="interactions&#45;of&#45;styling&#45;and&#45;scripting&#58;list&#45;is&#45;empty"></a>[empty](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-is-empty)&#44; then return true&#46;

2.  If <var>document</var>&#39;s <a id="interactions&#45;of&#45;styling&#45;and&#45;scripting&#58;node&#45;navigable"></a>[node navigable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#node-navigable) is null&#44; then return false&#46;

3.  Let <var>containerDocument</var> be <var>document</var>&#39;s <a id="interactions&#45;of&#45;styling&#45;and&#45;scripting&#58;node&#45;navigable&#45;2"></a>[node navigable](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#node-navigable)&#39;s <a id="interactions&#45;of&#45;styling&#45;and&#45;scripting&#58;nav&#45;container"></a>[container document](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/commit-snapshots/877d6146487f15b15bc8dd0e3c625063a37b456a/#nav-container)&#46;

4.  If <var>containerDocument</var> is non&#45;null and <var>containerDocument</var>&#39;s <a id="interactions&#45;of&#45;styling&#45;and&#45;scripting&#58;script&#45;blocking&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;set&#45;2"></a>[script&#45;blocking style sheet set](#script-blocking-style-sheet-set) is not <a id="interactions&#45;of&#45;styling&#45;and&#45;scripting&#58;list&#45;is&#45;empty&#45;2"></a>[empty](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list-is-empty)&#44; then return true&#46;

5.  Return false&#46;

A <a id="interactions&#45;of&#45;styling&#45;and&#45;scripting&#58;document&#45;6"></a><code>[Document](html-language-foundations--877d6146487f--b55e73c31978.md#document)</code> <a id="has&#45;no&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;that&#45;is&#45;blocking&#45;scripts"></a><strong>has no style sheet that is blocking scripts</strong> if it does not <a id="interactions&#45;of&#45;styling&#45;and&#45;scripting&#58;has&#45;a&#45;style&#45;sheet&#45;that&#45;is&#45;blocking&#45;scripts"></a>[have a style sheet that is blocking scripts](#has-a-style-sheet-that-is-blocking-scripts)&#46;

### <a id="elements&#45;3"></a>Elements[](#elements-3)

<em>This section is non&#45;normative&#46;</em>

† Categories in the &#34;Parents&#34; column refer to parents that list the given categories in their content model&#44; not to elements that themselves are in those categories&#46; For example&#44; the <a id="elements&#45;3&#58;the&#45;a&#45;element&#45;2"></a><code>[a](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#the-a-element)</code> element&#39;s &#34;Parents&#34; column says &#34;phrasing&#34;&#44; so any element whose content model contains the &#34;phrasing&#34; category could be a parent of an <a id="elements&#45;3&#58;the&#45;a&#45;element&#45;3"></a><code>[a](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#the-a-element)</code> element&#46; Since the &#34;flow&#34; category includes all the &#34;phrasing&#34; elements&#44; that means the <a id="elements&#45;3&#58;the&#45;th&#45;element&#45;3"></a><code>[th](html-language-forms-tables-templates--877d6146487f--2dfc6f632608.md#the-th-element)</code> element could be a parent to an <a id="elements&#45;3&#58;the&#45;a&#45;element&#45;4"></a><code>[a](html-language-elements-text-links--877d6146487f--8833a85fd242.md#the-a-element)</code> element&#46;
